# P1b/P1c — Web v2 MVP (hybrid Civic · Editorial · Cartographic) — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Replace the single-page v1 web with the public Atlas v2 MVP — seven routed pages (`/overview`, `/atlas`, `/topics/:id`, `/emerging`, `/compare`, `/data`, `/about`) plus a print page `/brief` — that reads contract-v2 releases at run time (`data/latest.json` → `data/<release>/*.json`) or from a single offline HTML file, in Thai and English, light and dark, keyboard- and screen-reader-usable, styled by a new `DESIGN.md` (Civic modern base, Editorial headline + forest plot on Overview, Cartographic terrain map on `/atlas`).

**Architecture:** React 19 SPA under a `HashRouter`. One `DataSource` interface with two implementations — `HttpSource` (fetches `./data/…` relative to `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`) and `EmbeddedSource` (reads the `{latest, files}` bundle that `atlas publish` injects into `<script type="application/json" id="atlas-data">`). `loadRelease()` reads the manifest, checks schema major 2 with zod, loads the core files in parallel and the previous release's `overview.json` for Δ; topic details load lazily. Pages are thin; formatting, URL state, search, CSV and definition helpers are pure modules with unit tests. Styling = CSS custom properties from `DESIGN.md` (`src/styles/tokens.css`, light/dark) in hand-written CSS layers ported from the approved mood boards, Tailwind 4 utilities for layout only. Two build outputs from one codebase: the Pages-ready `dist/` and `dist/offline-template.html` (vite-plugin-singlefile, fonts inlined, no network). `npm run brief:pdf` prints `/brief` to A4 PDFs with Playwright.

**Tech Stack (exact pins):** react 19.3.1 · react-dom 19.3.1 · react-router 7.18.4 (HashRouter, declarative mode) · zod 4.6.7 · vite 7.3.7 · @vitejs/plugin-react 5.2.0 · tailwindcss 4.3.3 + @tailwindcss/vite 4.3.3 · vite-plugin-singlefile 2.3.4 · vitest 4.1.12 · @playwright/test 1.64.1 · @axe-core/playwright 4.14.0 · ajv 8.20.0 · typescript 5.9.3 · eslint 9.39.5 + @eslint/js 9.39.5 · typescript-eslint 8.71.2 · eslint-plugin-react-hooks 7.1.1 · eslint-plugin-react-refresh 0.4.26 · globals 16.5.0 · @types/react 19.3.0 · @types/react-dom 19.3.0 · @types/node 24.19.2 · json-schema-to-typescript 16.0.0 · @fontsource/sarabun 5.3.0 (only to vendor the woff2 files). Node 24 (CI), ≥ 22.12 locally. Versions checked against the npm registry on 2026-10-19: newer majors exist (react-router 8.4, vite 8.3, vitest 5.0, TypeScript 7.0, ESLint 10) and are deliberately **not** used — the user asked for react-router 7; `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6 needs Vite 8 (Rolldown) whose interaction with vite-plugin-singlefile is unproven here; typescript-eslint 8.71 supports TypeScript < 6.1; the current ESLint 9 flat config keeps working.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-18-tci-atlas-v2-design.md` — "Web v2" parts of P1, feature catalog E1–E4, E12, R1, R2, R9, R10, "Data contract v2", HashRouter + `BASE_URL` fetch, DataSource Http | Embedded, offline single HTML, PDF brief, WCAG 2.2 AA, TH/EN, dark mode, URL state, TCI brand via `web/src/brand/`, no italics in Thai. Delivery: `C:\Users\santi\.claude\plans\codebase-project-expressive-planet.md` § "ภาพรวมการส่งมอบ". Data contract: the reconciled P1a plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-p1a-core-analytics.md` Tasks 15 (models; `definition_version` default 3), 16 (release folder + `latest.json`), 18 (`atlas publish --site`, offline bundle `{latest, files}`). Design: the user chose **HYBRID** from the mood boards in the controller's `scratchpad\moodboards\` (README.md, civic.html, editorial.html, cartographic.html) — tokens and components below are copied from those files.

## Prerequisites (stop if any is false)

- P1a Tasks 15–18 are merged: `web/src/data/contract.schema.json` and `web/src/data/contract.ts` were regenerated from contract v2. Check: `grep -cE "export interface (Manifest|Overview|TaxonomyFile|TopicsFile|TopicDetail|SeriesFile|EdgesFile|KeywordsFile|Latest) " web/src/data/contract.ts` prints `9`. (Verified while writing this plan: the P1a Task 15 models, run through `npm run gen:types`, emit those nine container interfaces; nested model names are unstable — the Topic model comes out as `Topic1` — so `src/data/types.ts` derives nested types by indexed access.)
- `uv run atlas publish --site <dir>` exists in `pipeline/` (P1a Task 18) — only Task 13's script and docs call it.
- Work on a branch (e.g. `p1b-web-v2`); never push or create remotes without the owner's go-ahead.

## Global Constraints

- **Where:** every command runs in `web/` unless a step says otherwise. Verification gate for every task: `npm run lint && npx tsc -b && npm test && npm run build` (from Task 3 on also `npm run e2e`). Commit after each task; every commit message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Never `git push`.
- **Pins:** `package.json` uses the exact versions in Tech Stack (no `^`/`~`); `package-lock.json` is committed.
- **Data:** the web never calls an API or an LLM. It reads `data/latest.json` → `data/<release>/manifest.json` → `overview.json`, `taxonomy.json`, `topics.json`, `series.json`, `edges.json`, `keywords.json` (only when `manifest.modules` contains `"keywords"`), `topics/<id>.json` on demand, and `data/<previous_release>/overview.json` for Δ. URLs are relative: `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/…` with Vite `base: './'` — never an absolute `/data/…`. Types come only from the generated `src/data/contract.ts` through `src/data/types.ts` (containers by name, nested types by indexed access such as `TopicsFile['topics'][number]`); never hand-edit `contract.ts`. Fields with pydantic defaults are optional in the generated types — read them with `??`.
- **Contract facts the UI relies on:** `share_recent`, `share_baseline`, `share_growth`, `share_growth_lo`, `share_growth_hi` are fractions (0.10 = 10 %, +0.10 = +10 %/yr); the interval level is `manifest.ci_level` (default 0.9); `share_ratio: null` + `no_baseline: true` → show "ใหม่ — ไม่มีในช่วงฐาน / New — not in the baseline window"; `low_data_reason ∈ {few_articles, not_estimable}`; `data_quality.completeness_method ∈ {median, configured}` (no completeness share); the Emerging definition is **version 3** (user decision 2026-10-19) — always display `manifest.definition_version`, never a hard-coded number; `forecast` is null for LOW_DATA and `forecast.show === false` means "แนวโน้มไม่แน่นอน / Trend uncertain"; `metrics.journal_driven` explains WATCH; `metrics.orgs_unverified` = institution breadth not checked; colours of groups are token keys `cat-01…cat-12`.
- **Router:** `HashRouter` only (deep links survive reloads on Pages and work from `file://`). Never write an in-page anchor `href="#id"` (it would change the route) — use `scrollToId()` buttons and the `SkipLink` component.
- **No third-party requests at run time:** no Google Fonts, CDN, analytics or remote images. Sarabun is self-hosted from `src/assets/fonts/` (woff2, Thai + Latin subsets, weights 400/600/700) and inlined in the offline file.
- **Design:** `DESIGN.md` (repo root) wins over any generic catalog. Colours only through CSS custom properties defined in `src/styles/tokens.css` (`var(--…)` in CSS and inline SVG styles); no hex literals in `.tsx`. No dynamic Tailwind class strings (`bg-[${x}]`); class names built from data are allowed **only** for hand-written CSS classes that exist for every value (`chip-${status}`, `st-${status}`, `mk-${status}`, `wh-${status}`); dynamic colours go in `style={{ … var(--series-1) … }}`.
- **Status encoding:** always glyph + colour + text: ▲ EMERGING, ◆ WATCH, ● HOT, ▼ DECLINING, ■ STABLE, dashed ring LOW_DATA; display order EMERGING, WATCH, HOT, DECLINING, STABLE, LOW_DATA; status colours are reserved for statuses.
- **Thai typography:** no italics anywhere (`em, i, cite, dfn, var, address { font-style: normal }`); no letter-spacing or `text-transform` on Thai (English-only caps via `[lang="en"]`); body line-height 1.65, never < 1.5; Arabic numerals with en-US grouping in both languages; years are ค.ศ.; negative numbers use U+2212 "−".
- **Bilingual:** every UI string lives in `src/strings/*.ts` with both `th` and `en`; `src/lib/i18n.test.ts` enforces parity and placeholder equality. Alternate-language names are wrapped in an element with the right `lang`.
- **Self-hiding sections** (CLAUDE.md §6): a section whose data is missing (no brief, no keywords, no previous release, empty lists) renders nothing; a page with no release shows its `h1` and an explanation — never a white page.
- **Copyright/PDPA:** no abstracts anywhere; representative articles show title + journal + year + link to ThaiJO/DOI only; no person-level data or ranking.
- **Accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA):** skip link; visible focus = yellow `#ffd43b` + ink bar; targets ≥ 24 × 24 px (controls are 40–44 px); every chart has a table view; sortable headers carry `aria-sort`; tabs follow the ARIA tabs pattern with arrow/Home/End keys; map topics are focusable and also listed in text; `prefers-reduced-motion` respected; information never by colour alone.
- **Lint rules that shape the code** (current config, errors): `react-refresh/only-export-components` → a `.tsx` file exports only components (and constants); hooks, contexts and helpers live in `.ts` files. `eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7 compiler rules → no synchronous `setState` in an effect body (only in callbacks/handlers), no reading/writing `ref.current` during render, no mutation of props/state/memo results (copy before `.sort()`), no impure calls (`Date.now`, `Math.random`) during render.
- **TypeScript:** `strict`, `verbatimModuleSyntax` (`import type` for types), `erasableSyntaxOnly` (no `enum`, no constructor parameter properties), `noUnusedLocals/Parameters`.
- **UI tasks:** before writing UI the implementer invokes the `impeccable` skill (craft mode while building, polish mode in Task 13) and follows DESIGN.md; charts also follow the `dataviz` skill rules (thin marks, legend + direct labels, table view, hover layer).

## Decisions this plan takes (controller: confirm or override)

1. **Version lines:** react-router 7, Vite 7, vitest 4, TypeScript 5.9, ESLint 9 (see Tech Stack for why the newer majors are skipped).
2. **Tailwind 3 → 4** with `@tailwindcss/vite`; all shadcn/Radix packages are removed (none of the 53 `components/ui` files is imported by the current app). Component styling is hand-written CSS in cascade layers ported from the civic mood board; Tailwind utilities are for layout only.
3. **Definition constants:** contract v2 carries `definition_version`, `ci_level` and `last_complete_year` but not the thresholds or windows. The +10 %/yr bar, share ratio 1.8 / 0.8, large = top quartile (0.75), 30 articles, 3 journals + 3 institutions and the R/B/W window lengths (3/5/5 years) are constants in `src/lib/definition.ts` mirroring `config.example.yaml` defaults. **Recommended P1a follow-up:** publish a `manifest.definition` block so the web reads them (then `definition.ts` reads the manifest first).
4. **Fixtures** are produced by a deterministic Node generator (`scripts/make-fixtures.mjs`, 48 topics in 8 OpenAlex-style groups, every status and edge case) and validated against `contract.schema.json` with ajv in a unit test — not by running the Python pipeline. They are committed (synthetic, no real data) and CI fails if they drift from the generator.
5. **"Sample data" flag:** a release whose `manifest.embedding_provider` is `fixture` or `lite` (CPU fallback, never production) shows a "ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง / Sample data" banner.
6. **Highlighter** for the Overview headline is the Editorial turmeric `#ffe07a` drawn as a lower-half band (dark `rgba(214,150,20,.42)`), never a full yellow background — full yellow means keyboard focus.
7. **/compare series colours** are the dataviz reference slots 1–4 (blue, orange, aqua, yellow), validated adjacent-pair in both themes on our surfaces, always paired with a dash pattern, direct end labels and a table view.
8. **/emerging** has six tabs (EMERGING, WATCH, HOT, DECLINING, STABLE, LOW_DATA) with counts; the four signal statuses get the CI forest-plot chart view.
9. **Δ on KPIs** compares with `data/<previous_release>/overview.json` when the site still has that folder (`atlas publish` keeps older releases); the offline file carries one release, so Δ hides there.
10. **Pages deploy (owner pre-authorised):** Task 14 adds `.github/workflows/pages.yml` that builds the web and publishes it with the synthetic example release copied to `data/` — the "ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง / Sample data" banner shows because its `embedding_provider` is `fixture`. The controller runs the deployment; a real release later replaces the data folder via `atlas publish --site`. CI (Task 13) lints, type-checks, unit-tests, builds and runs the Playwright smoke; an optional job prints the brief PDF from fixtures as an artifact.
11. `scripts/import_atlas.sh` becomes "build the web, then `uv run atlas publish --site web/dist`"; `atlas_data.json` (v1.1) is no longer read by the web (dropping its export is a P2 pipeline clean-up).

## Review Focus

1. **The site is served from a sub-path or opened from `file://`** (GitHub Pages `/tci-research-atlas/`, a shared drive) → data still loads (relative `./data/…` or the embedded bundle) and `#/topics/T000123` deep links work after reload — Task 3 (`source.test.ts` base `./` and `/sub/`), Task 12 (offline file e2e with zero non-file requests).
2. **A release missing optional parts** (no `keywords.json`, `brief: null`, no previous release, `forecast: null`, zero topics) → every page renders its `h1`, the affected sections disappear, no console error — Task 3 (`release.test.ts` optional files) + the empty-data e2e run over all routes.
3. **Long Thai names, mixed TH/EN and tone-mark-damaged text on a 360 px phone** → no horizontal page scroll, tables stack into label/value cards, search for "ความตองการ" still finds "ความต้องการ" — Task 4 (`search.test.ts`), Task 3 e2e phone-width check (extended per page).
4. **Wrong or damaged data** (404 `latest.json`, schema 3.x, broken JSON) → a clear `role="alert"` banner naming the reason, never a white page — Task 3 (`release.test.ts` schema mismatch, e2e missing-data test).
5. **Keyboard-only use of the map and charts** → every EMERGING/WATCH/HOT/DECLINING topic is a focusable marker with a grid reference, every topic is findable in the gazetteer search, Enter selects, the selection is in the URL — Task 9 (e2e keyboard check) + axe on every route.

### Task 1: Design system — `DESIGN.md`, tokens (light/dark), self-hosted Sarabun, status glyphs, TCI wordmark, CVD/contrast test

The user picked the HYBRID direction. This task writes it down once (`DESIGN.md`, the normative token list) and mirrors it in CSS custom properties that every later task uses. Nothing renders yet — the current v1 app is untouched; the deliverable is proven by the palette test (CVD separation + WCAG contrast in both themes, computed, not eyeballed) and a green build.

**Files:**
- Create: `DESIGN.md` (repo root)
- Create: `web/src/styles/tokens.css`, `web/src/styles/fonts.css`, `web/src/styles/status.css`, `web/src/styles/palette.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/status.ts`, `web/src/lib/color.ts`, `web/src/components/StatusGlyph.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/brand/brand.ts`, `web/src/brand/Wordmark.tsx`, `web/src/brand/README.md`
- Create: `web/scripts/vendor-fonts.mjs`, `web/src/assets/fonts/` (6 × woff2 + `OFL.txt`, produced by the script)
- Create: `web/vitest.config.ts`, `web/tsconfig.test.json`
- Modify: `web/tsconfig.json` (reference), `web/tsconfig.app.json` (exclude tests), `web/package.json` (devDeps + scripts)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces:
  - `src/lib/status.ts`: `STATUSES` (ordered tuple), `type StatusId`, `SIGNAL_STATUSES`, `type SignalStatus`, `isStatus(v): v is StatusId`, `isSignal(s): s is SignalStatus`, `glyphPath(status, r): string` (SVG path centred on 0,0).
  - `src/lib/color.ts`: `type Cvd = 'protan' | 'deutan' | 'tritan'`, `contrastRatio(a, b): number`, `deltaE(a, b, cvd?): number` (OKLab ×100, Machado 2009 severity 1).
  - `src/components/StatusGlyph.tsx`: `<StatusGlyph status size? />`, `<StatusChip status label small? />`.
  - `src/brand/brand.ts`: `BRAND {name, mark, logo: BrandLogo | null, temporary}`, `type BrandLogo`; `src/brand/Wordmark.tsx`: `<Wordmark sub lang />`.
  - CSS custom properties (names used everywhere later): `--bg --surface --ink --ink-2 --border --border-2 --field-border --brand --brand-ink --brand-ink-2 --link --link-hover --focus --focus-ink --up --hl --btn --btn-hover --btn-ink --btn-edge --dot --shadow --st-{STATUS} --tag-{STATUS}-bg --tag-{STATUS}-ink --map-sheet --map-contour --map-grid --map-dot --cat-01…--cat-12 --series-1…--series-4 --font`. CSS classes: `.st-icon .st-mark .mk .mk-{STATUS} .st-{STATUS} .chip .chip-sm .chip-{STATUS}`.
  - npm scripts: `test` (vitest run), `vendor:fonts`.

````markdown
# Design System: TCI Research Atlas

## Overview

**Creative North Star: "The Public Statistical Release" (แถลงสถิติสาธารณะ)**

The Atlas reads like a statistics release from a national public body: a civic-blue header band, plain white pages, numbered figures and tables that can be cited, every chart paired with its table, every figure carrying a "ที่มา:" source line. That civic base runs on every page. Two signatures sit on top of it, each on one page only. The **Overview** opens with an editorial headline sentence whose figures are marked with a turmeric highlighter, and it shows growth honestly as a CI forest plot against the +10 %/yr bar. The **map page** (`/atlas`) is a topographic sheet: tinted field terrain with density contours, topics as settlements with A–J × 1–6 grid references, and a gazetteer that repeats every place in text.

Density is moderate and calm. Numbers are large and tabular, prose is short, and uncertainty is always visible (intervals, "แนวโน้มไม่แน่นอน", "ข้อมูลน้อย"). Thai and English are equal citizens; Thai sets the rhythm (Sarabun, generous line height, no italics).

**Key Characteristics:**
- Civic structure: header band, phase banner, page title + release metadata, "ในหน้านี้" rail, numbered figures/tables with source lines.
- Every chart has a Chart/Table tab; tables sort and become label/value cards on phones.
- Status is always glyph + colour + label (▲ ◆ ● ▼ ■ and a dashed ring).
- Flat, square-cornered surfaces; depth only for floating layers.
- One typeface (Sarabun) for everything.

## Colors

A sober civic palette — near-black ink on white, one deep blue for the institution, one link blue, one yellow that only ever means "keyboard focus" — plus a reserved six-colour status set and quiet terrain tints for the map.

### Primary
- **Civic Blue** (#0f3557; dark #0a2238): the header band, the drawer-style headers and the phase tag. It names the institution; never used for data.
- **Link Blue** (#1a5bb0; dark #8bbaf6): links, link-buttons, sort buttons, the 10 px rule under the header band, and the single-series trend line.

### Secondary
- **Focus Yellow** (#ffd43b): keyboard focus only (background + ink bar under links, outline + inset ink ring on controls). Also the skip link.
- **Turmeric Highlighter** (#ffe07a; dark rgba(214,150,20,.42)): the lower-half band behind figures and topic names in the Overview headline sentence. Nowhere else.
- **Go Green** (#00703c, hover #005a30): the primary action button ("ดูบทความใน ThaiJO", downloads) and positive KPI deltas (#00703c light, #4cc38a dark).

### Neutral
- **Ink** (#0b0c0e; dark #f3f4f5): text, axes, table header rules (2 px), form-field borders.
- **Ink Secondary** (#474e55; dark #b9bfc4): secondary text, source lines, tick labels.
- **Paper** (#ffffff; dark #0f1214) and **Paper Tint** (#f3f4f5; dark #1a1e21): page and panel backgrounds (findings, inactive tabs, footer, uncertain box).
- **Rules** (#b1b4b6 and #dcdee0; dark #5f676d and #2d3338): borders and row dividers. Decorative only — interactive boundaries use Ink.

### Status (reserved)
EMERGING #13a08a / dark #2da78c · WATCH #c47a0c / #c37511 · HOT #a3124f / #ad3678 · DECLINING #5b74cf / #5468da · STABLE and LOW_DATA #6f777b / #7b8389. Tag pairs (background / ink) are in the frontmatter; dark tags: EMERGING #12372f/#a3e6d2, WATCH #3d2a07/#f6d08c, HOT #3f1427/#f4aecb, DECLINING #1d2649/#bcc8f6, STABLE #262b2f/#d5d9dc, LOW_DATA uses the page background with a 1 px rule ring.

### Map
Map sheet #f6f7f4 / dark #111618; contour brown #9a6a35 / #c48d4f; grid rgba(11,12,14,.09) / rgba(226,233,229,.07); unnamed-topic dots rgba(11,12,14,.42) / rgba(226,233,229,.38). Field tints `cat-01…cat-12` (light → dark): #e8b5a6→#5b3229, #dbc99d→#4d4329, #c3d59c→#39492a, #e8d095→#544423, #a8d1a4→#284732, #b1c4da→#293c52, #d4bad6→#46314c, #9ccfca→#1f4746, #bfd8e8→#213f50, #e3bfcd→#4e2e3a, #cfd3a6→#3f4425, #d9cbb8→#463d31.

### Series (compare only)
series-1…4 = #2a78d6, #eb6834, #1baf7a, #eda100 (dark #3987e5, #d95926, #199e70, #c98500) — the dataviz reference order, validated as adjacent pairs; always with a dash pattern and a direct label.

### Named Rules
**The Reserved Status Rule.** The six status colours mean status and nothing else; a status is never shown by colour alone — glyph + colour + label, every time.
**The Yellow Means Focus Rule.** A full yellow background appears only on the focused element. The headline highlighter is a lower-half band, never a full fill.
**The Token-Only Rule.** Components use `var(--token)`; a hex value in a component is a bug. Group colours arrive from the data as token keys (`cat-07`), never hex.
**The Terrain Is Not Data Rule.** Field tints are background terrain; fields are always named by labels and in the gazetteer, so tint repetition beyond 12 groups is harmless.

## Typography

**Display / Body / Label Font:** Sarabun (with Noto Sans Thai, Leelawadee UI, system-ui), self-hosted woff2, Thai + Latin subsets, weights 400/600/700.

**Character:** Sarabun descends from TH Sarabun, the standard face of Thai government documents — familiar, plain and legible in both scripts; one family keeps the release sober.

### Hierarchy
- **Display** (700, clamp(2rem, 1.4rem + 2.2vw, 3rem), 1.3): the page `h1`, max 34ch.
- **Headline** (600, clamp(1.5rem, 1.1rem + 1.6vw, 2.5rem), 1.45): the Overview headline sentence only, max 30ch.
- **Title** (700, clamp(1.5rem, 1.2rem + 1vw, 2.25rem), 1.35): section `h2`.
- **Body** (400, 19 px ≥ 641 px wide / 16 px on phones, 1.65): prose, max 40em.
- **Label** (600, 0.875–1rem, 1.45): table headers, tab labels, chip text, KPI labels.
- **Numeric** (700, clamp(1.75rem, 1.4rem + 1vw, 2.375rem), 1.25, tabular figures): KPI values and finding stats.

### Named Rules
**The Upright Thai Rule.** Thai has no true italic: `em`, `i`, `cite`, `dfn`, `var`, `address` render upright; emphasis is weight 600/700. No letter-spacing and no case transforms on Thai — English-only caps use `[lang="en"]`.
**The Arabic Numerals Rule.** Both languages use Arabic digits with en-US grouping (248,310), the true minus sign (−), and Gregorian years (ค.ศ.), matching the data.

## Layout

A 1200 px max container with 16 px gutters on phones and 30 px from 900 px. Reading pages use a two-thirds column plus a 240 px "ในหน้านี้" rail (rail moves above the content under 900 px). `/atlas` uses the full container: map sheet plus a 320 px side column (key, layers, selection) from 1100 px, stacked below on smaller screens. Breakpoints: 640 (phone ↔ tablet; tables stack into label/value cards), 900, 1100. Spacing rhythm 4/8/16/24/40/64; sections are separated by 40 px; the footer starts 64 px below content. No horizontal page scroll at 360 px.

## Elevation & Depth

Flat by default: depth comes from borders, tints (Paper Tint panels) and the header band. A single shadow (`0 8px 28px rgba(11,12,14,.18)`, dark `rgba(0,0,0,.6)`) is used only for floating layers: the tooltip, the search listbox and the map's zoom control.

### Named Rules
**The Flat Page Rule.** Cards, KPI tiles and tables never cast shadows; if something floats, it is temporary.

## Shapes

Square corners everywhere (0 px): buttons, tabs, chips, panels, inputs. The only rounding is 2 px on map grid-reference tags. The header band ends with a 10 px Link Blue rule; tables have a 2 px Ink header rule and 1 px Rule Light row dividers; KPI tiles have a 1 px Rule border.

## Components

### Header band and navigation
Civic Blue band: the wordmark (temporary "TCI" box + name + Thai subtitle; the official logo swaps in at `web/src/brand/brand.ts`), language switch (two pressed-state buttons) and theme toggle (44 px, 2 px white outline). Below it, a white navigation row of text links (current page: 4 px Link Blue underline, `aria-current="page"`) and the topic search. A phase banner ("รุ่นทดลอง") and, for test releases, a "ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง" tag follow.

### Buttons
- **Shape:** square, 44 px tall.
- **Primary:** Go Green fill, white 700 text, 2 px darker bottom edge (#002d18).
- **Hover / Focus:** hover #005a30; focus = yellow fill, ink text, ink bottom edge.
- **Secondary:** 2 px Ink outline on Paper, 600 text; pressed state = Ink fill, Paper text.

### Chips (status tags)
Square tag with the status glyph, tinted background and dark ink (≥ 8:1). Small variant for tables and tooltips. LOW_DATA uses a 1 px rule ring and a dashed glyph.

### Tabs
ARIA tabs: Paper Tint inactive tabs, the selected tab joins its bordered panel (Paper, 1 px Rule). Arrow keys, Home and End move between tabs. Every figure has a "แผนภูมิ / ตาราง" tab pair.

### Tables
Caption numbered "ตาราง n:", 2 px Ink header rule, right-aligned tabular numbers, sortable headers as link-blue buttons with an up/down glyph and `aria-sort`. A "ดาวน์โหลด CSV" button sits above every data table. Under 640 px rows become two-column label/value cards (`data-label`).

### Figures
Title "แผนภูมิ n: …", legend (glyph + label), the chart, then "ที่มา: TCI Research Atlas … รอบ {release} · นิยามสถานะฉบับที่ {v}". A "วิธีอ่านแผนภูมินี้" disclosure explains the encoding in words.

### Inputs / search
2 px Ink border, square, 44 px; focus = 3 px yellow outline + inset ink ring. The topic search is an ARIA combobox with a floating listbox (shadow) showing glyph, name and the other-language name.

### Banners and notices
Phase banner: Civic Blue tag + text. Error banner (`role="alert"`): 4 px left rule in HOT ink on Paper Tint, a bold reason and what to do. Uncertain box ("แนวโน้มไม่แน่นอน"): 4 px left Rule on Paper Tint. WATCH inset: 2 px WATCH border on the WATCH tag background.

### Headline sentence (signature, Overview only)
The release's brief headline in Headline type, figures and topic names under the turmeric lower-half band (topic names are links), followed by a small note that the sentence was generated automatically from the system's figures (template or AI, numbers checked).

### CI forest plot (signature, Overview and /emerging)
One row per topic: name (+ field) | whisker on a shared axis | value. The whisker is the interval at `manifest.ci_level`, capped (arrow when clipped), with the status glyph at the estimate; a dashed vertical line marks the +10 %/yr EMERGING bar and a solid line marks zero. Grouped by status with each group's rule in one sentence.

### Terrain map (signature, /atlas only)
Canvas terrain on the map sheet: field tints by density, contour brown lines (every third line heavier), faint 10 × 6 grid with A–J / 1–6 references on the frame edges, unnamed topics as dots. EMERGING/WATCH/HOT/DECLINING topics are focusable SVG glyphs with grid references; labels have a sheet-colour halo and never collide. Layer toggles (statuses, contours, tints, names), zoom buttons (+, −, reset), drag to pan, Ctrl + wheel to zoom; nothing requires dragging. The gazetteer repeats every topic in text.

## Do's and Don'ts

### Do:
- **Do** pair every chart with a table tab, a numbered caption and a "ที่มา:" source line.
- **Do** show uncertainty: intervals at `manifest.ci_level`, "แนวโน้มไม่แน่นอน" when a forecast is not shown, "ใหม่ — ไม่มีในช่วงฐาน" when the baseline window had no articles.
- **Do** keep body line-height at 1.65 and text measure ≤ 40em.
- **Do** read colours from tokens and group colours from `cat-NN` keys.
- **Do** keep 44 px controls and the yellow focus treatment on every interactive element.

### Don't:
- **Don't** use italics, letter-spacing or uppercase on Thai text.
- **Don't** use a status colour for anything that is not a status, or a status without its glyph and label.
- **Don't** fill a background with yellow except for keyboard focus.
- **Don't** add shadows to cards, tiles or tables, or round their corners.
- **Don't** load fonts or anything else from a third-party server.
- **Don't** show abstracts or rank individual people.
````

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing palette test** — create `web/src/styles/palette.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { contrastRatio, deltaE } from '../lib/color'

const css = readFileSync(new URL('./tokens.css', import.meta.url), 'utf8')

function block(selector: string): Record<string, string> {
  const start = css.indexOf(`${selector} {`)
  if (start < 0) throw new Error(`tokens.css has no "${selector} {" block`)
  const end = css.indexOf('}', start)
  const vars: Record<string, string> = {}
  for (const m of css.slice(start, end).matchAll(/--([\w-]+):\s*([^;]+);/g)) vars[m[1]] = m[2].trim()
  return vars
}

const light = block(':root')
const dark = block(':root[data-theme="dark"]')
const darkFromOs = block(':root:not([data-theme="light"])')
const SIGNAL = ['EMERGING', 'WATCH', 'HOT', 'DECLINING'] as const
const ALL = [...SIGNAL, 'STABLE', 'LOW_DATA'] as const
const isHex = (v: string) => /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(v)
const allPairs = <T,>(xs: readonly T[]) => xs.flatMap((a, i) => xs.slice(i + 1).map((b) => [a, b] as const))

describe('design tokens', () => {
  it('the OS dark preference and the dark toggle use identical values', () => {
    expect(darkFromOs).toEqual(dark)
  })

  it('every light token except the font stack has a dark value', () => {
    const missing = Object.keys(light).filter((k) => k !== 'font' && !(k in dark))
    expect(missing).toEqual([])
  })

  for (const [name, t] of Object.entries({ light, dark })) {
    describe(`${name} theme`, () => {
      it('status colours stay apart for every pair (CVD ≥ 8, normal vision ≥ 15)', () => {
        for (const [a, b] of allPairs(SIGNAL)) {
          const ca = t[`st-${a}`]
          const cb = t[`st-${b}`]
          const cvd = Math.min(deltaE(ca, cb, 'protan'), deltaE(ca, cb, 'deutan'))
          expect(cvd, `${a}/${b} CVD`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(8)
          expect(deltaE(ca, cb), `${a}/${b} normal`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(15)
        }
      })

      it('compare series colours stay apart as adjacent pairs (CVD ≥ 8, normal vision ≥ 15)', () => {
        for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) {
          const ca = t[`series-${i}`]
          const cb = t[`series-${i + 1}`]
          expect(Math.min(deltaE(ca, cb, 'protan'), deltaE(ca, cb, 'deutan')), `series ${i}/${i + 1}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(8)
          expect(deltaE(ca, cb), `series ${i}/${i + 1} normal`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(15)
        }
      })

      it('status marks reach 3:1 on the page and on the map sheet', () => {
        for (const st of [...SIGNAL, 'STABLE']) {
          expect(contrastRatio(t[`st-${st}`], t.bg), `${st} on bg`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
          expect(contrastRatio(t[`st-${st}`], t['map-sheet']), `${st} on map`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
        }
      })

      it('status tags and text reach 4.5:1', () => {
        for (const st of ALL) {
          expect(contrastRatio(t[`tag-${st}-ink`], t[`tag-${st}-bg`]), `tag ${st}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5)
        }
        const textPairs: [string, string][] = [
          ['ink', 'bg'], ['ink', 'surface'], ['ink-2', 'bg'], ['ink-2', 'surface'], ['link', 'bg'], ['link', 'surface'],
          ['brand-ink', 'brand'], ['brand-ink-2', 'brand'], ['btn-ink', 'btn'], ['up', 'bg'], ['ink-2', 'map-sheet'],
        ]
        for (const [fg, bg] of textPairs) {
          expect(isHex(t[fg]) && isHex(t[bg]), `${fg}/${bg} are hex`).toBe(true)
          expect(contrastRatio(t[fg], t[bg]), `${fg} on ${bg}`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5)
        }
      })

      it('form-field borders and the focus ink bar reach 3:1', () => {
        expect(contrastRatio(t['field-border'], t.bg)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
        expect(contrastRatio(t['focus-ink'], t.focus)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)
      })
    })
  }
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `web/src/lib/color.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Colour maths for the palette test (not used at run time): WCAG 2 contrast and OKLab ΔE ×100,
 * optionally under the Machado, Oliveira & Fernandes (2009) severity-1.0 colour-vision-deficiency
 * simulation — the same model and thresholds as the dataviz skill's validator
 * (CVD target ≥ 8, normal-vision floor ≥ 15, marks ≥ 3:1).
 */
export type Cvd = 'protan' | 'deutan' | 'tritan'

type Row = readonly [number, number, number]
const MACHADO: Record<Cvd, readonly [Row, Row, Row]> = {
  protan: [[0.152286, 1.052583, -0.204868], [0.114503, 0.786281, 0.099216], [-0.003882, -0.048116, 1.051998]],
  deutan: [[0.367322, 0.860646, -0.227968], [0.280085, 0.672501, 0.047413], [-0.01182, 0.04294, 0.968881]],
  tritan: [[1.255528, -0.076749, -0.178779], [-0.078411, 0.930809, 0.147602], [0.004733, 0.691367, 0.3039]],
}

function linear(hex: string): [number, number, number] {
  const h = hex.trim().replace(/^#/, '')
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h)) throw new Error(`not a #rrggbb colour: ${hex}`)
  const ch = (i: number) => {
    const c = parseInt(h.slice(i, i + 2), 16) / 255
    return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4
  }
  return [ch(0), ch(2), ch(4)]
}

function luminance(hex: string): number {
  const [r, g, b] = linear(hex)
  return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b
}

export function contrastRatio(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [hi, lo] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((x, y) => y - x)
  return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05)
}

function oklab([r, g, b]: [number, number, number]): [number, number, number] {
  const l = Math.cbrt(0.4122214708 * r + 0.5363325363 * g + 0.0514459929 * b)
  const m = Math.cbrt(0.2119034982 * r + 0.6806995451 * g + 0.1073969566 * b)
  const s = Math.cbrt(0.0883024619 * r + 0.2817188376 * g + 0.6299787005 * b)
  return [
    0.2104542553 * l + 0.793617785 * m - 0.0040720468 * s,
    1.9779984951 * l - 2.428592205 * m + 0.4505937099 * s,
    0.0259040371 * l + 0.7827717662 * m - 0.808675766 * s,
  ]
}

function simulate(rgb: [number, number, number], cvd: Cvd): [number, number, number] {
  const M = MACHADO[cvd]
  const clamp = (c: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, c))
  return [0, 1, 2].map((i) => clamp(M[i][0] * rgb[0] + M[i][1] * rgb[1] + M[i][2] * rgb[2])) as [number, number, number]
}

/** Euclidean distance in OKLab ×100; with `cvd`, both colours are simulated first. */
export function deltaE(a: string, b: string, cvd?: Cvd): number {
  const la = cvd ? simulate(linear(a), cvd) : linear(a)
  const lb = cvd ? simulate(linear(b), cvd) : linear(b)
  const [p, q] = [oklab(la), oklab(lb)]
  return 100 * Math.hypot(p[0] - q[0], p[1] - q[1], p[2] - q[2])
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Status helpers, glyph component and status CSS**

Create `web/src/lib/status.ts`:

```ts
/** The six statuses of the Emerging definition, in display order, and their glyph shapes. */
export const STATUSES = ['EMERGING', 'WATCH', 'HOT', 'DECLINING', 'STABLE', 'LOW_DATA'] as const
export type StatusId = (typeof STATUSES)[number]
/** Statuses that carry a signal (they get CI whiskers, map markers and forecast attention). */
export const SIGNAL_STATUSES = ['EMERGING', 'WATCH', 'HOT', 'DECLINING'] as const
export type SignalStatus = (typeof SIGNAL_STATUSES)[number]

export function isStatus(v: unknown): v is StatusId {
  return typeof v === 'string' && (STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(v)
}

export function isSignal(s: StatusId): s is SignalStatus {
  return (SIGNAL_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(s)
}

const f = (n: number) => Number(n.toFixed(2))

/** SVG path of a status glyph centred on 0,0: ▲ EMERGING, ◆ WATCH, ● HOT, ▼ DECLINING, ■ STABLE, ○ LOW_DATA (dashed in CSS). */
export function glyphPath(status: StatusId, r: number): string {
  const a = r * 1.25
  switch (status) {
    case 'EMERGING':
      return `M0,${f(-a)}L${f(a * 0.93)},${f(a * 0.62)}L${f(-a * 0.93)},${f(a * 0.62)}Z`
    case 'DECLINING':
      return `M0,${f(a)}L${f(a * 0.93)},${f(-a * 0.62)}L${f(-a * 0.93)},${f(-a * 0.62)}Z`
    case 'WATCH':
      return `M0,${f(-a)}L${f(a)},0L0,${f(a)}L${f(-a)},0Z`
    case 'STABLE': {
      const q = f(r * 0.86)
      return `M${-q},${-q}H${q}V${q}H${-q}Z`
    }
    default:
      return `M${f(-r)},0A${f(r)},${f(r)} 0 1,0 ${f(r)},0A${f(r)},${f(r)} 0 1,0 ${f(-r)},0Z`
  }
}
```

Create `web/src/components/StatusGlyph.tsx`:

```tsx
import { glyphPath, type StatusId } from '../lib/status'

/** Decorative status glyph; the status name is always written next to it. */
export function StatusGlyph({ status, size = 14 }: { status: StatusId; size?: number }) {
  return (
    <svg className={`st-icon st-${status}`} width={size} height={size} viewBox="-8 -8 16 16" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
      <path className="st-mark" d={glyphPath(status, 5.6)} />
    </svg>
  )
}

/** Status tag: glyph + tinted background + the status name (never colour alone). */
export function StatusChip({ status, label, small = false }: { status: StatusId; label: string; small?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <span className={`chip chip-${status}${small ? ' chip-sm' : ''}`}>
      <StatusGlyph status={status} size={small ? 11 : 12} />
      <span>{label}</span>
    </span>
  )
}
```

Create `web/src/styles/status.css`:

```css
/* Status glyphs and tags — shape + colour + label, never colour alone (DESIGN.md). */
@layer components {
  .st-icon { flex: none; overflow: visible; display: inline-block; vertical-align: -0.12em; }
  .st-mark, .mk { stroke: var(--bg); stroke-width: 1.4; paint-order: stroke; }
  .chip .st-mark { stroke: none; }
  .st-EMERGING .st-mark, .mk-EMERGING { fill: var(--st-EMERGING); }
  .st-WATCH .st-mark, .mk-WATCH { fill: var(--st-WATCH); }
  .st-HOT .st-mark, .mk-HOT { fill: var(--st-HOT); }
  .st-DECLINING .st-mark, .mk-DECLINING { fill: var(--st-DECLINING); }
  .st-STABLE .st-mark, .mk-STABLE { fill: var(--st-STABLE); }
  .st-LOW_DATA .st-mark, .mk-LOW_DATA { fill: var(--bg); stroke: var(--st-LOW_DATA); stroke-width: 1.6; stroke-dasharray: 2.2 1.6; }

  .chip { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 2px 8px 1px 6px; font-weight: 700; font-size: 0.875rem; line-height: 1.5; white-space: nowrap; }
  .chip-sm { font-size: 0.8125rem; padding: 1px 6px 0 5px; }
  .chip-EMERGING { background: var(--tag-EMERGING-bg); color: var(--tag-EMERGING-ink); }
  .chip-WATCH { background: var(--tag-WATCH-bg); color: var(--tag-WATCH-ink); }
  .chip-HOT { background: var(--tag-HOT-bg); color: var(--tag-HOT-ink); }
  .chip-DECLINING { background: var(--tag-DECLINING-bg); color: var(--tag-DECLINING-ink); }
  .chip-STABLE { background: var(--tag-STABLE-bg); color: var(--tag-STABLE-ink); }
  .chip-LOW_DATA { background: var(--tag-LOW_DATA-bg); color: var(--tag-LOW_DATA-ink); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--border); }

  @media (forced-colors: active) {
    .mk, .st-mark { forced-color-adjust: none; }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Self-hosted Sarabun** — create `web/scripts/vendor-fonts.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Copy the Sarabun files the Atlas ships (Thai + Latin subsets, weights 400/600/700, woff2 only)
// and the SIL OFL 1.1 licence from @fontsource/sarabun into src/assets/fonts/.
// Run after changing the font set:  npm run vendor:fonts
import { copyFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs'
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

const web = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..')
const pkg = join(web, 'node_modules', '@fontsource', 'sarabun')
const out = join(web, 'src', 'assets', 'fonts')
mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true })
for (const subset of ['thai', 'latin']) {
  for (const weight of [400, 600, 700]) {
    const name = `sarabun-${subset}-${weight}-normal.woff2`
    copyFileSync(join(pkg, 'files', name), join(out, name))
  }
}
copyFileSync(join(pkg, 'LICENSE'), join(out, 'OFL.txt'))
console.log(`[fonts] Sarabun (thai+latin × 400/600/700) → ${out}`)
```

Add the scripts to `web/package.json` `"scripts"` (keep the existing ones):

```json
    "test": "vitest run",
    "vendor:fonts": "node scripts/vendor-fonts.mjs",
```

Expected: `[fonts] Sarabun (thai+latin × 400/600/700) → …src\assets\fonts`; the folder has 6 `.woff2` files (≈ 10–14 KB each) and `OFL.txt`.

- [ ] **Step 10: TCI brand — one place to swap the logo**

Create `web/src/brand/brand.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The ONE place that defines the TCI brand on the site.
 * Today: a temporary text wordmark (no TCI logo is imitated).
 * Official logo: put the file in src/brand/assets/, `import logoUrl from './assets/tci-logo.svg'`,
 * set `logo: { src: logoUrl, alt: { th: '…', en: '…' }, width: 40, height: 40 }` and `temporary: false`.
 * Vite fingerprints it for Pages and inlines it in the offline file; nothing else changes.
 */
export interface BrandLogo {
  src: string
  alt: { th: string; en: string }
  width: number
  height: number
}

export const BRAND: { name: string; mark: string; logo: BrandLogo | null; temporary: boolean } = {
  name: 'TCI Research Atlas',
  mark: 'TCI',
  logo: null,
  temporary: true,
}
```

Create `web/src/brand/Wordmark.tsx`:

```tsx
import { BRAND } from './brand'

/** Header wordmark: the official logo when configured in brand.ts, else the temporary "TCI" text mark. */
export function Wordmark({ sub, lang }: { sub: string; lang: 'th' | 'en' }) {
  return (
    <span className="wm">
      {BRAND.logo ? (
        <img className="wm-logo" src={BRAND.logo.src} alt={BRAND.logo.alt[lang]} width={BRAND.logo.width} height={BRAND.logo.height} />
      ) : (
        <span className="wm-box" aria-hidden="true">{BRAND.mark}</span>
      )}
      <span className="wm-text">
        <span className="wm-name">{BRAND.name}</span>
        <span className="wm-sub">{sub}</span>
      </span>
    </span>
  )
}
```

```markdown
# แบรนด์ TCI (brand)
```

### Task 2: Clean slate + toolchain + app shell (HashRouter, header band, navigation, TH/EN, dark mode, skip link)

Remove the v1 web (71-field bundle, `loader.ts`, every shadcn component, `kimi-plugin-inspect-react`, the hard-coded "200,003 บทความ 71 สาขา" in `index.html`), move to Tailwind 4, pin every dependency, and build the civic shell every page sits in. Pages are placeholders that render their `h1`; later tasks replace them.

**Files:**
- Delete: `web/src/components/ui/` (all 53 files), `web/src/sections/`, `web/src/pages/Home.tsx`, `web/src/data/atlas.ts`, `web/src/lib/loader.ts`, `web/src/lib/utils.ts`, `web/src/lib/i18n.ts` (v1), `web/src/hooks/`, `web/src/App.css`, `web/src/index.css`, `web/tailwind.config.js`, `web/postcss.config.js`, `web/components.json`, `web/public/data/atlas_data.example.json`
- Replace: `web/package.json`, `web/vite.config.ts`, `web/eslint.config.js`, `web/index.html`, `web/src/main.tsx`, `web/src/App.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/strings/{define,common,index}.ts`, `web/src/lib/{i18n,theme,scroll,useRouteFocus,useDocumentTitle}.ts`, `web/src/lib/i18n.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/components/{Layout,Header,Footer,SkipLink,PhaseBanner,LangSwitch,ThemeToggle,PageHeader}.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/pages/{OverviewPage,AtlasPage,TopicPage,EmergingPage,ComparePage,DataPage,AboutPage,BriefPage,NotFoundPage}.tsx` (placeholders except NotFound)
- Create: `web/src/styles/{index,base,layout}.css`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (repo root)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 1): `Wordmark`, `BRAND`, `tokens.css`, `fonts.css`, `status.css`.
- Produces:
  - `src/strings/define.ts`: `interface Entry {th, en}`, `defineStrings(d)`; `src/strings/index.ts`: `PARTS`, `STR`, `type StrKey`.
  - `src/lib/i18n.ts`: `type Lang`, `type Vars`, `getLang()`, `setLang(lang)`, `syncDocumentLang()`, `translate(lang, key, vars?)`, `useI18n(): I18n` with `{ lang, otherLang, t(key, vars?), pick(th, en), name({th,en}), other({th,en}), setLang }`. Initial language: `?lang=` inside the hash → `localStorage['atlas-lang']` → `th`.
  - `src/lib/theme.ts`: `type Theme`, `currentTheme()`, `setTheme(theme)`, `useTheme(): Theme` (explicit choice in `data-theme` + `localStorage['atlas-theme']`, else the OS preference).
  - `src/lib/scroll.ts`: `prefersReducedMotion()`, `scrollToId(id)` (scroll + focus, HashRouter-safe).
  - `src/lib/useDocumentTitle.ts`: `useDocumentTitle(title)`; `src/lib/useRouteFocus.ts`: `useRouteFocus()`.
  - `src/components/PageHeader.tsx`: `<PageHeader title lede? meta?: {label, value}[] children? />` (renders the page `h1`, sets `document.title`).
  - `src/components/Layout.tsx`: route layout with `<main id="main" tabIndex={-1}>`.
  - CSS classes for later tasks: `.container .page-h .lede .meta .layout .main-col .rail .toc .sec .intro .hint .banner .loading`; Tailwind theme colours `bg-surface text-ink text-ink-2 border-rule border-rule-2 text-link bg-brand`.
  - Route table: `/brief` outside the layout; `/` → `/overview`; `overview`, `atlas`, `topics/:id`, `emerging`, `compare`, `data`, `about`, `*`.

Replace `web/vite.config.ts`:

```ts
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'

// base './' — the site works from any sub-path (GitHub Pages) and data is fetched relative to it.
export default defineConfig({
  base: './',
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  server: { port: 3000 },
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the failing i18n test** — create `web/src/lib/i18n.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { PARTS, STR } from '../strings'
import { translate } from './i18n'

const placeholders = (s: string) => [...s.matchAll(/\{(\w+)\}/g)].map((m) => m[1]).sort()

describe('i18n strings', () => {
  it('every key has Thai and English text with the same placeholders', () => {
    for (const [key, e] of Object.entries(STR)) {
      expect(e.th.trim(), `${key} th`).not.toBe('')
      expect(e.en.trim(), `${key} en`).not.toBe('')
      expect(placeholders(e.th), key).toEqual(placeholders(e.en))
    }
  })

  it('no key is defined in two string files', () => {
    const total = Object.values(PARTS).reduce((n, part) => n + Object.keys(part).length, 0)
    expect(total).toBe(Object.keys(STR).length)
  })

  it('no italics markup in Thai and no Thai script in English', () => {
    for (const [key, e] of Object.entries(STR)) {
      expect(/<\/?(i|em)\b/.test(e.th), key).toBe(false)
      expect(/[\u0E00-\u0E7F]/.test(e.en), `${key} en contains Thai`).toBe(false)
    }
  })

  it('fills placeholders and leaves unknown ones visible', () => {
    expect(translate('en', 'topicsN', { n: 3 })).toBe('3 topics')
    expect(translate('th', 'topicsN', { n: 1200 })).toBe('1200 หัวข้อ')
    expect(translate('th', 'topicsN', {})).toBe('{n} หัวข้อ')
  })
})
```

Run: `npx vitest run src/lib/i18n.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL — `Failed to resolve import "../strings"`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Strings and i18n**

Create `web/src/strings/define.ts`:

```ts
/** One UI string in both languages. Every string the UI shows is an Entry in src/strings/*.ts. */
export interface Entry {
  th: string
  en: string
}

export function defineStrings<T extends Record<string, Entry>>(d: T): T {
  return d
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/i18n.ts`:

```ts
import { useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
import { STR, type StrKey } from '../strings'
import type { Entry } from '../strings/define'

export type Lang = 'th' | 'en'
export type Vars = Record<string, string | number>

const KEY = 'atlas-lang'
const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
let current: Lang = detectLang()

/** ?lang= inside the hash (shared links, the PDF brief) → the visitor's last choice → Thai. */
function detectLang(): Lang {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'th'
  const fromUrl = new URLSearchParams(window.location.hash.split('?')[1] ?? '').get('lang')
  if (fromUrl === 'th' || fromUrl === 'en') return fromUrl
  try {
    const saved = window.localStorage.getItem(KEY)
    if (saved === 'th' || saved === 'en') return saved
  } catch {
    /* storage blocked: keep the default */
  }
  return 'th'
}

export function getLang(): Lang {
  return current
}

export function syncDocumentLang(): void {
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') document.documentElement.lang = current
}

export function setLang(lang: Lang): void {
  if (lang === current) return
  current = lang
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(KEY, lang)
  } catch {
    /* storage blocked: the choice lasts for this page view */
  }
  syncDocumentLang()
  listeners.forEach((l) => l())
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener)
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener)
  }
}

export function translate(lang: Lang, key: StrKey, vars?: Vars): string {
  const entry: Entry = STR[key]
  const s = entry[lang]
  return vars ? s.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (m, v: string) => (vars[v] != null ? String(vars[v]) : m)) : s
}

export interface I18n {
  lang: Lang
  otherLang: Lang
  t: (key: StrKey, vars?: Vars) => string
  pick: (th: string, en: string) => string
  name: (o: Entry) => string
  other: (o: Entry) => string
  setLang: (lang: Lang) => void
}

export function useI18n(): I18n {
  const lang = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getLang, (): Lang => 'th')
  return useMemo<I18n>(
    () => ({
      lang,
      otherLang: lang === 'th' ? 'en' : 'th',
      t: (key, vars) => translate(lang, key, vars),
      pick: (th, en) => (lang === 'en' ? en : th),
      name: (o) => (lang === 'en' ? o.en || o.th : o.th || o.en),
      other: (o) => (lang === 'en' ? o.th : o.en),
      setLang,
    }),
    [lang],
  )
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/theme.ts`:

```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'

export type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'

const KEY = 'atlas-theme'
const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
const media = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function' ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)') : null
media?.addEventListener('change', () => listeners.forEach((l) => l()))

/** The explicit choice (data-theme, set before first paint by index.html) or else the OS preference. */
export function currentTheme(): Theme {
  const chosen = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') : null
  if (chosen === 'light' || chosen === 'dark') return chosen
  return media?.matches ? 'dark' : 'light'
}

export function setTheme(theme: Theme): void {
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(KEY, theme)
  } catch {
    /* storage blocked: the choice lasts for this page view */
  }
  listeners.forEach((l) => l())
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener)
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener)
  }
}

export function useTheme(): Theme {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, currentTheme, (): Theme => 'light')
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/scroll.ts`:

```ts
export function prefersReducedMotion(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches === true
}

/** HashRouter-safe replacement for href="#id": scroll the element into view and move focus to its heading. */
export function scrollToId(id: string): void {
  const el = document.getElementById(id)
  if (!el) return
  el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: prefersReducedMotion() ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' })
  const target = el.matches('main, h1, h2, h3') ? el : (el.querySelector<HTMLElement>('h1, h2, h3') ?? el)
  if (!target.hasAttribute('tabindex')) target.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1')
  target.focus({ preventScroll: true })
}
```

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react'

export function useDocumentTitle(title: string): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = `${title} · TCI Research Atlas`
  }, [title])
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/useRouteFocus.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { useLocation } from 'react-router'

/** After a page change (not on first load, not on ?query changes): scroll to top and focus <main>. */
export function useRouteFocus(): void {
  const { pathname } = useLocation()
  const first = useRef(true)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (first.current) {
      first.current = false
      return
    }
    window.scrollTo(0, 0)
    document.getElementById('main')?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
  }, [pathname])
}
```

```tsx
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { setTheme, useTheme } from '../lib/theme'

/** A toggle button: the label stays "Dark mode", aria-pressed says whether it is on. */
export function ThemeToggle() {
  const theme = useTheme()
  const { t } = useI18n()
  const dark = theme === 'dark'
  return (
    <button type="button" className="themebtn" aria-pressed={dark} onClick={() => setTheme(dark ? 'light' : 'dark')}>
      <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
        <path d="M8 1.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 5 5 0 0 1 0-13z" fill="currentColor" />
        <circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6.25" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" />
      </svg>
      <span>{t('theme.dark')}</span>
    </button>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Link, NavLink } from 'react-router'
import { Wordmark } from '../brand/Wordmark'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import type { StrKey } from '../strings'
import { LangSwitch } from './LangSwitch'
import { ThemeToggle } from './ThemeToggle'

const NAV: readonly { to: string; key: StrKey }[] = [
  { to: '/overview', key: 'nav.overview' },
  { to: '/atlas', key: 'nav.atlas' },
  { to: '/emerging', key: 'nav.emerging' },
  { to: '/compare', key: 'nav.compare' },
  { to: '/data', key: 'nav.data' },
  { to: '/about', key: 'nav.about' },
]

export function Header() {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  return (
    <>
      <header className="hdr">
        <div className="container hdr-row">
          <Link to="/overview" className="hdr-wm">
            <Wordmark sub={t('brandSub')} lang={lang} />
          </Link>
          <div className="hdr-tools">
            <LangSwitch />
            <ThemeToggle />
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>
      <nav className="nav" aria-label={t('nav.label')}>
        <div className="container nav-row">
          <ul className="nav-list">
            {NAV.map((item) => (
              <li key={item.to}>
                <NavLink to={item.to} className="nav-link">
                  {t(item.key)}
                </NavLink>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <div className="nav-search" />
        </div>
      </nav>
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../lib/useDocumentTitle'

export interface MetaItem {
  label: string
  value: string
}

/** The page h1 (+ lede and civic release metadata). Every page renders exactly one. */
export function PageHeader({ title, lede, meta, children }: { title: string; lede?: ReactNode; meta?: MetaItem[]; children?: ReactNode }) {
  useDocumentTitle(title)
  return (
    <div className="page-h">
      <h1>{title}</h1>
      {lede ? <p className="lede">{lede}</p> : null}
      {meta && meta.length > 0 ? (
        <dl className="meta">
          {meta.map((m) => (
            <div key={m.label}>
              <dt>{m.label}</dt>
              <dd>{m.value}</dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      ) : null}
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Replace `web/src/App.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router'
import { Layout } from './components/Layout'
import AboutPage from './pages/AboutPage'
import AtlasPage from './pages/AtlasPage'
import BriefPage from './pages/BriefPage'
import ComparePage from './pages/ComparePage'
import DataPage from './pages/DataPage'
import EmergingPage from './pages/EmergingPage'
import NotFoundPage from './pages/NotFoundPage'
import OverviewPage from './pages/OverviewPage'
import TopicPage from './pages/TopicPage'

export default function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/brief" element={<BriefPage />} />
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route index element={<Navigate to="/overview" replace />} />
        <Route path="overview" element={<OverviewPage />} />
        <Route path="atlas" element={<AtlasPage />} />
        <Route path="topics/:id" element={<TopicPage />} />
        <Route path="emerging" element={<EmergingPage />} />
        <Route path="compare" element={<ComparePage />} />
        <Route path="data" element={<DataPage />} />
        <Route path="about" element={<AboutPage />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  )
}
```

```css
/* Element defaults (cascade layer "base", so Tailwind utilities still win). Thai: never italic. */
@layer base {
  html { -webkit-text-size-adjust: 100%; text-size-adjust: 100%; }
  body { margin: 0; background: var(--bg); color: var(--ink); font-family: var(--font); font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.65; font-kerning: normal; overflow-x: hidden; }
  @media (min-width: 641px) { body { font-size: 1.1875rem; } }
  em, i, cite, dfn, var, address { font-style: normal; }
  em, i { font-weight: 600; }
  ::selection { background: var(--focus); color: var(--focus-ink); }
  a { color: var(--link); text-decoration: underline; text-decoration-thickness: max(1px, 0.0625rem); text-underline-offset: 0.15em; }
  a:hover { color: var(--link-hover); text-decoration-thickness: max(3px, 0.1875rem); }
  a:focus-visible, a:focus-visible:hover { outline: 3px solid transparent; color: var(--focus-ink); background: var(--focus); box-shadow: 0 -2px var(--focus), 0 4px var(--focus-ink); text-decoration: none; }
  button:focus-visible, input:focus-visible, select:focus-visible, summary:focus-visible, [role="tab"]:focus-visible, [role="option"]:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid var(--focus); outline-offset: 0; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--focus-ink); }
  button { font: inherit; color: inherit; cursor: pointer; }
  h1, h2, h3 { margin: 0; font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.35; text-wrap: balance; }
  p { margin: 0; }
  main:focus, h1:focus, h2:focus, h3:focus { outline: none; }
  table { border-collapse: collapse; }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *, *::before, *::after { animation: none !important; transition: none !important; scroll-behavior: auto !important; }
  }
}
```

```css
  .nav-link:hover { color: var(--link); text-decoration: underline; }

  .phase { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-2); }

  .phase-tag { flex: none; display: inline-block; padding: 2px 8px 1px; background: var(--brand); color: var(--brand-ink); font-weight: 700; font-size: 0.875rem; }
  .phase-tag.is-sample { background: var(--tag-WATCH-bg); color: var(--tag-WATCH-ink); box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--st-WATCH); }

  main { display: block; min-height: 50vh; }

  .page-h { padding-block: 34px 8px; }
  .page-h h1 { font-size: clamp(2rem, 1.4rem + 2.2vw, 3rem); line-height: 1.3; max-width: 34ch; }
  .page-h .lede { margin-top: 14px; font-size: clamp(1.125rem, 1rem + 0.4vw, 1.5rem); line-height: 1.6; max-width: 40em; }
  .meta { margin: 22px 0 0; display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  .meta div { padding: 10px 14px 10px 0; min-width: 0; }
  .meta div + div { padding-left: 14px; border-left: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  .meta dt { font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--ink-2); }
  .meta dd { margin: 0; font-weight: 600; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5; }
  @media (max-width: 760px) {
    .meta { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; }
    .meta div:nth-child(3) { border-left: 0; padding-left: 0; }
    .meta div:nth-child(n+3) { border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  }

  .layout { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 240px); gap: 0 48px; padding-top: 18px; }
  .main-col, .rail { min-width: 0; }
  .toc { position: sticky; top: 16px; padding-top: 22px; }
  .toc h2 { font-size: 1.1875rem; }
  .toc ol { margin: 8px 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; border-left: 1px solid var(--border); }
  .toc li { padding: 4px 0 4px 14px; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5; }
  @media (max-width: 900px) { .layout { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } .rail { order: -1; } .toc { position: static; padding-top: 10px; } }
  .sec { padding-top: 40px; scroll-margin-top: 16px; }
  .sec > h2 { font-size: clamp(1.5rem, 1.2rem + 1vw, 2.25rem); line-height: 1.35; }
  .sec .intro { margin-top: 10px; max-width: 38em; }
  .hint { color: var(--ink-2); font-size: 0.9375rem; }

  .ftr { margin-top: 64px; background: var(--surface); border-top: 1px solid var(--border); padding-block: 34px 44px; }
  .ftr-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 2fr) minmax(0, 1fr); gap: 24px 44px; }
  .ftr h2 { font-size: 1.375rem; }
  .ftr p { margin-top: 10px; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.75; max-width: 42em; }
  .ftr ul { margin: 10px 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; display: grid; gap: 10px; font-size: 1rem; }
  .ftr .small { font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--ink-2); }
  .licence { margin-top: 26px; padding-top: 18px; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px 24px; font-size: 0.9375rem; }
  @media (max-width: 820px) { .ftr-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }

  .banner { margin-top: 16px; padding: 14px 18px; border-left: 6px solid var(--st-HOT); background: var(--surface); }
  .banner h2 { font-size: 1.1875rem; }
  .banner p { margin-top: 4px; font-size: 1rem; }
  .banner .detail { font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--ink-2); overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
  .loading { padding-block: 40px; color: var(--ink-2); }
}
```

### Task 3: Fixtures + DataSource (Http/Embedded) + release loader + banners + Playwright smoke harness

The web reads releases at run time and never ships data. This task builds the synthetic test releases (an example with every status and edge case, and an empty one), the two data sources, the loader with its failure modes (missing / network / damaged / wrong schema), the "no white page" banners, the dev/test servers, and the Playwright smoke spec every later page task extends.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/scripts/make-fixtures.mjs`, `web/fixtures/example/data/**`, `web/fixtures/empty/data/**` (generated, committed)
- Create: `web/src/data/types.ts`, `web/src/data/source.ts`, `web/src/data/release.ts`, `web/src/data/atlasContext.ts`, `web/src/data/AtlasProvider.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/data/source.test.ts`, `web/src/data/release.test.ts`, `web/src/data/fixtures.test.ts`, `web/test/fixtureData.ts` (test helper, outside the app build)
- Create: `web/src/components/DataBanner.tsx`, `web/src/components/RequireRelease.tsx`
- Create: `web/scripts/serve.mjs`, `web/playwright.config.ts`, `web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`
- Modify: `web/vite.config.ts` (dev data middleware), `web/src/main.tsx` (source + provider), `web/src/components/Layout.tsx`, `PhaseBanner.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `web/src/styles/layout.css` (append `.linkbtn`), `web/package.json` (scripts)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: generated `src/data/contract.ts` (P1a Task 15), `useI18n`, `PageHeader`.
- Produces:
  - `src/data/types.ts`: container types re-exported from the generated file (`EdgesFile KeywordsFile Latest Manifest Overview SeriesFile TaxonomyFile TopicDetail TopicsFile`) and nested types derived by indexed access (`Topic TopicMetrics TopicForecast TopicNowcast Status GroupInfo EmergingKeyword Edge Kpis Brief RepresentativeArticle CountItem`) — the generator names the Topic model `Topic1` because `EmergingKeyword.topic` claims `Topic`; `interface Bundle { latest: Latest; files: Record<string, unknown> }`.
  - `src/data/source.ts`: `type DataErrorKind = 'missing' | 'network' | 'parse' | 'schema'`, `class DataError {kind, detail}`, `toDataError(e)`, `interface DataSource { kind: 'http' | 'embedded'; latest(); file<T>(release, path): Promise<T | null> }`, `dataUrl(base, path)`, `class HttpSource(base, fetchImpl?)`, `class EmbeddedSource(bundle)`, `sourceFromEmbeddedText(text, base, fetchImpl?)` (never throws).
  - `src/data/release.ts`: `SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_MAJOR = 2`, `interface AtlasRelease { source, latest, manifest, overview, taxonomy, topics, series, edges, keywords | null, previousOverview | null, topicById, groupById, ciLevel, definitionVersion, sample }`, `schemaMajor(v)`, `loadRelease(source)`, `createDetailLoader(source, release)`, `seriesOf(release, id)`, `groupName(release, groupId, lang)`.
  - `src/data/atlasContext.ts`: `type AtlasState`, `AtlasContext`, `useAtlas()`, `useRelease(): AtlasRelease | null`, `type DetailState`, `useTopicDetail(id)`.
  - `src/data/AtlasProvider.tsx`: `<AtlasProvider source>`; `src/components/RequireRelease.tsx`: `<RequireRelease title>{(release) => …}</RequireRelease>`; `src/components/DataBanner.tsx`.
  - Fixture ids used by later tests: groups `F27 F33 F14 F11 F22 F12 F17 F29`; topics `T000101…T000806` (`T000{group 1–8}0{topic 1–6}`); notable: `T000201` AI in education (EMERGING, cross_field), `T000701` large language models (EMERGING, `no_baseline`, forecast hidden), `T000101` (HOT, `orgs_unverified`), `T000601` Buddhism online (WATCH, journal-driven), `T000205` print media (DECLINING), `T000402` (LOW_DATA few_articles), `T000706` (LOW_DATA not_estimable, forecast null). Example releases `2026Q4` (previous `2026Q3`, overview + manifest only); empty release `2026Q4` (no keywords module, `brief: null`).
  - npm scripts `fixtures`, `e2e`; `scripts/serve.mjs` exports `startServer({ site, data?, port? })`.
  - `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` markers `// checks:start` / `// checks:end` inside `CHECKS` for page tasks.
