```js
const STATUS_TH = { EMERGING: 'กำลังเกิดใหม่', WATCH: 'จับตา', HOT: 'กำลังมาแรง', DECLINING: 'ลดลง', STABLE: 'คงที่', LOW_DATA: 'ข้อมูลน้อย' }

function counts(status, flag) {
  if (flag === 'no_baseline') return YEARS.map((y) => (y < 2022 ? 0 : [18, 46, 95][y - 2022]))
  if (flag === 'flat') return YEARS.map((y) => (y < 2022 ? 0 : [11, 12, 10][y - 2022]))
  const base = { EMERGING: between(2, 4), WATCH: between(3, 6), HOT: between(150, 230), DECLINING: between(70, 130), STABLE: between(40, 120), LOW_DATA: between(1, 2.6) }[status]
  const g = { EMERGING: between(0.36, 0.46), WATCH: between(0.06, 0.1), HOT: between(0.1, 0.15), DECLINING: between(-0.1, -0.05), STABLE: between(0.02, 0.06), LOW_DATA: 0 }[status]
  return YEARS.map((y, i) => {
    let v = base * (1 + g) ** i
    if (status === 'WATCH' && y >= 2021) v *= 4.2 // a journal is indexed and uploads its back issues
    return Math.max(status === 'LOW_DATA' ? 1 : 0, Math.round(v * (0.94 + 0.12 * rand())))
  })
}

/** exp(OLS slope of ln share over the trend window) − 1, on years with articles. */
function shareGrowth(cs, totals) {
  const pts = W.map((y) => [y, cs[YEARS.indexOf(y)] / totals[YEARS.indexOf(y)]]).filter(([, s]) => s > 0)
  if (pts.length < 3) return null
  const mx = sum(pts.map(([x]) => x)) / pts.length
  const my = sum(pts.map(([, s]) => Math.log(s))) / pts.length
  const b = sum(pts.map(([x, s]) => (x - mx) * (Math.log(s) - my))) / sum(pts.map(([x]) => (x - mx) ** 2))
  return Math.exp(b) - 1
}

function interval(status, g) {
  if (g === null) return [null, null]
  if (status === 'EMERGING' || status === 'WATCH') return [Math.max(0.12, g - 0.12), g + 0.16]
  if (status === 'HOT') return [Math.max(0.01, g - 0.035), g + 0.045]
  if (status === 'DECLINING') return [g - 0.06, Math.min(-0.01, g + 0.04)]
  if (status === 'STABLE') return [g - 0.07, g + 0.07]
  return [g - 0.45, g + 0.45]
}

function forecast(status, cs, flag) {
  if (status === 'LOW_DATA') return null
  const show = status === 'HOT' || (status === 'EMERGING' && flag !== 'no_baseline') || (status === 'DECLINING' && rand() < 0.6) || (status === 'STABLE' && rand() < 0.4)
  if (!show) return { model: 'naive', mase: r4(between(1.02, 1.4)), skill_vs_naive: r4(between(-0.3, -0.01)), show: false, years: [], mean: [], lo80: [], hi80: [], lo95: [], hi95: [] }
  const last = cs[cs.length - 1]
  const g = cs[cs.length - 4] > 0 ? (last / cs[cs.length - 4]) ** (1 / 3) - 1 : 0
  const mean = [r1(last * (1 + g)), r1(last * (1 + g) ** 2)]
  const band = (f) => mean.map((m, i) => r1(m * f[i]))
  return {
    model: status === 'STABLE' ? 'ets_damped' : 'nb_glm', mase: r4(between(0.55, 0.95)), skill_vs_naive: r4(between(0.08, 0.4)), show: true,
    years: [2025, 2026], mean, lo80: band([0.85, 0.78]), hi80: band([1.16, 1.25]), lo95: band([0.76, 0.66]), hi95: band([1.27, 1.4]),
  }
}

function lineage(flag, id) {
  if (flag === 'no_baseline') return { born: '2026Q3', event: 'new', parents: [] }
  if (flag === 'split') return { born: '2026Q2', event: 'split', parents: ['T000105'] }
  if (id === 'T000706') return { born: '2026Q4', event: 'merge', parents: ['T000703', 'T000704'] }
  return { born: '2026Q1', event: 'continue', parents: [] }
}

function buildExample() {
  const specs = []
  GROUPS.forEach(([, , , , , topics], gi) => topics.forEach((tp, ti) => specs.push({ gi, ti, id: `T000${gi + 1}0${ti + 1}`, th: tp[0], en: tp[1], keywords: tp[2], status: tp[3], flag: tp[4] ?? null })))
  for (const s of specs) s.counts = counts(s.status, s.flag)
  const other = YEARS.map((_, i) => 1000 + 60 * i)
  const totals = YEARS.map((_, i) => sum(specs.map((s) => s.counts[i])) + other[i])
  const totalR = sum(at(YEARS, totals, R))
  const totalB = sum(at(YEARS, totals, B))

  for (const s of specs) {
    s.countR = sum(at(YEARS, s.counts, R))
    s.shareR = s.countR / totalR
    s.shareB = sum(at(YEARS, s.counts, B)) / totalB
    s.g = s.flag === 'flat' ? null : shareGrowth(s.counts, totals)
    ;[s.lo, s.hi] = interval(s.status, s.g)
  }
  const maxShare = Math.max(...specs.map((s) => s.shareR))

  const topics = specs.map((s) => {
    const [hx, hy] = HUBS[s.gi]
    const angle = ((s.ti * 60 + between(-15, 15)) * Math.PI) / 180
    const dist = between(45, 85)
    const fc = forecast(s.status, s.counts, s.flag)
    const signal = ['EMERGING', 'WATCH', 'HOT', 'DECLINING'].includes(s.status)
    const group = GROUPS[s.gi]
    return {
      id: s.id, group: group[0], th: s.th, en: s.en,
      description: `ตัวอย่าง: งานวิจัยเกี่ยวกับ${s.th}`,
      keywords: s.keywords, label_source: 'keywords', size: sum(s.counts),
      x: r1(hx + Math.cos(angle) * dist), y: r1(hy + Math.sin(angle) * dist), r: r1(6 + 20 * Math.sqrt(s.shareR / maxShare)),
      status: s.status,
      score: r1({ EMERGING: between(70, 95), WATCH: between(55, 75), HOT: between(45, 65), DECLINING: between(5, 20), STABLE: between(15, 45), LOW_DATA: between(0, 10) }[s.status]),
      metrics: {
        count_recent: s.countR, share_recent: r6(s.shareR), share_baseline: r6(s.shareB),
        share_ratio: s.shareB === 0 ? null : r4(s.shareR / s.shareB), no_baseline: s.shareB === 0,
        share_growth: s.g === null ? null : r4(s.g), share_growth_lo: s.lo === null ? null : r4(s.lo), share_growth_hi: s.hi === null ? null : r4(s.hi),
        acceleration: r4(between(-0.05, 0.1)),
        burst_frac: { EMERGING: 0.6667, WATCH: 0.6667, HOT: 0.3333 }[s.status] ?? 0,
        burst_years: { EMERGING: [2023, 2024], WATCH: [2021, 2022], HOT: [2024] }[s.status] ?? [],
        novelty: r4(s.status === 'EMERGING' ? between(0.8, 0.95) : between(0.05, 0.4)),
        coherence: r4(between(0.55, 0.8)),
        n_journals_R: s.status === 'WATCH' ? 2 : Math.round(s.status === 'LOW_DATA' ? between(1, 3) : s.status === 'HOT' ? between(40, 90) : between(8, 40)),
        n_orgs_R: s.status === 'WATCH' ? 3 : Math.round(s.status === 'LOW_DATA' ? between(1, 4) : s.status === 'HOT' ? between(30, 80) : between(6, 45)),
        new_journal_share: r4(s.status === 'WATCH' ? between(0.6, 0.8) : between(0, 0.15)),
        journal_hhi: r4(s.status === 'WATCH' ? between(0.55, 0.7) : between(0.03, 0.2)),
        journal_driven: s.flag === 'journal', orgs_unverified: s.flag === 'orgs',
        low_data_reason: s.status !== 'LOW_DATA' ? null : s.flag === 'flat' ? 'not_estimable' : 'few_articles',
      },
      forecast: fc,
      lineage: lineage(s.flag, s.id),
      sdg: [],
      subfield: group[3], subfield_en: group[4],
      cross_field: s.flag === 'cross', taxonomy_vote: r4(s.flag === 'cross' ? 0.42 : between(0.6, 0.95)),
      nowcast: fc && fc.show ? { year: 2025, as_of_month: 9, ytd: Math.round(fc.mean[0] * 0.68), mean: fc.mean[0] } : null,
      _signal: signal,
    }
  })

  const groupSizes = GROUPS.map(([gid]) => sum(topics.filter((t) => t.group === gid).map((t) => t.size)))
  const rank = [...groupSizes.keys()].sort((a, b) => groupSizes[b] - groupSizes[a])
  const groups = rank.map((gi, r) => ({
    id: GROUPS[gi][0], th: GROUPS[gi][1], en: GROUPS[gi][2], color: `cat-${String(r + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
    size: groupSizes[gi], n_topics: 6, x: HUBS[gi][0], y: HUBS[gi][1], r: 120,
  }))
  const subfields = GROUPS.map(([gid, , , sid, sname], gi) => ({ id: sid, en: sname, group: gid, n_topics: 6, size: groupSizes[gi] }))

  const edges = []
  for (let gi = 0; gi < 8; gi++) for (let ti = 1; ti < 6; ti++) edges.push({ s: `T000${gi + 1}0${ti}`, t: `T000${gi + 1}0${ti + 1}`, w: r4(between(0.55, 0.9)) })
  edges.push({ s: 'T000201', t: 'T000701', w: 0.82 }, { s: 'T000106', t: 'T000701', w: 0.77 }, { s: 'T000305', t: 'T000701', w: 0.58 })

  const details = topics.map((t, n) => {
    const neighbors = edges.filter((e) => e.s === t.id || e.t === t.id).sort((a, b) => b.w - a.w).map((e) => (e.s === t.id ? e.t : e.s)).slice(0, 5)
    const articles = [1, 2, 3, 4].map((k) => {
      const j = (n + k) % JOURNALS.length
      const year = 2020 + ((n + k) % 5)
      const num = 10000 + n * 10 + k
      return {
        article_id: `so02:example${j}:${num}`, title: `${t.th}: กรณีศึกษาที่ ${k}`, title_en: `${t.en}: case study ${k}`, year,
        journal: JOURNALS[j], url: `https://so02.tci-thaijo.org/index.php/example${j}/article/view/${num}`, doi: `10.14456/example.${year}.${num}`, gx: null, gy: null,
      }
    })
    const share = pct(t.metrics.share_recent)
    const narrative = t._signal
      ? {
          th: `${t.th}อยู่ในสถานะ${STATUS_TH[t.status]} สัดส่วนบทความช่วง 2022–2024 อยู่ที่ ${share}${t.metrics.share_growth === null ? '' : ` และเปลี่ยนแปลง ${signed(t.metrics.share_growth)} ต่อปี`} (ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง)`,
          en: `${t.en} is ${t.status.toLowerCase()}: ${share} of articles in 2022–2024${t.metrics.share_growth === null ? '' : `, changing ${signed(t.metrics.share_growth)} a year`} (sample data).`,
          source: 'template',
        }
      : null
    return {
      schema_version: SCHEMA, id: t.id, articles,
      journals: [0, 1, 2].map((k) => ({ key: JOURNALS[(n + k) % JOURNALS.length], count: 40 - 11 * k })),
      institutions: [0, 1, 2].map((k) => ({ key: ORGS[(n + k) % ORGS.length], count: 25 - 7 * k })),
      keywords: t.keywords, neighbors, narrative,
    }
  })
  for (const t of topics) delete t._signal

  const by = (st) => topics.filter((t) => t.status === st)
  const fastest = [...by('EMERGING')].sort((a, b) => b.metrics.share_growth - a.metrics.share_growth)[0]
  const topHot = [...by('HOT')].sort((a, b) => b.metrics.share_recent - a.metrics.share_recent)[0]
  const decl = [...by('DECLINING')].sort((a, b) => a.metrics.share_growth - b.metrics.share_growth)[0]
  const watch = by('WATCH')[0]
  const brief = {
    headline_th: `รอบ ${RELEASE} มีหัวข้อกำลังเกิดใหม่ ${by('EMERGING').length} หัวข้อ และ${fastest.th}โตเร็วที่สุด ${signed(fastest.metrics.share_growth)} ต่อปี`,
    headline_en: `In ${RELEASE}, ${by('EMERGING').length} topics are emerging and ${fastest.en} is growing fastest, ${signed(fastest.metrics.share_growth)} a year`,
    bullets_th: [
      `หัวข้อกำลังมาแรง ${by('HOT').length} หัวข้อ นำโดย${topHot.th} (${pct(topHot.metrics.share_recent)} ของบทความช่วง 2022–2024)`,
      `หัวข้อที่ลดลง ${by('DECLINING').length} หัวข้อ เช่น ${decl.th} ซึ่งสัดส่วนเปลี่ยนแปลง ${signed(decl.metrics.share_growth)} ต่อปี`,
      `หัวข้อจับตา ${by('WATCH').length} หัวข้อโตจากวารสารเดียว เช่น ${watch.th} ควรตรวจสอบก่อนอ้างอิง`,
    ],
    bullets_en: [
      `${by('HOT').length} topics are hot, led by ${topHot.en} (${pct(topHot.metrics.share_recent)} of articles in 2022–2024)`,
      `${by('DECLINING').length} topics are declining, such as ${decl.en}, whose share is changing ${signed(decl.metrics.share_growth)} a year`,
      `${by('WATCH').length} watch topics grew from a single journal, such as ${watch.en}; check them before citing`,
    ],
    source: 'template',
  }

  const yearMissing = 37
  const assigned = sum(topics.map((t) => t.size))
  const articles = sum(totals) + yearMissing
  const statusCounts = Object.fromEntries(['EMERGING', 'WATCH', 'HOT', 'DECLINING', 'STABLE', 'LOW_DATA'].map((st) => [st, by(st).length]))
  const last = totals[totals.length - 1]
  const overview = {
    schema_version: SCHEMA, release: RELEASE, previous_release: PREVIOUS, generated_at: GENERATED_AT, definition_version: 3, last_complete_year: 2024,
    kpis: { articles, assigned_articles: assigned, topics: topics.length, groups: groups.length, journals: 412, institutions: 187, pct_abstract_en: 74.2, pct_doi: 61.5, pct_keywords: 96.1 },
    status_counts: statusCounts,
    corpus_by_year: YEARS.map((year, i) => ({ year, articles: totals[i] })),
    corpus_forecast: [{ year: 2025, articles: r1(last * 1.06) }, { year: 2026, articles: r1(last * 1.12) }],
    nowcast: { year: 2025, as_of_month: 9, ytd: Math.round(last * 0.71), mean: r1(last * 1.06) },
    lineage_events: { continue: 45, new: 1, split: 1, merge: 1 },
    retired_topics: ['T000999'],
    data_quality: { year_missing: yearMissing, unassigned_articles: sum(other), completeness_method: 'median', text_quality: { thai_pua_repaired: 214, tone_marks_missing: 19, decode_replaced: 3 } },
    brief,
  }
  const previousOverview = {
    ...overview, release: PREVIOUS, previous_release: '2026Q2', brief: null,
    kpis: { articles: articles - 1850, assigned_articles: assigned - 1700, topics: 46, groups: 8, journals: 405, institutions: 183, pct_abstract_en: 73.6, pct_doi: 60.9, pct_keywords: 95.8 },
    status_counts: { EMERGING: 4, WATCH: 2, HOT: 6, DECLINING: 5, STABLE: 25, LOW_DATA: 4 },
  }
  const keywords = [
    { term: 'chatgpt', count_recent: 212, share_ratio: null, no_baseline: true, growth: 1.42, growth_lo: 0.88, n_active_years: 3, n_orgs_R: 41, n_journals_R: 37, journal_driven: false, emerging: true, topic: 'T000701' },
    { term: 'generative ai', count_recent: 158, share_ratio: 9.4, no_baseline: false, growth: 0.95, growth_lo: 0.61, n_active_years: 4, n_orgs_R: 33, n_journals_R: 29, journal_driven: false, emerging: true, topic: 'T000201' },
    { term: 'ไมโครพลาสติก', count_recent: 64, share_ratio: 5.1, no_baseline: false, growth: 0.48, growth_lo: 0.22, n_active_years: 5, n_orgs_R: 14, n_journals_R: 12, journal_driven: false, emerging: true, topic: 'T000406' },
    { term: 'ยานยนต์ไฟฟ้า', count_recent: 77, share_ratio: 4.2, no_baseline: false, growth: 0.41, growth_lo: 0.19, n_active_years: 6, n_orgs_R: 18, n_journals_R: 15, journal_driven: false, emerging: true, topic: 'T000502' },
    { term: 'soft power', count_recent: 39, share_ratio: 3.3, no_baseline: false, growth: 0.37, growth_lo: 0.12, n_active_years: 3, n_orgs_R: 9, n_journals_R: 8, journal_driven: false, emerging: true, topic: null },
    { term: 'ธรรมะออนไลน์', count_recent: 58, share_ratio: 7.8, no_baseline: false, growth: 0.66, growth_lo: 0.3, n_active_years: 3, n_orgs_R: 3, n_journals_R: 1, journal_driven: true, emerging: false, topic: 'T000601' },
  ]
  const files = {
    'overview.json': overview,
    'taxonomy.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, source: 'openalex', version: 'fixture-1', holdout_accuracy_subfield: 0.81, holdout_accuracy_field: 0.93, groups, subfields },
    'topics.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, topics },
    'series.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, years: YEARS, corpus_total: totals, topics: Object.fromEntries(topics.map((t) => [t.id, specs.find((s) => s.id === t.id).counts])) },
    'edges.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, edges },
    'keywords.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, keywords },
    ...Object.fromEntries(details.map((d) => [`topics/${d.id}.json`, d])),
  }
  return { files, previousOverview, nOrgsUnverified: topics.filter((t) => t.metrics.orgs_unverified).length, nTopics: topics.length }
}

function manifest(release, previous, modules, entries, extra = {}) {
  return {
    schema_version: SCHEMA, release, generated_at: GENERATED_AT, pipeline_version: 'fixture', config_hash: '0000000000000000',
    definition_version: 3, ci_level: 0.9, as_of_year: 2025, trimmed_years: [2025], embedding_provider: 'fixture', embedding_model: 'fixture|none|0',
    taxonomy_version: 'fixture-1', modules, files: entries, previous_release: previous, n_orgs_unverified: 0, ingest_errors: 0,
    labels: { provider: 'off', llm: 0, keywords: 0, overrides: 0 }, llm: [], ...extra,
  }
}

function writeRelease(dataDir, release, files, makeManifest) {
  const entries = []
  for (const [path, obj] of Object.entries(files).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
    const bytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(obj), 'utf8')
    const file = join(dataDir, release, path)
    mkdirSync(dirname(file), { recursive: true })
    writeFileSync(file, bytes)
    entries.push({ path, bytes: bytes.length, sha256: createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex') })
  }
  writeFileSync(join(dataDir, release, 'manifest.json'), JSON.stringify(makeManifest(entries)))
}

function writeLatest(dataDir, release) {
  writeFileSync(join(dataDir, 'latest.json'), JSON.stringify({ release, path: `${release}/manifest.json`, schema_version: SCHEMA, generated_at: GENERATED_AT }))
}

const example = join(ROOT, 'example', 'data')
const empty = join(ROOT, 'empty', 'data')
rmSync(join(ROOT, 'example'), { recursive: true, force: true })
rmSync(join(ROOT, 'empty'), { recursive: true, force: true })

const ex = buildExample()
writeRelease(example, PREVIOUS, { 'overview.json': ex.previousOverview }, (entries) => manifest(PREVIOUS, '2026Q2', ['overview'], entries))
writeRelease(example, RELEASE, ex.files, (entries) =>
  manifest(RELEASE, PREVIOUS, ['overview', 'taxonomy', 'topics', 'topic_detail', 'series', 'edges', 'keywords'], entries, {
    n_orgs_unverified: ex.nOrgsUnverified, ingest_errors: 2, labels: { provider: 'off', llm: 0, keywords: ex.nTopics, overrides: 0 },
  }))
writeLatest(example, RELEASE)

writeRelease(empty, RELEASE, {
  'overview.json': {
    schema_version: SCHEMA, release: RELEASE, previous_release: null, generated_at: GENERATED_AT, definition_version: 3, last_complete_year: 2024,
    kpis: { articles: 0, assigned_articles: 0, topics: 0, groups: 0, journals: 0, institutions: 0, pct_abstract_en: 0, pct_doi: 0, pct_keywords: 0 },
    status_counts: {}, corpus_by_year: [], corpus_forecast: [], nowcast: null, lineage_events: {}, retired_topics: [],
    data_quality: { year_missing: 0, unassigned_articles: 0, completeness_method: 'median', text_quality: {} }, brief: null,
  },
  'taxonomy.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, source: 'agglomerative', version: null, holdout_accuracy_subfield: null, holdout_accuracy_field: null, groups: [], subfields: [] },
  'topics.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, topics: [] },
  'series.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, years: [], corpus_total: [], topics: {} },
  'edges.json': { schema_version: SCHEMA, edges: [] },
}, (entries) => manifest(RELEASE, null, ['overview', 'taxonomy', 'topics', 'topic_detail', 'series', 'edges'], entries, { trimmed_years: [] }))
writeLatest(empty, RELEASE)

console.log(`[fixtures] example ${RELEASE} (${ex.nTopics} topics, previous ${PREVIOUS}) and empty ${RELEASE} → ${ROOT}`)
```

```json
    "e2e": "playwright test",
```

```ts
import Ajv, { type ValidateFunction } from 'ajv'
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto'
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

const WEB = fileURLToPath(new URL('../..', import.meta.url))
const schema = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(WEB, 'src', 'data', 'contract.schema.json'), 'utf8')) as { definitions: Record<string, unknown> }
const ajv = new Ajv({ allErrors: true, strict: false })
const compiled = new Map<string, ValidateFunction>()
const MODEL: Record<string, string> = {
  'latest.json': 'Latest', 'manifest.json': 'Manifest', 'overview.json': 'Overview', 'taxonomy.json': 'TaxonomyFile', 'topics.json': 'TopicsFile',
  'series.json': 'SeriesFile', 'edges.json': 'EdgesFile', 'keywords.json': 'KeywordsFile',
}

function validate(path: string, value: unknown): string[] {
  const name = path.startsWith('topics/') ? 'TopicDetail' : MODEL[path]
  if (!name) return [`no contract model for ${path}`]
  let fn = compiled.get(name)
  if (!fn) {
    fn = ajv.compile({ $ref: `#/definitions/${name}`, definitions: schema.definitions })
    compiled.set(name, fn)
  }
  return fn(value) ? [] : (fn.errors ?? []).map((e) => `${path}${e.instancePath} ${e.message ?? ''}`)
}

interface ManifestLike {
  previous_release?: string | null
  files: { path: string; bytes: number; sha256: string }[]
}

function checkRelease(dataDir: string, release: string): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = []
  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dataDir, release, 'manifest.json'), 'utf8')) as ManifestLike
  problems.push(...validate('manifest.json', manifest))
  for (const f of manifest.files) {
    const bytes = readFileSync(join(dataDir, release, f.path))
    if (bytes.length !== f.bytes) problems.push(`${release}/${f.path}: ${bytes.length} bytes, manifest says ${f.bytes}`)
    if (createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex') !== f.sha256) problems.push(`${release}/${f.path}: sha256 differs`)
    problems.push(...validate(f.path, JSON.parse(bytes.toString('utf8'))))
  }
  return problems
}

for (const set of ['example', 'empty'] as const) {
  describe(`fixtures/${set}`, () => {
    const dataDir = join(WEB, 'fixtures', set, 'data')

    it('latest.json and every listed file match the contract, size and sha256', () => {
      const latest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dataDir, 'latest.json'), 'utf8')) as { release: string; path: string }
      expect(validate('latest.json', latest)).toEqual([])
      expect(latest.path).toBe(`${latest.release}/manifest.json`)
      expect(checkRelease(dataDir, latest.release)).toEqual([])
      const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dataDir, latest.path), 'utf8')) as ManifestLike
      const prev = manifest.previous_release
      if (prev && existsSync(join(dataDir, prev, 'manifest.json'))) expect(checkRelease(dataDir, prev)).toEqual([])
    })
  })
}
```

Create `web/test/fixtureData.ts` (shared by unit tests; outside `src/` so the app build never sees `node:` imports):

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join, relative, sep } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
import type { Bundle } from '../src/data/types'

export const FIXTURES = fileURLToPath(new URL('../fixtures', import.meta.url))

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(dir).flatMap((name) => {
    const p = join(dir, name)
    return statSync(p).isDirectory() ? walk(p) : [p]
  })
}

/** What `atlas publish` injects into the offline HTML: {latest, files} of the newest release. */
export function bundleOf(set: 'example' | 'empty'): Bundle {
  const data = join(FIXTURES, set, 'data')
  const latest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(data, 'latest.json'), 'utf8')) as Bundle['latest']
  const root = join(data, latest.release)
  const files: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  for (const p of walk(root)) files[relative(root, p).split(sep).join('/')] = JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, 'utf8'))
  return { latest, files }
}

/** A fetch that serves <base>data/** from a fixture set, recording every URL it was asked for. */
export function fixtureFetch(set: 'example' | 'empty', base: string, asked: string[] = []): typeof fetch {
  const data = join(FIXTURES, set, 'data')
  return (async (input: RequestInfo | URL) => {
    const url = String(input)
    asked.push(url)
    const prefix = `${base.endsWith('/') ? base : `${base}/`}data/`
    if (!url.startsWith(prefix)) return new Response('wrong base', { status: 400 })
    try {
      return new Response(readFileSync(join(data, url.slice(prefix.length))), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } })
    } catch {
      return new Response('not found', { status: 404 })
    }
  }) as typeof fetch
}
```

```ts
describe('dataUrl', () => {

describe('HttpSource', () => {

    const calls: RequestInit[] = []
    const inner = fixtureFetch('example', '/sub/', asked)

    await expect(new HttpSource('./', respond(404, 'x')).latest()).rejects.toMatchObject({ kind: 'missing' })
  })

      throw new TypeError('Failed to fetch')
    }) as typeof fetch
    await expect(new HttpSource('./', offline).latest()).rejects.toMatchObject({ kind: 'network' })
    await expect(new HttpSource('./', respond(500, 'boom')).file('2026Q4', 'topics.json')).rejects.toMatchObject({ kind: 'network' })
    await expect(new HttpSource('./', respond(200, '{"release":')).latest()).rejects.toMatchObject({ kind: 'parse' })
    await expect(new HttpSource('./', respond(200, '{"hello":1}')).latest()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DataError)
```

```ts
/** The data contract v2 types the web uses — generated from pipeline/atlas/schema/contract.py (never edit contract.ts).
 *  Nested types are derived by indexed access: json-schema-to-typescript names interfaces after titles, and a field
 *  titled "Topic" (EmergingKeyword.topic) pushes the Topic model to `Topic1` — container names are stable, nested ones are not. */
import type { EdgesFile, KeywordsFile, Latest, Overview, TaxonomyFile, TopicDetail, TopicsFile } from './contract'

export type { EdgesFile, KeywordsFile, Latest, Manifest, Overview, SeriesFile, TaxonomyFile, TopicDetail, TopicsFile } from './contract'

export type Topic = TopicsFile['topics'][number]
export type TopicMetrics = Topic['metrics']
export type TopicForecast = NonNullable<Topic['forecast']>
export type TopicNowcast = NonNullable<Topic['nowcast']>
export type Status = Topic['status']
export type GroupInfo = TaxonomyFile['groups'][number]
export type EmergingKeyword = KeywordsFile['keywords'][number]
export type Edge = EdgesFile['edges'][number]
export type Kpis = Overview['kpis']
export type Brief = NonNullable<Overview['brief']>
export type RepresentativeArticle = TopicDetail['articles'][number]
export type CountItem = NonNullable<TopicDetail['journals']>[number]

/** What `atlas publish` embeds in the offline HTML: the newest release only. */

  latest: Latest
  files: Record<string, unknown>
}
```

Create `web/src/data/source.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod'
import type { Bundle, Latest } from './types'

export type DataErrorKind = 'missing' | 'network' | 'parse' | 'schema'

/** Why data could not be shown; the banner explains each kind in words. */
export class DataError extends Error {
  readonly kind: DataErrorKind
  readonly detail: string
  constructor(kind: DataErrorKind, detail: string) {
    super(`${kind}: ${detail}`)
    this.name = 'DataError'
    this.kind = kind
    this.detail = detail
  }
}

export function toDataError(e: unknown): DataError {
  return e instanceof DataError ? e : new DataError('network', e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e))
}

export interface DataSource {
  readonly kind: 'http' | 'embedded'
  latest(): Promise<Latest>
  /** A file of a release (path relative to data/<release>/); null when that release has no such file. */
  file<T>(release: string, path: string): Promise<T | null>
}

const LatestShape = z.object({ release: z.string().min(1), path: z.string().min(1), schema_version: z.string().optional(), generated_at: z.string() })

function checkLatest(raw: unknown, where: string): Latest {
  const parsed = LatestShape.safeParse(raw)
  if (!parsed.success) throw new DataError('parse', `${where}: ${parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'invalid'}`)
  return raw as Latest
}

/** `${BASE_URL}data/<path>` — relative, so the site works from any sub-path and never asks for /data/ at the host root. */
export function dataUrl(base: string, path: string): string {
  return `${base.endsWith('/') ? base : `${base}/`}data/${path}`
}

/** GitHub Pages / any static host: fetch data/latest.json, then data/<release>/… */
export class HttpSource implements DataSource {
  readonly kind = 'http' as const
  private readonly base: string
  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch

  constructor(base: string, fetchImpl: typeof fetch = (input, init) => fetch(input, init)) {
    this.base = base
    this.fetchImpl = fetchImpl
  }

  private async get(path: string, optional: boolean): Promise<unknown> {
    const url = dataUrl(this.base, path)
    let res: Response
    try {
      res = await this.fetchImpl(url, { cache: 'no-cache' })
    } catch (e) {
      throw new DataError('network', `${url}: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`)
    }
    if (res.status === 404) {
      if (optional) return null
      throw new DataError('missing', url)
    }
    if (!res.ok) throw new DataError('network', `${url}: HTTP ${res.status}`)
    const text = await res.text()
    try {
      return JSON.parse(text) as unknown
    } catch {
      throw new DataError('parse', url)
    }
  }

  async latest(): Promise<Latest> {
    return checkLatest(await this.get('latest.json', false), 'data/latest.json')
  }

  async file<T>(release: string, path: string): Promise<T | null> {
    return (await this.get(`${release}/${path}`, true)) as T | null
  }
}

/** The single-file offline edition: the release travels inside the HTML. */
export class EmbeddedSource implements DataSource {
  readonly kind = 'embedded' as const
  private readonly bundle: Bundle

  constructor(bundle: Bundle) {
    this.bundle = bundle
  }

  async latest(): Promise<Latest> {
    return checkLatest(this.bundle.latest, 'embedded atlas-data')
  }

  async file<T>(release: string, path: string): Promise<T | null> {
    if (release !== this.bundle.latest?.release) return null
    const value = this.bundle.files[path]
    return value === undefined ? null : (value as T)
  }
}

class BrokenSource implements DataSource {
  readonly kind = 'embedded' as const
  private readonly error: DataError

  constructor(error: DataError) {
    this.error = error
  }

  latest(): Promise<Latest> {
    return Promise.reject(this.error)
  }

  file<T>(): Promise<T | null> {
    return Promise.reject(this.error)
  }
}

/** Pick the source from the <script id="atlas-data"> slot. Never throws: a damaged slot becomes a parse error on read. */
export function sourceFromEmbeddedText(text: string | null | undefined, base: string, fetchImpl?: typeof fetch): DataSource {
  const raw = (text ?? '').trim()
  if (raw === '' || raw === 'null') return new HttpSource(base, fetchImpl)
  let bundle: unknown
  try {
    bundle = JSON.parse(raw)
  } catch {
    return new BrokenSource(new DataError('parse', 'embedded atlas-data is not JSON'))
  }
  if (!bundle || typeof bundle !== 'object' || !('latest' in bundle) || !('files' in bundle)) {
    return new BrokenSource(new DataError('parse', 'embedded atlas-data has no {latest, files}'))
  }
  return new EmbeddedSource(bundle as Bundle)
}
```

Create `web/src/data/release.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod'
import { DataError, type DataSource } from './source'
import type {
  EdgesFile, EmergingKeyword, GroupInfo, KeywordsFile, Latest, Manifest, Overview, SeriesFile, TaxonomyFile, Topic, TopicDetail, TopicsFile,
} from './types'

export const SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_MAJOR = 2
/** Embedding providers that never produce a real release (fixtures, the CPU fallback). */
const SAMPLE_PROVIDERS = new Set(['fixture', 'lite'])

export interface AtlasRelease {
  source: DataSource['kind']
  latest: Latest
  manifest: Manifest
  overview: Overview
  taxonomy: TaxonomyFile
  topics: Topic[]
  series: SeriesFile
  edges: EdgesFile['edges']
  keywords: EmergingKeyword[] | null
  previousOverview: Overview | null
  topicById: ReadonlyMap<string, Topic>
  groupById: ReadonlyMap<string, GroupInfo>
  ciLevel: number
  definitionVersion: number
  sample: boolean
}

export function schemaMajor(version: string | null | undefined): number | null {
  const m = /^(\d+)\./.exec(version ?? '')
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : null
}

const ManifestShape = z.looseObject({
  release: z.string(),
  schema_version: z.string().optional(),
  modules: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
  previous_release: z.string().nullable().optional(),
})

async function required<T>(source: DataSource, release: string, path: string): Promise<T> {
  const value = await source.file<T>(release, path)
  if (value === null) throw new DataError('missing', `${release}/${path}`)
  return value
}

function expectArray(value: unknown, where: string): void {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new DataError('parse', where)
}

/** latest → manifest (schema 2.x) → core files in parallel → previous overview (for Δ, optional). */
export async function loadRelease(source: DataSource): Promise<AtlasRelease> {
  const latest = await source.latest()
  const release = latest.release
  const manifest = await required<Manifest>(source, release, 'manifest.json')
  if (!ManifestShape.safeParse(manifest).success) throw new DataError('parse', `${release}/manifest.json`)
  const version = manifest.schema_version ?? latest.schema_version ?? `${SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_MAJOR}.0.0`
  if (schemaMajor(version) !== SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_MAJOR) throw new DataError('schema', version)
  const modules = new Set(manifest.modules ?? [])
  const [overview, taxonomy, topicsFile, series, edgesFile, keywordsFile] = await Promise.all([
    required<Overview>(source, release, 'overview.json'),
    required<TaxonomyFile>(source, release, 'taxonomy.json'),
    required<TopicsFile>(source, release, 'topics.json'),
    required<SeriesFile>(source, release, 'series.json'),
    source.file<EdgesFile>(release, 'edges.json'),
    modules.has('keywords') ? source.file<KeywordsFile>(release, 'keywords.json') : Promise.resolve(null),
  ])
  expectArray(topicsFile.topics, `${release}/topics.json`)
  expectArray(taxonomy.groups, `${release}/taxonomy.json`)
  expectArray(series.years, `${release}/series.json`)
  expectArray(overview.corpus_by_year, `${release}/overview.json`)
  const previous = manifest.previous_release ?? overview.previous_release ?? null
  const previousOverview = previous ? await source.file<Overview>(previous, 'overview.json').catch(() => null) : null
  const topics = topicsFile.topics
  return {
    source: source.kind,
    latest,
    manifest,
    overview,
    taxonomy,
    topics,
    series,
    edges: edgesFile?.edges ?? [],
    keywords: keywordsFile?.keywords ?? null,
    previousOverview,
    topicById: new Map(topics.map((t) => [t.id, t])),
    groupById: new Map(taxonomy.groups.map((g) => [g.id, g])),
    ciLevel: manifest.ci_level ?? 0.9,
    definitionVersion: manifest.definition_version ?? overview.definition_version ?? 0,
    sample: SAMPLE_PROVIDERS.has(manifest.embedding_provider ?? ''),
  }
}

/** topics/<id>.json on demand, one request per topic; a failed request may be retried later. */
export function createDetailLoader(source: DataSource, release: string): (id: string) => Promise<TopicDetail | null> {
  const cache = new Map<string, Promise<TopicDetail | null>>()
  return (id) => {
    let pending = cache.get(id)
    if (!pending) {
      pending = source.file<TopicDetail>(release, `topics/${id}.json`)
      pending.catch(() => cache.delete(id))
      cache.set(id, pending)
    }
    return pending
  }
}

export function seriesOf(release: AtlasRelease, id: string): number[] | null {
  return release.series.topics[id] ?? null
}

export function groupName(release: AtlasRelease, groupId: string, lang: 'th' | 'en'): string {
  const g = release.groupById.get(groupId)
  if (!g) return groupId
  return lang === 'en' ? g.en || g.th : g.th || g.en
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: React context, provider, banners**

Create `web/src/data/atlasContext.ts`:

```ts
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { AtlasRelease } from './release'
import type { DataError } from './source'
import type { TopicDetail } from './types'

export type AtlasState =
  | { status: 'loading' }
  | { status: 'ready'; release: AtlasRelease; detail: (id: string) => Promise<TopicDetail | null> }
  | { status: 'error'; error: DataError }

export const AtlasContext = createContext<AtlasState>({ status: 'loading' })

export function useAtlas(): AtlasState {
  return useContext(AtlasContext)
}

export function useRelease(): AtlasRelease | null {
  const state = useAtlas()
  return state.status === 'ready' ? state.release : null
}

export type DetailState = { status: 'loading' | 'missing' | 'error'; detail: null } | { status: 'ready'; detail: TopicDetail }

/** topics/<id>.json for one topic; state is keyed by id so a new id never shows the previous topic's detail. */
export function useTopicDetail(id: string | undefined): DetailState {
  const state = useAtlas()
  const loader = state.status === 'ready' ? state.detail : null
  const [entry, setEntry] = useState<{ id: string; result: DetailState } | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id || !loader) return
    let alive = true
    loader(id).then(
      (detail) => {
        if (alive) setEntry({ id, result: detail ? { status: 'ready', detail } : { status: 'missing', detail: null } })
      },
      () => {
        if (alive) setEntry({ id, result: { status: 'error', detail: null } })
      },
    )
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [id, loader])
  if (!id) return { status: 'missing', detail: null }
  return entry && entry.id === id ? entry.result : { status: 'loading', detail: null }
}
```

Create `web/src/data/AtlasProvider.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { AtlasContext, type AtlasState } from './atlasContext'
import { createDetailLoader, loadRelease } from './release'
import { toDataError, type DataSource } from './source'

export function AtlasProvider({ source, children }: { source: DataSource; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<AtlasState>({ status: 'loading' })
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    loadRelease(source).then(
      (release) => {
        if (alive) setState({ status: 'ready', release, detail: createDetailLoader(source, release.latest.release) })
      },
      (e: unknown) => {
        if (alive) setState({ status: 'error', error: toDataError(e) })
      },
    )
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [source])
  return <AtlasContext.Provider value={state}>{children}</AtlasContext.Provider>
}
```

Create `web/src/components/DataBanner.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useAtlas } from '../data/atlasContext'
import type { DataErrorKind } from '../data/source'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import type { StrKey } from '../strings'

const TITLE: Record<DataErrorKind, StrKey> = { missing: 'error.missing.title', network: 'error.network.title', parse: 'error.parse.title', schema: 'error.schema.title' }
const BODY: Record<Exclude<DataErrorKind, 'schema'>, StrKey> = { missing: 'error.missing.body', network: 'error.network.body', parse: 'error.parse.body' }

/** "No white page": every data failure becomes a banner that says why and what to do. */
export function DataBanner() {
  const state = useAtlas()
  const { t } = useI18n()
  if (state.status !== 'error') return null
  const { kind, detail } = state.error
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <div className="banner" role="alert">
        <h2>{t(TITLE[kind])}</h2>
        <p>{kind === 'schema' ? t('error.schema.body', { found: detail }) : t(BODY[kind])}</p>
        {kind === 'schema' ? null : <p className="detail">{t('error.detail', { detail })}</p>}
        <p className="mt-2">
          <button type="button" className="linkbtn" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
            {t('reload')}
          </button>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `web/src/components/RequireRelease.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useAtlas } from '../data/atlasContext'
import type { AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { PageHeader } from './PageHeader'

/**
 * Renders `children(release)` once data is ready; otherwise the page h1 plus "loading" or a pointer to the banner.
 * Put hooks in a component rendered by the children function, never in the function itself.
 */
export function RequireRelease({ title, children }: { title: string; children: (release: AtlasRelease) => ReactNode }) {
  const state = useAtlas()
  const { t } = useI18n()
  if (state.status === 'ready') return <>{children(state.release)}</>
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={title} />
      {state.status === 'loading' ? (
        <p className="loading" role="status">{t('loading')}</p>
      ) : (
        <p className="loading">{t('noData.body')}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Replace `web/vite.config.ts`:

```ts
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { createReadStream, existsSync, statSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve, sep } from 'node:path'
import { defineConfig, type Plugin } from 'vite'

/** `npm run dev` serves /data/* from fixtures (or ATLAS_DATA_DIR) — the built site never contains data. */
function atlasDevData(dir: string): Plugin {
  const root = resolve(dir)
  return {
    name: 'atlas-dev-data',
    apply: 'serve',
    configureServer(server) {
      server.middlewares.use('/data', (req, res) => {
        const rel = decodeURIComponent((req.url ?? '/').split('?')[0])
        const file = resolve(root, `.${rel}`)
        if (!file.startsWith(root + sep) || !existsSync(file) || !statSync(file).isFile()) {
          res.statusCode = 404
          res.end('not found')
          return
        }
        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8')
        createReadStream(file).pipe(res)
      })
    },
  }
}

// base './' — the site works from any sub-path (GitHub Pages) and data is fetched relative to it.
export default defineConfig({
  base: './',
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss(), atlasDevData(process.env.ATLAS_DATA_DIR ?? 'fixtures/example/data')],
  server: { port: 3000 },
})
```

Create `web/scripts/serve.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Tiny static server for the built site (Playwright smoke tests, the PDF brief).
//   node scripts/serve.mjs --site dist [--data fixtures/example/data] [--port 4173]
// With --data, /data/* comes from that folder instead of <site>/data, so tests never copy data into dist/.
import { readFile, stat } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { createServer } from 'node:http'
import { extname, resolve, sep } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

const TYPES = {
  '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8',
  '.json': 'application/json; charset=utf-8', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.woff2': 'font/woff2', '.png': 'image/png',
  '.pdf': 'application/pdf', '.txt': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8',
}

function inside(root, rel) {
  const base = resolve(root)
  const file = resolve(base, `.${rel}`)
  return file === base || file.startsWith(base + sep) ? file : null
}

export function startServer({ site, data = null, port = 4173 }) {
  const server = createServer(async (req, res) => {
    try {
      let rel = decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url ?? '/', 'http://localhost').pathname)
      let root = site
      if (data && rel.startsWith('/data/')) {
        root = data
        rel = rel.slice('/data'.length)
      }
      if (rel.endsWith('/')) rel += 'index.html'
      const file = inside(root, rel)
      const ok = file ? await stat(file).then((s) => s.isFile(), () => false) : false
      if (!ok) {
        res.writeHead(404, { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' })
        res.end('not found')
        return
      }
      res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': TYPES[extname(file)] ?? 'application/octet-stream', 'cache-control': 'no-cache' })
      res.end(await readFile(file))
    } catch (e) {
      res.writeHead(500, { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' })
      res.end(String(e))
    }
  })
  return new Promise((done) => server.listen(port, '127.0.0.1', () => done(server)))
}

if (process.argv[1] && resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  const args = {}
  for (let i = 2; i < process.argv.length; i += 2) args[process.argv[i].replace(/^--/, '')] = process.argv[i + 1]
  const server = await startServer({ site: args.site ?? 'dist', data: args.data ?? null, port: Number(args.port ?? 4173) })
  console.log(`[serve] http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/  site=${args.site ?? 'dist'}${args.data ? ` data=${args.data}` : ''}`)
}
```

Create `web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`:

```ts
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright'
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test'

const EXAMPLE = 'http://127.0.0.1:4173/'
const EMPTY = 'http://127.0.0.1:4174/'

const ROUTES = [
  { name: 'overview', path: '/overview' },
  { name: 'atlas', path: '/atlas' },
  { name: 'topic', path: '/topics/T000201' },
  { name: 'emerging', path: '/emerging' },
  { name: 'compare', path: '/compare?ids=T000201,T000701' },
  { name: 'data', path: '/data' },
  { name: 'about', path: '/about' },
  { name: 'brief', path: '/brief' },
] as const

type RouteName = (typeof ROUTES)[number]['name']
type Check = (page: Page) => Promise<void>

/** Page-specific checks against the example release; each page task adds one line between the markers. */
const CHECKS: Partial<Record<RouteName, Check>> = {
  // checks:start
  // checks:end
}

/** Routes that must not scroll sideways at 360 px. */
const PHONE = ['/overview', '/emerging', '/topics/T000201', '/atlas', '/compare?ids=T000201,T000701', '/data', '/about']

function collectErrors(page: Page): string[] {
  const errors: string[] = []
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(`pageerror: ${e.message}`))
  page.on('console', (m) => {
    if (m.type() === 'error') errors.push(`console: ${m.text()}`)
  })
  return errors
}

async function seriousA11yViolations(page: Page): Promise<string[]> {
  const result = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21a', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa']).analyze()
  return result.violations
    .filter((v) => v.impact === 'serious' || v.impact === 'critical')
    .map((v) => `${v.id}: ${v.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(' ')).join(' | ')}`)
}

for (const route of ROUTES) {
  test(`${route.name}: example release renders in light and dark without serious a11y issues`, async ({ page }) => {
    const errors = collectErrors(page)
    await page.goto(`${EXAMPLE}#${route.path}`)
    await expect(page.locator('h1').first()).toBeVisible()
    await CHECKS[route.name]?.(page)
    expect(await seriousA11yViolations(page)).toEqual([])
    await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: 'dark' })
    expect(await seriousA11yViolations(page)).toEqual([])
    expect(errors).toEqual([])
  })

  test(`${route.name}: an empty release renders without errors`, async ({ page }) => {
    const errors = collectErrors(page)
    await page.goto(`${EMPTY}#${route.path}`)
    await expect(page.locator('h1').first()).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toHaveCount(0)
    expect(errors).toEqual([])
  })
}

test('a missing data release shows a banner, not a white page', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.route('**/data/latest.json', (r) => r.fulfill({ status: 404, body: 'not found' }))
  await page.goto(`${EXAMPLE}#/overview`)
  await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('latest.json')
  await expect(page.locator('h1').first()).toBeVisible()
})

test('a sample release is labelled as sample data', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto(`${EXAMPLE}#/overview`)
  await expect(page.locator('.phase-tag.is-sample')).toBeVisible()
})

test('pages do not scroll sideways on a 360 px phone', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 360, height: 780 })
  for (const path of PHONE) {
    await page.goto(`${EXAMPLE}#${path}`)
    await expect(page.locator('h1').first()).toBeVisible()
    const overflow = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth)
    expect(overflow, path).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0)
  }
})
```

Run: `npx playwright install chromium` (once per machine), then `npm run build && npm run e2e`
Expected: PASS — 16 route tests + 3 others (placeholder pages render their `h1`; the example release shows the sample tag; a 404 on `latest.json` shows the "ยังไม่มีชุดข้อมูลบนเว็บไซต์นี้" banner).

### Task 4: Pure helpers — formatting, headline highlighting, URL state, CSV, Thai search, definition, sorting

Everything a page needs that is not React lives here, is pure and is unit-tested: numbers in both languages (fractions in, percent out, true minus), the Editorial headline's highlighted figures and linked topic names, URL-state codecs (filters and selection in the hash, round-trippable, defaults omitted), CSV with a BOM for Excel and a formula-injection guard, Thai/English search with tone-mark folding and `Intl.Segmenter('th')`, and the definition constants and helpers.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/format.ts`, `web/src/lib/highlight.ts`, `web/src/lib/urlState.ts`, `web/src/lib/useUrlState.ts`, `web/src/lib/csv.ts`, `web/src/lib/search.ts`, `web/src/lib/definition.ts`, `web/src/lib/sort.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/format.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/urlState.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/csv.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/search.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/definition.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Lang` (`lib/i18n`), `STATUSES`/`StatusId` (`lib/status`), `Topic` (`data/types`).
- Produces:
  - `format.ts`: `MINUS`, `DASH`, `fmtInt(n)`, `fmtNum(x, digits)`, `fmtPct(fraction, digits = 1)`, `fmtSignedPct(fraction, digits = 1)`, `fmtSignedInt(n)`, `fmtPp(points, lang)`, `fmtRatio(x)`, `fmtCompact(n)`, `fmtDate(iso, lang)`, `ciPct(level)`, `kpiDelta(value, prev, unit: 'count' | 'pct', lang): string | null`.
  - `highlight.ts`: `type Segment = {kind:'text', text} | {kind:'figure', text} | {kind:'topic', text, id}`, `segmentHeadline(text, topics, { figures?, minNameLength? })` (lossless: joined segment texts equal the input).
  - `urlState.ts`: `interface Field<V>`, `interface Codec<T> { defaults; parse(params); write(state) }`, `makeCodec`, `oneOf`, `setOf`, `topicList`, `optionalTopic`, `text`; `VIEWS`/`type View`; `EMERGING_SORTS`/`type EmergingSort`; `LAYERS`/`type Layer`; codecs `emergingCodec` `{status, sort, dir, view}`, `atlasCodec` `{sel, hide, layers, q}`, `compareCodec` `{ids, metric, view}`, `overviewCodec` `{st, pf}`, `topicCodec` `{trend}`; types `EmergingUrlState`, `AtlasUrlState`, `CompareUrlState`.
  - `useUrlState.ts`: `useUrlState(codec): [state, update(patch)]` (replace-navigation, keeps `lang`).
  - `csv.ts`: `interface CsvColumn<R> {header, value(row)}`, `csvCell(v)`, `toCsv(rows, columns)`, `downloadText(filename, text, mime?)`.
  - `search.ts`: `foldText(s)`, `segmentWords(s)`, `interface SearchDoc {id, th, en, keywords, size}`, `buildSearchIndex(docs)`, `searchTopics(index, query, limit = 10): {id, score}[]`.
  - `definition.ts`: `DEFINITION` constants, `windows(lastCompleteYear): {R, B, W}`, `fmtWindow([a, b])`, `quantile(sorted, q)`, `largeShareThreshold(topics)`, `type Quadrant`, `QUADRANTS`, `quadrantOf(topic, threshold)`.
  - `sort.ts`: `type SortDir`, `sortBy(rows, value, dir)` (missing values always last; Thai collation for text).

Create `web/src/lib/format.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { DASH, fmtCompact, fmtDate, fmtInt, fmtPct, fmtPp, fmtRatio, fmtSignedInt, fmtSignedPct, kpiDelta } from './format'
import { segmentHeadline, type Segment } from './highlight'

describe('number formatting', () => {
  it('groups integers the same way in both languages and uses the true minus', () => {
    expect(fmtInt(248310)).toBe('248,310')
    expect(fmtInt(-5)).toBe('\u22125')
    expect(fmtInt(null)).toBe(DASH)
    expect(fmtInt(Number.NaN)).toBe(DASH)
  })

  it('turns fractions into percentages', () => {
    expect(fmtPct(0.0123, 2)).toBe('1.23%')
    expect(fmtPct(0.742)).toBe('74.2%')
    expect(fmtPct(undefined)).toBe(DASH)
  })

  it('signs growth and never prints −0', () => {
    expect(fmtSignedPct(0.152)).toBe('+15.2%')
    expect(fmtSignedPct(-0.05)).toBe('\u22125.0%')
    expect(fmtSignedPct(0.00001)).toBe('±0.0%')
    expect(fmtSignedPct(1.4237, 0)).toBe('+142%')
    expect(fmtSignedInt(6140)).toBe('+6,140')
    expect(fmtSignedInt(0)).toBe('±0')
  })

  it('formats percentage points, ratios, compact ticks and dates', () => {
    expect(fmtPp(74.2 - 73.6, 'th')).toBe('+0.6 จุด')
    expect(fmtPp(-0.24, 'en')).toBe('\u22120.2 pp')
    expect(fmtRatio(1.84)).toBe('1.8×')
    expect(fmtCompact(12500)).toBe('13K')
    expect(fmtCompact(1500)).toBe('1.5K')
    expect(fmtCompact(950)).toBe('950')
    expect(fmtDate('2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', 'en')).toBe('1 October 2026')
    expect(fmtDate('2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00', 'th')).toContain('2026')
    expect(fmtDate('not a date', 'th')).toBe('not a date')
  })

  it('describes KPI changes against the previous release', () => {
    expect(kpiDelta(248310, 242170, 'count', 'th')).toBe('+6,140 (+2.5%)')
    expect(kpiDelta(74.2, 73.6, 'pct', 'en')).toBe('+0.6 pp')
    expect(kpiDelta(10, null, 'count', 'th')).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('segmentHeadline', () => {
  const topics = [{ id: 'T000201', th: 'AI เพื่อการศึกษา', en: 'AI in education' }]
  const kinds = (segs: Segment[], kind: Segment['kind']) => segs.filter((s) => s.kind === kind).map((s) => s.text)

  it('marks figures and links topic names, but not years or numbers inside words', () => {
    const text = 'รอบ 2026Q4 มีหัวข้อกำลังเกิดใหม่ 5 หัวข้อ และAI เพื่อการศึกษาโตเร็วที่สุด +38.0% ต่อปี'
    const segs = segmentHeadline(text, topics)
    expect(segs.map((s) => s.text).join('')).toBe(text)
    expect(kinds(segs, 'figure')).toEqual(['5', '+38.0%'])
    expect(segs.find((s) => s.kind === 'topic')).toMatchObject({ id: 'T000201', text: 'AI เพื่อการศึกษา' })
  })

  it('matches English names case-insensitively and skips COVID-19 style numbers', () => {
    const segs = segmentHeadline('ai in education grew 12% while COVID-19 papers fell \u22123.1% since 2020', topics)
    expect(kinds(segs, 'topic')).toEqual(['ai in education'])
    expect(kinds(segs, 'figure')).toEqual(['12%', '\u22123.1%'])
  })

  it('can link names without marking figures, and ignores very short names', () => {
    const segs = segmentHeadline('AI เพื่อการศึกษา 5 หัวข้อ', [...topics, { id: 'T000999', th: 'AI', en: 'AI' }], { figures: false })
    expect(kinds(segs, 'figure')).toEqual([])
    expect(kinds(segs, 'topic')).toEqual(['AI เพื่อการศึกษา'])
  })
})
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { atlasCodec, compareCodec, emergingCodec, overviewCodec, topicCodec, type AtlasUrlState, type EmergingUrlState } from './urlState'

describe('URL state codecs', () => {
  it('emerging: round-trips and writes nothing for the defaults', () => {
    const s: EmergingUrlState = { status: 'WATCH', sort: 'journals', dir: 'asc', view: 'chart' }
    expect(emergingCodec.parse(emergingCodec.write(s))).toEqual(s)
    expect(emergingCodec.write(emergingCodec.defaults).toString()).toBe('')
  })

  it('invalid or tampered values fall back to the defaults', () => {
    expect(emergingCodec.parse(new URLSearchParams('status=BOGUS&sort=x&dir=up&view=3d'))).toEqual(emergingCodec.defaults)
  })

  it('atlas: selection, hidden statuses in canonical order, an empty layer set, Thai search text', () => {
    const s: AtlasUrlState = { sel: 'T000201', hide: ['LOW_DATA', 'STABLE'], layers: [], q: 'ผู้สูงอายุ & AI' }
    expect(atlasCodec.parse(new URLSearchParams(atlasCodec.write(s).toString()))).toEqual({ ...s, hide: ['STABLE', 'LOW_DATA'] })
    expect(atlasCodec.parse(new URLSearchParams('sel=T12&hide=HOT,NOPE'))).toMatchObject({ sel: null, hide: ['HOT'], layers: ['contours', 'tints', 'names'] })
  })

  it('compare: keeps the order, drops duplicates and bad ids, caps at four', () => {
    const ids = compareCodec.parse(new URLSearchParams('ids=T000701,T000201,T000701,X,T000101,T000102,T000103')).ids
    expect(ids).toEqual(['T000701', 'T000201', 'T000101', 'T000102'])
    const s = { ids: ['T000201', 'T000701'], metric: 'count', view: 'table' } as const
    expect(compareCodec.parse(compareCodec.write({ ...s, ids: [...s.ids] }))).toEqual(s)
  })

  it('overview and topic views', () => {
    expect(overviewCodec.parse(overviewCodec.write({ st: 'table', pf: 'chart' }))).toEqual({ st: 'table', pf: 'chart' })
    expect(topicCodec.write({ trend: 'chart' }).toString()).toBe('')
  })
})
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { csvCell, toCsv } from './csv'

describe('CSV', () => {
  it('starts with a BOM, uses CRLF and keeps raw numbers', () => {
    const csv = toCsv([{ id: 'T000201', th: 'AI เพื่อการศึกษา', g: 0.1234 }], [
      { header: 'id', value: (r) => r.id },
      { header: 'name_th', value: (r) => r.th },
      { header: 'share_growth', value: (r) => r.g },
    ])
    expect(csv).toBe('\uFEFFid,name_th,share_growth\r\nT000201,AI เพื่อการศึกษา,0.1234\r\n')
  })

  it('quotes separators, quotes and newlines; leaves missing values empty', () => {
    expect(csvCell('a,b')).toBe('"a,b"')
    expect(csvCell('say "hi"')).toBe('"say ""hi"""')
    expect(csvCell('two\nlines')).toBe('"two\nlines"')

    expect(csvCell(Number.NaN)).toBe('')
    expect(csvCell(true)).toBe('true')
  })

  it('neutralises spreadsheet formulas in text cells', () => {
    expect(csvCell('=SUM(A1)')).toBe("'=SUM(A1)")
    expect(csvCell('@cmd')).toBe("'@cmd")
    expect(csvCell(-3)).toBe('-3')
```

```ts
import type { Lang } from './i18n'

/** Both languages use Arabic digits with en-US grouping, the true minus sign and Gregorian years (DESIGN.md). */
export const MINUS = '\u2212'
export const DASH = '–'

const formatters = new Map<number, Intl.NumberFormat>()
function nf(digits: number): Intl.NumberFormat {
  let f = formatters.get(digits)
  if (!f) {
    f = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: digits, maximumFractionDigits: digits })
    formatters.set(digits, f)
  }
  return f
}

const finite = (x: number | null | undefined): x is number => typeof x === 'number' && Number.isFinite(x)

export function fmtNum(x: number | null | undefined, digits: number): string {
  return finite(x) ? nf(digits).format(x).replace('-', MINUS) : DASH
}

export function fmtInt(n: number | null | undefined): string {
  return finite(n) ? fmtNum(Math.round(n), 0) : DASH
}

/** 0.0123 → "1.23%" (the contract stores shares and growth as fractions). */
export function fmtPct(fraction: number | null | undefined, digits = 1): string {
  return finite(fraction) ? `${fmtNum(fraction * 100, digits)}%` : DASH
}

/** +0.152 → "+15.2%", −0.05 → "−5.0%", values that round to zero → "±0.0%". */
export function fmtSignedPct(fraction: number | null | undefined, digits = 1): string {
  if (!finite(fraction)) return DASH
  const v = Number((fraction * 100).toFixed(digits))
  if (v === 0) return `±${nf(digits).format(0)}%`
  return `${v > 0 ? '+' : MINUS}${nf(digits).format(Math.abs(v))}%`
}

export function fmtSignedInt(n: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (!finite(n)) return DASH
  const v = Math.round(n)
  if (v === 0) return '±0'
  return `${v > 0 ? '+' : MINUS}${nf(0).format(Math.abs(v))}`
}

/** A change between two percentages, in percentage points: "+0.6 จุด" / "+0.6 pp". */
export function fmtPp(points: number, lang: Lang): string {
  const v = Number(points.toFixed(1))
  const unit = lang === 'en' ? 'pp' : 'จุด'
  if (v === 0) return `±0.0 ${unit}`
  return `${v > 0 ? '+' : MINUS}${nf(1).format(Math.abs(v))} ${unit}`
}

export function fmtRatio(x: number | null | undefined): string {
  return finite(x) ? `${fmtNum(x, 1)}×` : DASH
}

/** Axis ticks: 950, 1.5K, 13K, 2.4M. */
export function fmtCompact(n: number): string {
  const a = Math.abs(n)
  const sign = n < 0 ? MINUS : ''
  if (a >= 1e6) return `${sign}${(a / 1e6).toFixed(a >= 1e7 ? 0 : 1).replace(/\.0$/, '')}M`
  if (a >= 1e4) return `${sign}${Math.round(a / 1e3)}K`
  if (a >= 1e3) return `${sign}${(a / 1e3).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '')}K`
  return `${sign}${Math.round(a)}`
}

export function fmtDate(iso: string, lang: Lang): string {
  const d = new Date(iso)
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return iso
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(lang === 'th' ? 'th-TH-u-ca-gregory' : 'en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' }).format(d)
}

/** manifest.ci_level 0.9 → 90 */
export function ciPct(level: number): number {
  return Math.round(level * 100)
}

/** "+6,140 (+2.5%)" for counts, "+0.6 จุด" for percentages; null when there is nothing to compare with. */
export function kpiDelta(value: number, prev: number | null | undefined, unit: 'count' | 'pct', lang: Lang): string | null {
  if (!finite(prev) || !finite(value)) return null
  if (unit === 'pct') return fmtPp(value - prev, lang)
  const d = value - prev
  return prev > 0 ? `${fmtSignedInt(d)} (${fmtSignedPct(d / prev, 1)})` : fmtSignedInt(d)
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/highlight.ts`:

```ts
/** Split a sentence into plain text, figures to highlight and topic names to link (Editorial headline). */
export type Segment = { kind: 'text'; text: string } | { kind: 'figure'; text: string } | { kind: 'topic'; text: string; id: string }

interface Named {
  id: string
  th: string
  en: string
}

// A number (Arabic or Thai digits) with an optional sign and unit, not glued to a Latin letter, digit or hyphen
// (so "COVID-19" and "2026Q4" stay plain text).
const FIGURE = /(?<![A-Za-z0-9.,\-\u2212])[+\u2212-]?(?:\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?|[\u0E50-\u0E59][\u0E50-\u0E59,]*(?:\.[\u0E50-\u0E59]+)?)(?![A-Za-z0-9])(?:\s?(?:%|×|เท่า))?/g
const YEAR = /^(19|20)\d{2}$/

export function segmentHeadline(text: string, topics: readonly Named[], opts: { figures?: boolean; minNameLength?: number } = {}): Segment[] {
  const figures = opts.figures ?? true
  const minLength = opts.minNameLength ?? 4
  const names = topics
    .flatMap((t) => [t.th, t.en].filter((n) => n && n.length >= minLength).map((name) => ({ id: t.id, name: name.toLowerCase() })))
    .sort((a, b) => b.name.length - a.name.length)
  const lower = text.toLowerCase()
  const taken: { start: number; end: number; id: string }[] = []
  for (const { id, name } of names) {
    for (let from = 0, i = lower.indexOf(name); i >= 0; from = i + name.length, i = lower.indexOf(name, from)) {
      const end = i + name.length
      if (!taken.some((r) => i < r.end && end > r.start)) taken.push({ start: i, end, id })
    }
  }
  taken.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)

  const out: Segment[] = []
  const pushText = (s: string) => {
    if (!s) return
    if (!figures) {
      out.push({ kind: 'text', text: s })
      return
    }
    let last = 0
    for (const m of s.matchAll(FIGURE)) {
      const value = m[0]
      const start = m.index ?? 0
      if (YEAR.test(value.replace(/^[+\u2212-]/, ''))) continue
      if (start > last) out.push({ kind: 'text', text: s.slice(last, start) })
      out.push({ kind: 'figure', text: value })
      last = start + value.length
    }
    if (last < s.length) out.push({ kind: 'text', text: s.slice(last) })
  }
  let pos = 0
  for (const r of taken) {
    pushText(text.slice(pos, r.start))
    out.push({ kind: 'topic', text: text.slice(r.start, r.end), id: r.id })
    pos = r.end
  }
  pushText(text.slice(pos))
  return out
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/urlState.ts`:

```ts
import { STATUSES, type StatusId } from './status'

/** One URL query key with a default (defaults are never written, so shared links stay short). */
export interface Field<V> {
  key: string
  def: V
  parse(raw: string | null): V
  write(value: V): string | null
}

export interface Codec<T> {
  defaults: T
  parse(params: URLSearchParams): T
  write(state: T): URLSearchParams
}

export function makeCodec<T extends object>(fields: { [K in keyof T]: Field<T[K]> }): Codec<T> {
  const entries = Object.entries(fields) as [keyof T & string, Field<unknown>][]
  const defaults = Object.fromEntries(entries.map(([k, f]) => [k, f.def])) as T
  return {
    defaults,
    parse(params) {
      return Object.fromEntries(entries.map(([k, f]) => [k, f.parse(params.get(f.key))])) as T
    },
    write(state) {
      const out = new URLSearchParams()
      for (const [k, f] of entries) {
        const v = f.write(state[k])
        if (v !== null) out.set(f.key, v)
      }
      return out
    },
  }
}

export function oneOf<V extends string>(key: string, values: readonly V[], def: V): Field<V> {
  return {
    key,
    def,
    parse: (raw) => (raw !== null && (values as readonly string[]).includes(raw) ? (raw as V) : def),
    write: (v) => (v === def ? null : v),
  }
}

/** A set written in canonical order; "" in the URL means "none" (different from the default). */
export function setOf<V extends string>(key: string, values: readonly V[], def: readonly V[]): Field<V[]> {
  const norm = (xs: readonly string[]): V[] => values.filter((v) => xs.includes(v))
  const defN = norm(def)
  const same = (a: readonly V[]) => a.length === defN.length && a.every((v, i) => v === defN[i])
  return {
    key,
    def: defN,
    parse: (raw) => (raw === null ? defN : raw === '' ? [] : norm(raw.split(','))),
    write: (v) => {
      const n = norm(v)
      return same(n) ? null : n.join(',')
    },
  }
}

const TOPIC_ID = /^T\d{6}$/

/** Ordered topic ids, deduplicated, invalid ids dropped, at most `max`. */
export function topicList(key: string, max: number): Field<string[]> {
  return {
    key,
    def: [],
    parse: (raw) => [...new Set((raw ?? '').split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter((s) => TOPIC_ID.test(s)))].slice(0, max),
    write: (v) => (v.length ? v.join(',') : null),
  }
}

export function optionalTopic(key: string): Field<string | null> {
  return { key, def: null, parse: (raw) => (raw && TOPIC_ID.test(raw) ? raw : null), write: (v) => v }
}

export function text(key: string, maxLength: number): Field<string> {
  return { key, def: '', parse: (raw) => (raw ?? '').slice(0, maxLength), write: (v) => (v ? v.slice(0, maxLength) : null) }
}

export const VIEWS = ['chart', 'table'] as const
export type View = (typeof VIEWS)[number]
export const EMERGING_SORTS = ['growth', 'lo', 'share', 'ratio', 'journals', 'orgs', 'score', 'articles', 'name'] as const
export type EmergingSort = (typeof EMERGING_SORTS)[number]
export const LAYERS = ['contours', 'tints', 'names'] as const
export type Layer = (typeof LAYERS)[number]

export interface EmergingUrlState {
  status: StatusId
  sort: EmergingSort
  dir: 'asc' | 'desc'
  view: View
}
export const emergingCodec = makeCodec<EmergingUrlState>({
  status: oneOf('status', STATUSES, 'EMERGING'),
  sort: oneOf('sort', EMERGING_SORTS, 'growth'),
  dir: oneOf('dir', ['asc', 'desc'] as const, 'desc'),
  view: oneOf('view', VIEWS, 'table'),
})

export interface AtlasUrlState {
  sel: string | null
  hide: StatusId[]
  layers: Layer[]
  q: string
}
export const atlasCodec = makeCodec<AtlasUrlState>({
  sel: optionalTopic('sel'),
  hide: setOf('hide', STATUSES, []),
  layers: setOf('layers', LAYERS, LAYERS),
  q: text('q', 80),
})

export interface CompareUrlState {
  ids: string[]
  metric: 'share' | 'count'
  view: View
}
export const compareCodec = makeCodec<CompareUrlState>({
  ids: topicList('ids', 4),
  metric: oneOf('metric', ['share', 'count'] as const, 'share'),
  view: oneOf('view', VIEWS, 'chart'),
})

export const overviewCodec = makeCodec<{ st: View; pf: View }>({ st: oneOf('st', VIEWS, 'chart'), pf: oneOf('pf', VIEWS, 'chart') })
export const topicCodec = makeCodec<{ trend: View }>({ trend: oneOf('trend', VIEWS, 'chart') })
```

Create `web/src/lib/useUrlState.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router'
import type { Codec } from './urlState'

/** Page state in the hash query (#/emerging?status=WATCH): shareable, survives reloads, defaults not written. */
export function useUrlState<T>(codec: Codec<T>): [T, (patch: Partial<T>) => void] {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams()
  const state = useMemo(() => codec.parse(params), [codec, params])
  const update = useCallback(
    (patch: Partial<T>) => {
      setParams(
        (prev) => {
          const next = codec.write({ ...codec.parse(prev), ...patch })
          const lang = prev.get('lang')
          if (lang) next.set('lang', lang)
          return next
        },
        { replace: true },
      )
    },
    [codec, setParams],
  )
  return [state, update]
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/csv.ts`:

```ts
export interface CsvColumn<R> {
  header: string
  value: (row: R) => string | number | boolean | null | undefined
}

/** One CSV cell: raw numbers, quoted text when needed, and text starting with = + - @ prefixed with ' (formula injection). */
export function csvCell(v: string | number | boolean | null | undefined): string {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return ''
  if (typeof v === 'number') return Number.isFinite(v) ? String(v) : ''
  if (typeof v === 'boolean') return String(v)
  const safe = /^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(v) ? `'${v}` : v
  return /[",\r\n]/.test(safe) ? `"${safe.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : safe
}

/** UTF-8 BOM (so Excel shows Thai), header row, CRLF line ends. */
export function toCsv<R>(rows: readonly R[], columns: readonly CsvColumn<R>[]): string {
  const lines = [columns.map((c) => csvCell(c.header)).join(','), ...rows.map((r) => columns.map((c) => csvCell(c.value(r))).join(','))]
  return `\uFEFF${lines.join('\r\n')}\r\n`
}

/** Save text as a file — works on Pages and in the offline file:// edition (no server involved). */
export function downloadText(filename: string, text: string, mime = 'text/csv;charset=utf-8'): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: mime }))
  const a = document.createElement('a')
  a.href = url
  a.download = filename
  document.body.append(a)
  a.click()
  a.remove()
  window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000)
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/search.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Topic search in Thai and English, entirely in the browser.
 * Text is folded before matching: Thai tone marks and other above-marks (U+0E47–U+0E4C) are removed, the decomposed
 * sara am (U+0E4D U+0E32) is joined, zero-width characters vanish, Latin is lower-cased without accents — so text damaged
 * by PDF conversion ("ความตองการ") still finds "ความต้องการ". Thai words come from Intl.Segmenter('th').
 */
const ABOVE_MARKS = /[\u0E47-\u0E4C]/g
const INVISIBLE = /[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u00AD]/g
const DECOMPOSED_AM = /\u0E4D\u0E32/g
const LATIN_ACCENTS = /[\u0300-\u036F]/g

export function foldText(s: string): string {
  return s
    .normalize('NFC')
    .replace(DECOMPOSED_AM, '\u0E33')
    .replace(INVISIBLE, '')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(LATIN_ACCENTS, '')
    .replace(ABOVE_MARKS, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

const segmenter = typeof Intl !== 'undefined' && 'Segmenter' in Intl ? new Intl.Segmenter('th', { granularity: 'word' }) : null

export function segmentWords(s: string): string[] {
  if (!segmenter) return s.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)
  return [...segmenter.segment(s)].filter((x) => x.isWordLike).map((x) => x.segment)
}

export interface SearchDoc {
  id: string
  th: string
  en: string
  keywords: readonly string[]
  size: number
}

interface Indexed {
  id: string
  size: number
  names: string[]
  nameWords: string[]
  keywords: string
}

export interface SearchIndex {
  docs: Indexed[]
}

export function buildSearchIndex(docs: readonly SearchDoc[]): SearchIndex {
  return {
    docs: docs.map((d) => {
      const names = [d.th, d.en].map(foldText).filter(Boolean)
      return { id: d.id, size: d.size, names, nameWords: names.flatMap((n) => segmentWords(n)), keywords: d.keywords.map(foldText).join(' | ') }
    }),
  }
}

export interface SearchHit {
  id: string
  score: number
}

/** Name exact 100 · name prefix 80 · name contains 60 · every word in the name 40 · keyword phrase 30 · every word in keywords 20. */
export function searchTopics(index: SearchIndex, query: string, limit = 10): SearchHit[] {
  const q = foldText(query)
  if (!q) return []
  const tokens = segmentWords(q)
  const hits: (SearchHit & { size: number })[] = []
  for (const d of index.docs) {
    let score = 0
    for (const n of d.names) {
      if (n === q) score = Math.max(score, 100)
      else if (n.startsWith(q)) score = Math.max(score, 80)
      else if (n.includes(q)) score = Math.max(score, 60)
    }
    if (score === 0 && tokens.length > 0 && tokens.every((tk) => d.nameWords.some((w) => w.startsWith(tk)) || d.names.some((n) => n.includes(tk)))) score = 40
    if (score === 0 && d.keywords.includes(q)) score = 30
    if (score === 0 && tokens.length > 0 && tokens.every((tk) => d.keywords.includes(tk))) score = 20
    if (score > 0) hits.push({ id: d.id, score, size: d.size })
  }
  hits.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || b.size - a.size || a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
  return hits.slice(0, limit).map(({ id, score }) => ({ id, score }))
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/definition.ts`:

```ts
import type { Topic } from '../data/types'

/**
 * Emerging-definition constants shown in the UI. They mirror pipeline/config.example.yaml defaults
 * (docs/EMERGING_SPEC.md) because contract v2 publishes only definition_version, ci_level and last_complete_year.
 * When the manifest gains a `definition` block, read it first and keep these as fallbacks.
 */
export const DEFINITION = {
  emergingMinGrowthLo: 0.1,
  emergingMinShareRatio: 1.8,
  decliningMaxShareRatio: 0.8,
  largeQuantile: 0.75,
  minArticlesRecent: 30,
  minJournals: 3,
  minOrgs: 3,
  recentYears: 3,
  baselineYears: 5,
  trendYears: 5,
} as const

export interface Windows {
  R: [number, number]
  B: [number, number]
  W: [number, number]
}

export function windows(lastCompleteYear: number): Windows {
  const L = lastCompleteYear
  const { recentYears, baselineYears, trendYears } = DEFINITION
  return { R: [L - recentYears + 1, L], B: [L - recentYears - baselineYears + 1, L - recentYears], W: [L - trendYears + 1, L] }
}

export const fmtWindow = (w: readonly [number, number]): string => `${w[0]}–${w[1]}`

/** Linear interpolation between closest ranks (pandas' default) on an ascending array. */
export function quantile(sorted: readonly number[], q: number): number {
  if (sorted.length === 0) return Number.NaN
  const pos = (sorted.length - 1) * q
  const lo = Math.floor(pos)
  const hi = Math.ceil(pos)
  return sorted[lo] + (sorted[hi] - sorted[lo]) * (pos - lo)
}

/** "Large" = share_recent at or above the 0.75 quantile of classified (non-LOW_DATA) topics. */
export function largeShareThreshold(topics: readonly Topic[]): number | null {
  const shares = topics.filter((t) => t.status !== 'LOW_DATA').map((t) => t.metrics.share_recent).filter((s) => Number.isFinite(s))
  if (shares.length === 0) return null
  return quantile([...shares].sort((a, b) => a - b), DEFINITION.largeQuantile)
}

export type Quadrant = 'stars' | 'rising' | 'mature' | 'declining'
export const QUADRANTS: readonly Quadrant[] = ['stars', 'rising', 'mature', 'declining']

export function quadrantOf(t: Topic, threshold: number): Quadrant | null {
  const g = t.metrics.share_growth
  if (t.status === 'LOW_DATA' || g === null || g === undefined) return null
  const large = t.metrics.share_recent >= threshold
  if (g > 0) return large ? 'stars' : 'rising'
  return large ? 'mature' : 'declining'
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/sort.ts`:

```ts
export type SortDir = 'asc' | 'desc'

const collator = new Intl.Collator('th')

/** A sorted copy; missing values (null, undefined, NaN) always go last whatever the direction. */
export function sortBy<R>(rows: readonly R[], value: (row: R) => number | string | null | undefined, dir: SortDir): R[] {
  const sign = dir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1
  const missing = (v: number | string | null | undefined) => v === null || v === undefined || (typeof v === 'number' && !Number.isFinite(v))
  return [...rows].sort((a, b) => {
    const va = value(a)
    const vb = value(b)
    const ma = missing(va)
    const mb = missing(vb)
    if (ma || mb) return ma === mb ? 0 : ma ? 1 : -1
    if (typeof va === 'number' && typeof vb === 'number') return (va - vb) * sign
    return collator.compare(String(va), String(vb)) * sign
  })
}
```

Create `web/src/charts/scale.ts`:

```ts
export interface Linear {
  (v: number): number
  invert(px: number): number
}

export function linear(d0: number, d1: number, r0: number, r1: number): Linear {
  const span = d1 - d0 || 1
  const f = ((v: number) => r0 + ((v - d0) / span) * (r1 - r0)) as Linear
  f.invert = (px: number) => d0 + ((px - r0) / (r1 - r0 || 1)) * span
  return f
}

/** Log10 scale; values ≤ 0 are drawn at the lower end. */
export function logScale(d0: number, d1: number, r0: number, r1: number): (v: number) => number {
  const l0 = Math.log10(d0)
  const l1 = Math.log10(d1)
  return (v: number) => r0 + ((Math.log10(Math.max(v, d0)) - l0) / (l1 - l0 || 1)) * (r1 - r0)
}

/** Round tick values (1, 2, 5 × 10ⁿ) covering [lo, hi]. */
export function niceTicks(lo: number, hi: number, count: number): number[] {
  const span = hi - lo
  if (!(span > 0)) return [lo]
  let step = 10 ** Math.floor(Math.log10(span / count))
  const err = (count / span) * step
  if (err <= 0.15) step *= 10
  else if (err <= 0.35) step *= 5
  else if (err <= 0.75) step *= 2
  const out: number[] = []
  for (let v = Math.ceil(lo / step) * step; v <= hi + step * 1e-9; v += step) out.push(Math.round(v * 1e9) / 1e9)
  return out
}

export function clamp(v: number, lo: number, hi: number): number {
  return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v))
}
```

Create `web/src/lib/useElementSize.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

/** Measure an element with ResizeObserver (callback ref). Charts draw nothing until the width is known. Also returns the element. */
export function useElementSize<T extends Element>(): [(el: T | null) => void, { width: number; height: number }, T | null] {
  const [el, setEl] = useState<T | null>(null)
  const [size, setSize] = useState({ width: 0, height: 0 })
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!el) return
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(([entry]) => {
      const { width, height } = entry.contentRect
      setSize((s) => (s.width === Math.round(width) && s.height === Math.round(height) ? s : { width: Math.round(width), height: Math.round(height) }))
    })
    ro.observe(el)
    return () => ro.disconnect()
  }, [el])
  return [setEl, size, el]
}
```

```ts
/** Hover/focus tooltip. It only repeats what the page also shows in text or tables. */
export interface TooltipApi {
  show(content: ReactNode, x: number, y: number): void
  showAt(content: ReactNode, el: Element): void
  hide(): void
}

export const TooltipContext = createContext<TooltipApi>({ show() {}, showAt() {}, hide() {} })

export function useTooltip(): TooltipApi {
```

```ts
import type { TopicMetrics } from '../data/types'
import { DEFINITION } from './definition'
import { ciPct, DASH, fmtRatio, fmtSignedPct } from './format'
import type { I18n } from './i18n'
import type { StatusId } from './status'

type T = I18n['t']

export function statusName(t: T, status: StatusId): string {
  return t(`status.${status}` as const)
}

export function growthText(t: T, m: TopicMetrics): string {
  return m.share_growth === null || m.share_growth === undefined ? DASH : `${fmtSignedPct(m.share_growth)} ${t('perYear')}`
}

export function ciText(t: T, m: TopicMetrics, ciLevel: number): string | null {
  if (m.share_growth_lo === null || m.share_growth_lo === undefined || m.share_growth_hi === null || m.share_growth_hi === undefined) return null
  return t('ciRange', { level: ciPct(ciLevel), lo: fmtSignedPct(m.share_growth_lo), hi: fmtSignedPct(m.share_growth_hi) })
}

/** share_recent / share_baseline, or "new — no baseline" when the baseline window had no articles. */
export function ratioText(t: T, m: TopicMetrics): string {
  if (m.no_baseline || m.share_ratio === null || m.share_ratio === undefined) return t('noBaseline')
  return fmtRatio(m.share_ratio)
}

export function lowDataText(t: T, m: TopicMetrics): string | null {
  if (m.low_data_reason === 'few_articles') return t('lowData.few_articles', { n: DEFINITION.minArticlesRecent, y: DEFINITION.recentYears })
  if (m.low_data_reason === 'not_estimable') return t('lowData.not_estimable')
  return null
}
```
