- [ ] **Step 5: Trend chart** — create `web/src/charts/TrendChart.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import type { TopicForecast, TopicNowcast } from '../data/types'
import { fmtCompact, fmtInt } from '../lib/format'
import { glyphPath } from '../lib/status'
import { useTooltip } from '../lib/tooltip'
import { useElementSize } from '../lib/useElementSize'
import { linear, niceTicks } from './scale'

export interface TrendLabels {
  name: string
  forecast: string
  articles: string
  range80: (lo: string, hi: string) => string
}

/** Articles a year (line + area), and — only when the release shows a forecast — 80 % and 95 % bands with a dashed mean. */
export function TrendChart({ years, counts, forecast, nowcast, labels }: {
  years: readonly number[]
  counts: readonly number[]
  forecast: TopicForecast | null
  nowcast: TopicNowcast | null
  labels: TrendLabels
}) {
  const [ref, { width: W }] = useElementSize<HTMLDivElement>()
  const tip = useTooltip()
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<number | null>(null)
  if (W === 0 || years.length === 0) return <div ref={ref} className="tr-host" />
  const fc = forecast && forecast.show && forecast.years.length > 0 ? forecast : null
  const last = years[years.length - 1]
  const extra = [...(fc ? fc.years : []), ...(nowcast && !years.includes(nowcast.year) && !(fc?.years ?? []).includes(nowcast.year) ? [nowcast.year] : [])]
  const all = [...years, ...extra].sort((a, b) => a - b)
  const first = all[0]
  const end = all[all.length - 1]
  const H = Math.round(Math.max(220, Math.min(320, W * 0.5)))
  const M = { t: 22, r: 18, b: 32, l: 48 }
  const maxV = Math.max(1, ...counts, ...(fc ? fc.hi95 : []), ...(nowcast ? [nowcast.mean] : []))
  const ticks = niceTicks(0, maxV * 1.05, 4)
  const yMax = Math.max(ticks[ticks.length - 1] ?? maxV, maxV * 1.08)
  const x = linear(first, end === first ? first + 1 : end, M.l, W - M.r)
  const y = linear(0, yMax, H - M.b, M.t)
  const lastV = counts[counts.length - 1] ?? 0
  const line = years.map((yr, i) => `${i ? 'L' : 'M'}${x(yr).toFixed(1)},${y(counts[i] ?? 0).toFixed(1)}`).join('')
  const area = `${line}L${x(last).toFixed(1)},${y(0).toFixed(1)}L${x(years[0]).toFixed(1)},${y(0).toFixed(1)}Z`
  const band = (lo: readonly number[], hi: readonly number[]) =>
    fc
      ? `M${x(last)},${y(lastV)}${fc.years.map((yr, i) => `L${x(yr)},${y(hi[i])}`).join('')}${[...fc.years.keys()].reverse().map((i) => `L${x(fc.years[i])},${y(lo[i])}`).join('')}Z`
      : ''
  const meanLine = fc ? `M${x(last)},${y(lastV)}${fc.years.map((yr, i) => `L${x(yr)},${y(fc.mean[i])}`).join('')}` : ''
  const step = W < 420 ? 3 : 2
  const yearTicks = all.filter((yr, i) => i === all.length - 1 || (yr - first) % step === 0)
  const content = (yr: number) => {
    const i = years.indexOf(yr)
    if (i >= 0) return (<><div className="tip-title">{yr}</div><div className="tip-row"><strong>{fmtInt(counts[i])}</strong> {labels.articles}</div></>)
    const j = fc ? fc.years.indexOf(yr) : -1
    if (fc && j >= 0) {
      return (
        <>
          <div className="tip-title">{yr} · {labels.forecast}</div>
          <div className="tip-row"><strong>{fmtInt(fc.mean[j])}</strong> {labels.articles}</div>
          <div className="tip-row tip-muted">{labels.range80(fmtInt(fc.lo80[j]), fmtInt(fc.hi80[j]))}</div>
        </>
      )
    }
    if (nowcast && nowcast.year === yr) return (<><div className="tip-title">{yr}</div><div className="tip-row"><strong>{fmtInt(nowcast.mean)}</strong> {labels.articles}</div></>)
    return null
  }
  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="tr-host">
      <svg className="tr" width={W} height={H} viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`} role="img" aria-label={labels.name}>
        {ticks.map((v) => (
          <g key={v}>
            <line x1={M.l} x2={W - M.r} y1={y(v)} y2={y(v)} className={v === 0 ? 'tr-base' : 'tr-gl'} />
            <text x={M.l - 8} y={y(v) + 4} textAnchor="end" className="tr-tick">{fmtCompact(v)}</text>
          </g>
        ))}
        {yearTicks.map((yr) => (
          <text key={yr} x={x(yr)} y={H - M.b + 18} textAnchor="middle" className="tr-tick">{yr}</text>
        ))}
        {fc ? (
          <>
            <rect x={x(last + 0.5)} y={M.t} width={Math.max(0, W - M.r - x(last + 0.5))} height={H - M.b - M.t} className="tr-fzone" />
            <text x={x(last + 0.5) + 6} y={M.t + 12} className="tr-flabel">{labels.forecast}</text>
            <path d={band(fc.lo95, fc.hi95)} className="tr-band95" />
            <path d={band(fc.lo80, fc.hi80)} className="tr-band" />
          </>
        ) : null}
        <path d={area} className="tr-area" />
        <path d={line} className="tr-line" />
        {fc ? <path d={meanLine} className="tr-fline" /> : null}
        <circle cx={x(last)} cy={y(lastV)} r={4} className="tr-end" />
        <text x={x(last) - 8} y={y(lastV) - 10} textAnchor="end" className="tr-endlabel">{fmtInt(lastV)}</text>
        {nowcast ? <path transform={`translate(${x(nowcast.year)},${y(nowcast.mean)})`} d={glyphPath('WATCH', 4.4)} className="tr-now" /> : null}
        {hover !== null ? <line x1={x(hover)} x2={x(hover)} y1={M.t} y2={H - M.b} className="tr-cross" /> : null}
        <rect
          x={M.l}
          y={M.t}
          width={W - M.l - M.r}
          height={H - M.t - M.b}
          className="tr-hit"
          onPointerMove={(e) => {
            const r = e.currentTarget.ownerSVGElement?.getBoundingClientRect()
            if (!r) return
            const yr = Math.max(first, Math.min(end, Math.round(x.invert(((e.clientX - r.left) * W) / r.width))))
            setHover(yr)
            const c = content(yr)
            if (c) tip.show(c, e.clientX, e.clientY)
          }}
          onPointerLeave={() => {
            setHover(null)
            tip.hide()
          }}
        />
      </svg>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router'
import { CiWhisker } from '../components/CiWhisker'
import { DataTable } from '../components/DataTable'
import { EmptyState } from '../components/EmptyState'
import { Figure } from '../components/Figure'
import { OnThisPage } from '../components/OnThisPage'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { StatusChip, StatusGlyph } from '../components/StatusGlyph'
import { TopicLink } from '../components/TopicLink'
import { TopicSearch } from '../components/TopicSearch'
import { TrendChart } from '../charts/TrendChart'
import { useTopicDetail } from '../data/atlasContext'
import { groupName, neighborsOf, seriesOf, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { CountItem, Topic } from '../data/types'
import { DEFINITION, fmtWindow, windows } from '../lib/definition'
import { ciPct, DASH, fmtInt, fmtNum, fmtPct, fmtSignedPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { ciText, growthText, lowDataText, ratioText, statusName } from '../lib/topicText'
import { topicCodec } from '../lib/urlState'
import { useUrlState } from '../lib/useUrlState'
import { FOREST_DOMAIN } from './topicColumns'

export default function TopicPage() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return <RequireRelease title={t('tp.loadingTitle')}>{(release) => <TopicView release={release} id={id ?? ''} />}</RequireRelease>
}

function TopicView({ release, id }: { release: AtlasRelease; id: string }) {
  const topic = release.topicById.get(id)
  return topic ? <TopicContent key={topic.id} release={release} topic={topic} /> : <TopicMissing id={id} />
}

function TopicMissing({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('tp.notFound', { id: id || DASH })} lede={t('tp.notFound.body')} />
      <div className="mt-6 max-w-xl">
        <TopicSearch id="missing-search" label={t('search.label')} onPick={(tid) => navigate(`/topics/${tid}`)} />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

interface TrendRow {
  year: number
  kind: 'actual' | 'forecast' | 'nowcast'
  articles: number | null
  share: number | null
  mean: number | null
  lo80: number | null
  hi80: number | null
  lo95: number | null
  hi95: number | null
}

function TopicContent({ release, topic }: { release: AtlasRelease; topic: Topic }) {
  const { t, name, other, otherLang, lang } = useI18n()
  const detail = useTopicDetail(topic.id)
  const [state, update] = useUrlState(topicCodec)
  const m = topic.metrics
  const level = ciPct(release.ciLevel)
  const w = windows(release.overview.last_complete_year)
  const group = groupName(release, topic.group, lang)
  const years = release.series.years
  const counts = seriesOf(release, topic.id) ?? []
  const fc = topic.forecast ?? null
  const showFc = Boolean(fc?.show && fc.years.length > 0)
  const nowcast = topic.nowcast ?? null
  const ready = detail.status === 'ready' ? detail.detail : null
  const relatedIds = ready?.neighbors?.length ? ready.neighbors : neighborsOf(release, topic.id)
  const related = relatedIds.map((rid) => release.topicById.get(rid)).filter((r): r is Topic => r !== undefined).slice(0, 8)
  const keywords = ready?.keywords?.length ? ready.keywords : (topic.keywords ?? [])
  const narrative = ready?.narrative ?? null
  const journals = ready?.journals ?? []
  const institutions = ready?.institutions ?? []
  const ci = ciText(t, m, release.ciLevel)
  const lineage = topic.lineage ?? {}
  const event = lineage.event ?? 'new'
  const source = t('source.line', { release: release.overview.release, v: release.definitionVersion })

  const trendRows: TrendRow[] = [
    ...years.map((yr, i) => ({
      year: yr, kind: 'actual' as const, articles: counts[i] ?? 0,
      share: release.series.corpus_total[i] ? (counts[i] ?? 0) / release.series.corpus_total[i] : null,
      mean: null, lo80: null, hi80: null, lo95: null, hi95: null,
    })),
    ...(showFc && fc
      ? fc.years.map((yr, i) => ({ year: yr, kind: 'forecast' as const, articles: null, share: null, mean: fc.mean[i], lo80: fc.lo80[i], hi80: fc.hi80[i], lo95: fc.lo95[i], hi95: fc.hi95[i] }))
      : []),
  ]
  const toc = [
    ...(narrative ? [{ id: 'narrative', label: t('tp.narrative.h') }] : []),
    { id: 'numbers', label: t('tp.numbers.h') },
    { id: 'trend', label: t('tp.trend.h') },
    ...(keywords.length ? [{ id: 'keywords', label: t('tp.keywords.h') }] : []),
    { id: 'articles', label: t('tp.articles.h') },
    ...(related.length ? [{ id: 'related', label: t('tp.related.h') }] : []),
    ...(journals.length ? [{ id: 'journals', label: t('tp.journals.h') }] : []),
    ...(institutions.length ? [{ id: 'orgs', label: t('tp.orgs.h') }] : []),
  ]
  const countTable = (id: string, title: string, rows: readonly CountItem[], header: string) => (
    <DataTable
      caption={title}
      rows={rows}
      rowKey={(r) => r.key}
      stack={false}
      csvFilename={`atlas-${release.overview.release}-${topic.id}-${id}.csv`}
      columns={[
        { key: 'key', header, rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => r.key, sortValue: (r) => r.key },
        { key: 'count', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtInt(r.count), sortValue: (r) => r.count },
      ]}
    />
  )

  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={name(topic)} lede={topic.description || undefined}>
        <p className="tp-alt" lang={otherLang}>{other(topic)}</p>
        <div className="tp-tags">
          <StatusChip status={topic.status} label={statusName(t, topic.status)} />
          <span className="tp-field">{t('tp.field', { f: group })}</span>
          {topic.subfield_en ? <span className="tp-sub" lang="en">{topic.subfield_en}</span> : null}
          {topic.cross_field ? <span className="tp-badge">{t('tp.crossField')}</span> : null}
        </div>
        <p className="tp-lineage">
          {t(`tp.lineage.${event}` as const, { born: lineage.born ?? DASH })}
          {(lineage.parents ?? []).map((p, i) => {
            const parent = release.topicById.get(p)
            return (
              <span key={p}>
                {i ? ', ' : ' '}
                {parent ? <Link to={`/topics/${p}`}>{name(parent)}</Link> : p}
              </span>
            )
          })}
        </p>
      </PageHeader>
      <div className="layout">
        <div className="main-col">
          {narrative ? (
            <Section id="narrative" title={t('tp.narrative.h')}>
              <p className="tp-narr">{lang === 'en' ? narrative.en : narrative.th}</p>
              <p className="headline-src">{t(narrative.source === 'llm' ? 'autoSummary.llm' : 'autoSummary.template')}</p>
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          <Section id="numbers" title={t('tp.numbers.h')}>
            {m.journal_driven || topic.status === 'WATCH' ? (
              <div className="inset">
                <b><StatusGlyph status="WATCH" size={14} />{t('journalDriven')}</b>
                <p>{t('journalDrivenBody')}</p>
              </div>
            ) : null}
            <dl className="summary">
              <div>
                <dt>{t('tp.share')}</dt>
                <dd>{fmtPct(m.share_recent, 2)}<small>{t('tp.shareSub', { w: fmtWindow(w.R), n: fmtInt(m.count_recent) })}</small></dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('tp.growth')}</dt>
                <dd>
                  {m.share_growth === null || m.share_growth === undefined ? (lowDataText(t, m) ?? DASH) : growthText(t, m)}
                  {ci ? <small>{ci}</small> : null}
                  {ci ? (
                    <CiWhisker status={topic.status} value={m.share_growth} lo={m.share_growth_lo} hi={m.share_growth_hi} domain={FOREST_DOMAIN} refValue={DEFINITION.emergingMinGrowthLo} width={240} height={22} />
                  ) : null}
                </dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.ratio')}</dt>
                <dd>{ratioText(t, m)}<small>{t('tp.ratioSub', { r: fmtWindow(w.R), b: fmtWindow(w.B) })}</small></dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.journals')}</dt>
                <dd>{fmtInt(m.n_journals_R)}<small>{t('tp.inWindow', { w: fmtWindow(w.R) })}</small></dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.orgs')}</dt>
                <dd>{fmtInt(m.n_orgs_R)}<small>{m.orgs_unverified ? t('orgsUnverified') : t('tp.inWindow', { w: fmtWindow(w.R) })}</small></dd>
              </div>
              {topic.status === 'LOW_DATA' ? (
                <div>
                  <dt>{t('col.reason')}</dt>
                  <dd>{lowDataText(t, m) ?? DASH}</dd>
                </div>
              ) : null}
            </dl>
          </Section>

          <Section id="trend" title={t('tp.trend.h')}>
            {counts.length ? (
              <Figure
                id="fig-trend"
                number={1}
                title={t('tp.trend.title', { a: years[0], b: years[years.length - 1] })}
                source={source}
                view={state.trend}
                onView={(trend) => update({ trend })}
                legend={
                  <div className="legend">
                    <span><i className="lg-line" aria-hidden="true" />{t('actual')}</span>
                    {showFc ? <span><i className="lg-line is-dashed" aria-hidden="true" />{t('forecast')}</span> : null}
                    {showFc ? <span><i className="lg-band" aria-hidden="true" />{t('tp.legend.band')}</span> : null}
                    {nowcast ? <span><StatusGlyph status="WATCH" size={12} />{t('tp.nowcastLabel')}</span> : null}
                  </div>
                }
                chart={
                  <TrendChart
                    years={years}
                    counts={counts}
                    forecast={showFc ? fc : null}
                    nowcast={nowcast}
                    labels={{ name: `${t('tp.trend.title', { a: years[0], b: years[years.length - 1] })} · ${name(topic)}`, forecast: t('forecast'), articles: t('col.articles'), range80: (lo, hi) => t('tp.range80', { lo, hi }) }}
                  />
                }
                table={
                  <DataTable
                    caption={t('table.n', { n: 1, title: t('tp.trend.table') })}
                    rows={trendRows}
                    rowKey={(r) => `${r.kind}-${r.year}`}
                    stack={false}
                    csvFilename={`atlas-${release.overview.release}-${topic.id}-trend.csv`}
                    columns={[
                      { key: 'year', header: t('col.year'), rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => (r.kind === 'forecast' ? t('tp.forecastYear', { year: r.year }) : String(r.year)), csv: (r) => r.year },
                      { key: 'kind', header: 'kind', cell: () => null, csv: (r) => r.kind, csvOnly: true },
                      { key: 'articles', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => (r.articles === null ? '' : fmtInt(r.articles)), csv: (r) => r.articles },
                      { key: 'share', header: t('tp.col.shareOfYear'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => (r.share === null ? '' : fmtPct(r.share, 2)), csv: (r) => r.share },
                      { key: 'mean', header: t('forecast'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => (r.mean === null ? '' : fmtInt(r.mean)), csv: (r) => r.mean },
                      { key: 'r80', header: t('tp.col.range80'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => (r.lo80 === null ? '' : `${fmtInt(r.lo80)}–${fmtInt(r.hi80)}`), csv: (r) => (r.lo80 === null ? null : `${r.lo80}-${r.hi80}`) },
                      { key: 'r95', header: t('tp.col.range95'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => (r.lo95 === null ? '' : `${fmtInt(r.lo95)}–${fmtInt(r.hi95)}`), csv: (r) => (r.lo95 === null ? null : `${r.lo95}-${r.hi95}`) },
                    ]}
                  />
                }
                note={
                  <>
                    {showFc && fc ? (
                      <p className="note">{t('tp.fc.model', { m: fc.model, mase: fmtNum(fc.mase, 2), skill: fmtSignedPct(fc.skill_vs_naive, 0) })}</p>
                    ) : (
                      <div className="uncertain">
                        <b>{t('fc.uncertain')}</b>
                        <p>{topic.status === 'LOW_DATA' || !fc ? t('fc.lowData') : t('fc.noSkill')}</p>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {nowcast ? <p className="note">{t('tp.nowcast', { year: nowcast.year, month: nowcast.as_of_month, ytd: fmtInt(nowcast.ytd), mean: fmtInt(nowcast.mean) })}</p> : null}
                  </>
                }
              />
            ) : (
              <EmptyState title={t('tp.noSeries')} />
            )}
          </Section>

          {keywords.length ? (
            <Section id="keywords" title={t('tp.keywords.h')}>
              <ul className="kw-list">
                {keywords.map((k) => <li key={k}>{k}</li>)}
              </ul>
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          <Section id="articles" title={t('tp.articles.h')} intro={t('tp.articles.intro')}>
            {detail.status === 'loading' ? (
              <p className="loading" role="status">{t('loading')}</p>
            ) : ready && ready.articles.length ? (
              <ol className="articles">
                {ready.articles.map((a) => (
                  <li key={a.article_id}>
                    {a.url ? (
                      <a href={a.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">{a.title}<span className="sr-only"> {t('newTab')}</span></a>
                    ) : (
                      <span>{a.title}</span>
                    )}
                    {a.title_en && a.title_en !== a.title ? <span className="art-alt" lang="en">{a.title_en}</span> : null}
                    <span className="art-meta">
                      {[a.journal, a.year ? String(a.year) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}
                      {a.doi ? (
                        <>
                          {' · '}
                          <a href={`https://doi.org/${a.doi}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">DOI {a.doi}<span className="sr-only"> {t('newTab')}</span></a>
                        </>
                      ) : null}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ol>
            ) : (
              <p className="note">{t('tp.articles.none')}</p>
            )}
          </Section>

          {related.length ? (
            <Section id="related" title={t('tp.related.h')}>
              <ul className="related">
                {related.map((r) => (
                  <li key={r.id}>
                    <TopicLink topic={r} showStatus />
                    <span className="topic-sub">{statusName(t, r.status)} · {growthText(t, r.metrics)}</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          {journals.length ? (
            <Section id="journals" title={t('tp.journals.h')}>
              {countTable('journals', t('table.n', { n: 2, title: t('tp.journals.table') }), journals, t('col.journal'))}
            </Section>
          ) : null}
          {institutions.length ? (
            <Section id="orgs" title={t('tp.orgs.h')}>
              {countTable('institutions', t('table.n', { n: 3, title: t('tp.orgs.table') }), institutions, t('col.institution'))}
            </Section>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <aside className="rail" aria-label={t('onThisPage')}>
          <OnThisPage items={toc} />
          <p className="mt-6">
            <Link className="btn-secondary" to={`/compare?ids=${topic.id}`}>{t('addToCompare')}</Link>
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 hint">{level}% · {topic.id}</p>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Topic styles** — create `web/src/styles/topic.css` and add `@import "./topic.css";` after `@import "./charts.css";` in `index.css`:

```css
/* Topic page: title block, key-figure list, trend chart, keyword list, article list, related list. */
@layer components {
  .tp-alt { margin-top: 6px; color: var(--ink-2); font-size: 1.0625rem; }
  .tp-tags { margin-top: 12px; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 8px 14px; font-size: 0.9375rem; }
  .tp-sub { color: var(--ink-2); }
  .tp-badge { display: inline-block; padding: 1px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--ink); font-weight: 600; font-size: 0.875rem; }
  .tp-lineage { margin-top: 10px; font-size: 0.9375rem; color: var(--ink-2); }
  .tp-narr { font-size: 1.0625rem; line-height: 1.75; max-width: 42em; }

  .summary { margin: 14px 0 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); font-size: 1rem; }
  .summary > div { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 13rem) minmax(0, 1fr); gap: 4px 16px; padding: 10px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  .summary dt { font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.5; }
  .summary dd { margin: 0; line-height: 1.55; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .summary dd small { display: block; color: var(--ink-2); font-size: 0.875rem; }
  .summary .whisker { margin-top: 6px; }
  @media (max-width: 480px) { .summary > div { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }

  .tr-host { width: 100%; min-height: 220px; }
  .tr { display: block; overflow: visible; }
  .tr text { font-family: var(--font); }
  .tr-gl { stroke: var(--border-2); stroke-width: 1; }
  .tr-base { stroke: var(--ink); stroke-width: 1; }
  .tr-tick { font-size: 12.5px; fill: var(--ink-2); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .tr-area { fill: var(--link); opacity: 0.08; }
  .tr-line { fill: none; stroke: var(--link); stroke-width: 3; stroke-linejoin: round; stroke-linecap: round; }
  .tr-end { fill: var(--link); stroke: var(--bg); stroke-width: 2; }
  .tr-fzone { fill: var(--surface); }
  .tr-flabel { font-size: 12.5px; fill: var(--ink-2); font-weight: 600; }
  .tr-band95 { fill: var(--link); opacity: 0.1; }
  .tr-band { fill: var(--link); opacity: 0.22; }
  .tr-fline { fill: none; stroke: var(--link); stroke-width: 3; stroke-dasharray: 6 4; }
  .tr-endlabel { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; fill: var(--ink); paint-order: stroke; stroke: var(--bg); stroke-width: 3px; }
  .tr-now { fill: var(--bg); stroke: var(--st-WATCH); stroke-width: 2.5; }
  .tr-cross { stroke: var(--ink); stroke-width: 1; }
  .tr-hit { fill: transparent; cursor: crosshair; }
  .lg-line { display: inline-block; width: 22px; border-top: 3px solid var(--link); }
  .lg-line.is-dashed { border-top-style: dashed; }
  .lg-band { display: inline-block; width: 22px; height: 10px; background: var(--link); opacity: 0.22; }

  .kw-list { margin: 12px 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }
  .kw-list li { padding: 2px 10px; border: 1px solid var(--border); font-size: 0.9375rem; }
  .articles { margin: 14px 0 0; padding-left: 1.4em; display: grid; gap: 12px; }
  .articles a { font-weight: 600; }
  .art-alt { display: block; font-size: 0.9375rem; color: var(--ink-2); }
  .art-meta { display: block; font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--ink-2); }
  .related { margin: 12px 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; display: grid; gap: 10px; }
}
```

### Task 9: `/atlas` — Cartographic terrain map (canvas pan/zoom, layer toggles, field tints, contours, grid refs, focusable topics) + gazetteer

The map page is the Cartographic signature: tinted field terrain with density contours on a survey sheet, A–J × 1–6 grid references, status glyphs for every EMERGING/WATCH/HOT/DECLINING topic as focusable buttons, other topics as dots, labels that never collide, layer toggles, zoom buttons, drag to pan and Ctrl + wheel to zoom (nothing requires dragging), and a gazetteer that lists every signal topic in text with its grid reference and finds any topic by name. Selection, hidden statuses, layers and the gazetteer query live in the URL (`#/atlas?sel=T000201&hide=STABLE&layers=tints,names&q=…`).

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/features/atlas/mapSpace.ts`, `web/src/features/atlas/terrain.ts`, `web/src/features/atlas/mapSpace.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/measure.ts`, `web/src/features/atlas/TerrainMap.tsx`, `web/src/features/atlas/Gazetteer.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/strings/atlas.ts`, `web/src/styles/map.css`
- Replace: `web/src/pages/AtlasPage.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/strings/index.ts`, `web/src/styles/index.css`, `web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `atlasCodec`/`LAYERS`/`Layer`, `useUrlState`, `useElementSize` (returns the element too), `useTheme`, `useTooltip`, `TopicTip`, `CiWhisker`, `StatusGlyph`/`StatusChip`, `buildSearchIndex`/`searchTopics`, `sortBy`, `groupName`, `prefersReducedMotion`, `topicText` helpers.
- Produces:
  - `mapSpace.ts`: `MAP_W=1000`, `MAP_H=640`, `COLS`, `ROWS=6`, `MIN_ZOOM=0.8`, `MAX_ZOOM=6`, `interface MapPoint {id, x, y, r, status, group, groupIndex, share}`, `interface View {k, tx, ty}`, `interface UserView {s, dx, dy}`, `interface Box`, `toMapPoints(topics, groups, pad?)`, `gridRef(x, y)`, `fitScreen(w, h, pad?)`, `combineView(fit, user, w, h)`, `zoomAt(user, factor, px, py, w, h)`, `groupCentroids(points)`, `overlaps(a, b, pad?)`, `placeLabels(cands, obstacles, frame, force?)`, `placeCentered(cands, frame)`.
  - `terrain.ts`: `interface TerrainPoint {x, y, r, w, g}`, `interface Terrain {gw, gh, cell, owner, band, segs}`, `LEVELS`, `buildTerrain(points, nGroups, width?, height?, cell?)`.
  - `lib/measure.ts`: `textWidth(text, px, weight = 600)` (canvas `measureText` in Sarabun; estimate without a canvas).
  - `TerrainMap.tsx`: `interface TerrainMapHandle { reveal(id) }`, `interface MapGroup {name, color, cx, cy, n}`, `interface MapControls`, `<TerrainMap ref points groups terrain hidden layers selected onSelect describe tipFor names label describedBy controls />`.
  - `Gazetteer.tsx`: `<Gazetteer release points query onQuery onPick selected />`.
  - Strings `map.*`, `layer.contours|tints|names`.

Follow `DESIGN.md` (Terrain map, Map colours, The Terrain Is Not Data Rule). - [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests** — create `web/src/features/atlas/mapSpace.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import type { GroupInfo, Topic } from '../../data/types'
import { combineView, fitScreen, gridRef, MAP_H, MAP_W, placeCentered, placeLabels, toMapPoints, zoomAt } from './mapSpace'
import { buildTerrain } from './terrain'

const topic = (id: string, x: number, y: number, share: number, group = 'F01') =>
  ({ id, x, y, group, status: 'STABLE', metrics: { share_recent: share } }) as unknown as Topic
const groups = [{ id: 'F01' }, { id: 'F02' }] as unknown as GroupInfo[]

describe('map space', () => {
  it('names grid references A–J across and 1–6 down', () => {
    expect(gridRef(0, 0)).toBe('A1')
    expect(gridRef(999, 639)).toBe('J6')
    expect(gridRef(550, 330)).toBe('F4')
    expect(gridRef(-20, 9999)).toBe('A6')
  })

  it('fits any layout into the sheet with a margin, sizing marks by share', () => {
    const pts = toMapPoints([topic('T000001', -5, 2, 0.01), topic('T000002', 40, -8, 0.04, 'F02'), topic('T000003', 12, 30, 0.0)], groups)
    for (const p of pts) {
      expect(p.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(29.9)
      expect(p.x).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAP_W - 29.9)
      expect(p.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(29.9)
      expect(p.y).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAP_H - 29.9)
    }
    expect(pts[1].r).toBeGreaterThan(pts[0].r)
    expect(pts[1].groupIndex).toBe(1)
    expect(toMapPoints([topic('T000009', 7, 7, 0.1)], groups)[0]).toMatchObject({ x: MAP_W / 2, y: MAP_H / 2 })
    expect(toMapPoints([], groups)).toEqual([])
  })

  it('zooms about the pointer without moving the map point under it, within the zoom limits', () => {
    const W = 900
    const H = 600
    const fit = fitScreen(W, H)
    const start = { s: 1, dx: 30, dy: -10 }
    const before = combineView(fit, start, W, H)
    const mx = (200 - before.tx) / before.k
    const my = (150 - before.ty) / before.k
    const after = combineView(fit, zoomAt(start, 1.4, 200, 150, W, H), W, H)
    expect(mx * after.k + after.tx).toBeCloseTo(200, 6)
    expect(my * after.k + after.ty).toBeCloseTo(150, 6)
    expect(zoomAt({ s: 5.5, dx: 0, dy: 0 }, 2, 0, 0, W, H).s).toBe(6)
  })

  it('places labels without overlaps, drops blocked ones and always places forced ones', () => {
    const frame = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 400, h: 300 }
    const at = (id: string) => ({ id, text: id, width: 60, height: 13, x: 100, y: 100, radius: 6 })
    const placed = placeLabels(['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e'].map(at), [], frame, new Set(['e']))
    expect(placed.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(['a', 'b', 'e'])
    expect(placed.map((p) => p.anchor)).toEqual(['start', 'end', 'middle'])
    expect(placeLabels([at('a')], [{ x: 0, y: 0, w: 400, h: 300 }], frame)).toEqual([])
    const fields = placeCentered([{ id: 'x', text: 'X', width: 80, height: 16, x: 100, y: 100 }, { id: 'y', text: 'Y', width: 80, height: 16, x: 110, y: 104 }], frame)
    expect(fields.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual(['x'])
  })
})

describe('terrain', () => {
  it('builds density bands, owners and contours for two groups', () => {
    const t = buildTerrain([{ x: 200, y: 200, r: 10, w: 1, g: 0 }, { x: 700, y: 400, r: 10, w: 1, g: 1 }], 2)
    const at = (x: number, y: number) => Math.floor(y / t.cell) * t.gw + Math.floor(x / t.cell)
    expect(t.owner[at(200, 200)]).toBe(0)
    expect(t.owner[at(700, 400)]).toBe(1)
    expect(t.band[at(200, 200)]).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(t.band[at(5, 630)]).toBe(0)
    expect(t.segs.some((s) => s.length > 0)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('survives an empty map', () => {
    const t = buildTerrain([], 0)
    expect(t.band.every((b) => b === 0)).toBe(true)
    expect(t.segs.every((s) => s.length === 0)).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Map geometry and terrain (pure)** — create `web/src/features/atlas/mapSpace.ts`:

```ts
import type { GroupInfo, Topic } from '../../data/types'
import type { StatusId } from '../../lib/status'

/** The map sheet: a fixed 1000 × 640 space with a 10 × 6 reference grid (A–J across, 1–6 down). */
export const MAP_W = 1000
export const MAP_H = 640
export const COLS = 'ABCDEFGHIJ'
export const ROWS = 6
export const MIN_ZOOM = 0.8
export const MAX_ZOOM = 6

export interface MapPoint {
  id: string
  x: number
  y: number
  r: number
  status: StatusId
  group: string
  groupIndex: number
  share: number
}

/** Map space → screen: screen = map × k + t. */
export interface View {
  k: number
  tx: number
  ty: number
}

/** The visitor's zoom (s) and pan (dx, dy) on top of the fitted view. */
export interface UserView {
  s: number
  dx: number
  dy: number
}

export interface Box {
  x: number
  y: number
  w: number
  h: number
}

/** Fit the topics' layout coordinates into the sheet (aspect kept, `pad` margin); radius grows with √share. */
export function toMapPoints(topics: readonly Topic[], groups: readonly GroupInfo[], pad = 30): MapPoint[] {
  if (topics.length === 0) return []
  const xs = topics.map((t) => t.x)
  const ys = topics.map((t) => t.y)
  const x0 = Math.min(...xs)
  const y0 = Math.min(...ys)
  const spanX = Math.max(...xs) - x0
  const spanY = Math.max(...ys) - y0
  const k = Math.min((MAP_W - 2 * pad) / (spanX || 1), (MAP_H - 2 * pad) / (spanY || 1))
  const ox = (MAP_W - spanX * k) / 2 - x0 * k
  const oy = (MAP_H - spanY * k) / 2 - y0 * k
  const maxShare = Math.max(1e-9, ...topics.map((t) => t.metrics.share_recent))
  const index = new Map(groups.map((g, i) => [g.id, i]))
  return topics.map((t) => ({
    id: t.id,
    x: t.x * k + ox,
    y: t.y * k + oy,
    r: 3.5 + 12 * Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, t.metrics.share_recent) / maxShare),
    status: t.status,
    group: t.group,
    groupIndex: index.get(t.group) ?? -1,
    share: t.metrics.share_recent,
  }))
}

export function gridRef(x: number, y: number): string {
  const col = Math.max(0, Math.min(COLS.length - 1, Math.floor(x / 100)))
  const row = Math.max(1, Math.min(ROWS, Math.floor(y / (MAP_H / ROWS)) + 1))
  return `${COLS[col]}${row}`
}

/** The whole sheet centred in a width × height viewport. */
export function fitScreen(width: number, height: number, pad = 18): View {
  const k = Math.max(0.01, Math.min((width - 2 * pad) / MAP_W, (height - 2 * pad) / MAP_H))
  return { k, tx: (width - MAP_W * k) / 2, ty: (height - MAP_H * k) / 2 }
}

export function combineView(fit: View, user: UserView, width: number, height: number): View {
  const cx = width / 2
  const cy = height / 2
  return { k: fit.k * user.s, tx: (fit.tx - cx) * user.s + cx + user.dx, ty: (fit.ty - cy) * user.s + cy + user.dy }
}

/** Zoom by `factor` keeping the screen point (px, py) over the same map point. */
export function zoomAt(user: UserView, factor: number, px: number, py: number, width: number, height: number): UserView {
  const s = Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, user.s * factor))
  const f = s / user.s
  const cx = width / 2
  const cy = height / 2
  return { s, dx: px - cx - f * (px - cx - user.dx), dy: py - cy - f * (py - cy - user.dy) }
}

/** Mean position of each group's topics (key = group index). */
export function groupCentroids(points: readonly MapPoint[]): Map<number, { x: number; y: number; n: number }> {
  const acc = new Map<number, { x: number; y: number; n: number }>()
  for (const p of points) {
    if (p.groupIndex < 0) continue
    const a = acc.get(p.groupIndex) ?? { x: 0, y: 0, n: 0 }
    acc.set(p.groupIndex, { x: a.x + p.x, y: a.y + p.y, n: a.n + 1 })
  }
  return new Map([...acc].map(([g, a]) => [g, { x: a.x / a.n, y: a.y / a.n, n: a.n }]))
}

export function overlaps(a: Box, b: Box, pad = 0): boolean {
  return !(a.x + a.w + pad < b.x || b.x + b.w + pad < a.x || a.y + a.h + pad < b.y || b.y + b.h + pad < a.y)
}

export interface LabelCandidate {
  id: string
  text: string
  width: number
  height: number
  x: number
  y: number
  radius: number
}

export interface PlacedLabel {
  id: string
  text: string
  x: number
  y: number
  anchor: 'start' | 'middle' | 'end'
  box: Box
}

/**
 * Greedy label placement around each mark (right, left, above, below), in candidate order. A label is skipped when
 * every position leaves the frame or hits a placed label or an obstacle (marks — except its own — and field labels).
 * Ids in `force` (hovered, selected) are always placed, above the mark if nothing else fits.
 */
export function placeLabels(cands: readonly LabelCandidate[], obstacles: readonly (Box & { id?: string })[], frame: Box, force: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set()): PlacedLabel[] {
  const placed: PlacedLabel[] = []
  for (const c of cands) {
    const options: [number, number, PlacedLabel['anchor']][] = [
      [c.x + c.radius + 5, c.y + c.height / 3, 'start'],
      [c.x - c.radius - 5, c.y + c.height / 3, 'end'],
      [c.x, c.y - c.radius - 7, 'middle'],
      [c.x, c.y + c.radius + c.height + 2, 'middle'],
    ]
    const boxOf = (x: number, y: number, anchor: PlacedLabel['anchor']): Box => ({
      x: anchor === 'start' ? x : anchor === 'end' ? x - c.width : x - c.width / 2,
      y: y - c.height,
      w: c.width,
      h: c.height + 3,
    })
    const inFrame = (b: Box) => b.x >= frame.x && b.y >= frame.y && b.x + b.w <= frame.x + frame.w && b.y + b.h <= frame.y + frame.h
    const free = (b: Box) => !placed.some((p) => overlaps(p.box, b, 4)) && !obstacles.some((o) => o.id !== c.id && overlaps(o, b, 2))
    const hit = options.find(([x, y, a]) => inFrame(boxOf(x, y, a)) && free(boxOf(x, y, a)))
    const chosen = hit ?? (force.has(c.id) ? options[2] : undefined)
    if (chosen) placed.push({ id: c.id, text: c.text, x: chosen[0], y: chosen[1], anchor: chosen[2], box: boxOf(chosen[0], chosen[1], chosen[2]) })
  }
  return placed
}

/** Field names centred on their group, biggest groups first; a name that would collide or leave the frame is dropped. */
export function placeCentered(cands: readonly { id: string; text: string; width: number; height: number; x: number; y: number }[], frame: Box): PlacedLabel[] {
  const placed: PlacedLabel[] = []
  for (const c of cands) {
    const box = { x: c.x - c.width / 2, y: c.y - c.height, w: c.width, h: c.height + 4 }
    const inside = box.x >= frame.x && box.y >= frame.y && box.x + box.w <= frame.x + frame.w && box.y + box.h <= frame.y + frame.h
    if (inside && !placed.some((p) => overlaps(p.box, box, 8))) placed.push({ id: c.id, text: c.text, x: c.x, y: c.y, anchor: 'middle', box })
  }
  return placed
}
```

Create `web/src/features/atlas/terrain.ts`:

```ts
import { MAP_H, MAP_W } from './mapSpace'

/** One topic as terrain input: map position, radius, weight w (share^0.75) and group index g (-1 = none). */
export interface TerrainPoint {
  x: number
  y: number
  r: number
  w: number
  g: number
}

export interface Terrain {
  gw: number
  gh: number
  cell: number
  /** group owning each cell (strongest density), -1 if none */
  owner: Int16Array
  /** density band 0 (empty) … LEVELS.length */
  band: Uint8Array
  /** contour segments per level: x0,y0,x1,y1,… in map units */
  segs: Float32Array[]
}

/** Contour levels as fractions of the peak density (every third line is drawn heavier). */
export const LEVELS = [0.035, 0.07, 0.12, 0.19, 0.28, 0.4, 0.55, 0.72, 0.9]

type Pt = [number, number]
const CASES: Record<number, (s: { T: Pt; R: Pt; B: Pt; L: Pt }) => Pt[][]> = {
  1: ({ L, B }) => [[L, B]],
  2: ({ B, R }) => [[B, R]],
  3: ({ L, R }) => [[L, R]],
  4: ({ T, R }) => [[T, R]],
  5: ({ L, T, B, R }) => [[L, T], [B, R]],
  6: ({ T, B }) => [[T, B]],
  7: ({ L, T }) => [[L, T]],
  8: ({ L, T }) => [[L, T]],
  9: ({ T, B }) => [[T, B]],
  10: ({ T, R, L, B }) => [[T, R], [L, B]],
  11: ({ T, R }) => [[T, R]],
  12: ({ L, R }) => [[L, R]],
  13: ({ B, R }) => [[B, R]],
  14: ({ L, B }) => [[L, B]],
}

/** Gaussian density per group on a grid → hypsometric bands, the owning group per cell, marching-squares contours. */
export function buildTerrain(points: readonly TerrainPoint[], nGroups: number, width = MAP_W, height = MAP_H, cell = 4): Terrain {
  const gw = Math.ceil(width / cell)
  const gh = Math.ceil(height / cell)
  const groups = Math.max(1, nGroups)
  const per = Array.from({ length: groups }, () => new Float32Array(gw * gh))
  const total = new Float32Array(gw * gh)
  for (const p of points) {
    if (!(p.w > 0)) continue
    const sig = Math.max(9, p.r * 1.25)
    const rad = sig * 3
    const i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((p.x - rad) / cell))
    const i1 = Math.min(gw - 1, Math.ceil((p.x + rad) / cell))
    const j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((p.y - rad) / cell))
    const j1 = Math.min(gh - 1, Math.ceil((p.y + rad) / cell))
    const fi = p.g >= 0 && p.g < groups ? p.g : -1
    for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
      for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
        const dx = i * cell + cell / 2 - p.x
        const dy = j * cell + cell / 2 - p.y
        const v = p.w * Math.exp(-(dx * dx + dy * dy) / (2 * sig * sig))
        if (fi >= 0) per[fi][j * gw + i] += v
        total[j * gw + i] += v
      }
    }
  }
  let max = 0
  for (const v of total) if (v > max) max = v
  const levels = LEVELS.map((f) => f * max)
  const owner = new Int16Array(gw * gh).fill(-1)
  const band = new Uint8Array(gw * gh)
  for (let c = 0; c < total.length; c++) {
    let best = -1
    let bv = 0
    for (let f = 0; f < groups; f++) {
      if (per[f][c] > bv) {
        bv = per[f][c]
        best = f
      }
    }
    owner[c] = best
    let b = 0
    while (max > 0 && b < levels.length && total[c] >= levels[b]) b++
    band[c] = b
  }
  const segs = levels.map((lv) => {
    if (!(max > 0)) return new Float32Array(0)
    const out: number[] = []
    const val = (i: number, j: number) => total[j * gw + i]
    const lerp = (a: number, b: number, va: number, vb: number) => a + ((lv - va) / (vb - va || 1)) * (b - a)
    for (let j = 0; j < gh - 1; j++) {
      for (let i = 0; i < gw - 1; i++) {
        const v0 = val(i, j)
        const v1 = val(i + 1, j)
        const v2 = val(i + 1, j + 1)
        const v3 = val(i, j + 1)
        const idx = (v0 >= lv ? 8 : 0) | (v1 >= lv ? 4 : 0) | (v2 >= lv ? 2 : 0) | (v3 >= lv ? 1 : 0)
        if (idx === 0 || idx === 15) continue
        const x0 = i * cell + cell / 2
        const y0 = j * cell + cell / 2
        const x1 = x0 + cell
        const y1 = y0 + cell
        const sides = { T: [lerp(x0, x1, v0, v1), y0] as Pt, R: [x1, lerp(y0, y1, v1, v2)] as Pt, B: [lerp(x0, x1, v3, v2), y1] as Pt, L: [x0, lerp(y0, y1, v0, v3)] as Pt }
        for (const [a, b] of CASES[idx](sides)) out.push(a[0], a[1], b[0], b[1])
      }
    }
    return new Float32Array(out)
  })
  return { gw, gh, cell, owner, band, segs }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Text measuring** — create `web/src/lib/measure.ts`:

```ts
let ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D | null | undefined

/** Rendered width of a label in Sarabun (canvas measureText); a character estimate where no canvas exists. */
export function textWidth(text: string, px: number, weight = 600): number {
  if (ctx === undefined) ctx = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d') : null
  if (!ctx) return text.length * px * 0.58
  ctx.font = `${weight} ${px}px Sarabun, "Noto Sans Thai", system-ui, sans-serif`
  return ctx.measureText(text).width
}
```
