```ts
  'map.gaz.intro': { th: 'หัวข้อที่มีสถานะเด่นทุกหัวข้อ พร้อมพิกัดตารางบนแผนที่ อัตราโต และช่วงเชื่อมั่น {level}% หัวข้ออื่นค้นได้จากช่องค้นหา', en: 'Every topic with a signal status, with its grid reference, growth and {level}% confidence interval. Find any other topic with the search box.' },
  'map.gaz.search': { th: 'ค้นหาในดัชนีหัวข้อ', en: 'Search the gazetteer' },
  'map.gaz.results': { th: 'ผลการค้นหา {n} หัวข้อ', en: '{n} matching topics' },
  'map.gaz.none': { th: 'ไม่พบหัวข้อที่ตรงกับ “{q}”', en: 'No topics match “{q}”' },
  'map.gaz.foot': { th: 'เส้นแนวนอนคือช่วงเชื่อมั่น {level}% · เส้นประคือเกณฑ์ {bar} ต่อปี · เส้นทึบคือ 0', en: 'Horizontal line: {level}% CI · dashed: the {bar}-a-year bar · solid: zero' },
  'map.empty': { th: 'รอบข้อมูลนี้ยังไม่มีหัวข้อให้วางบนแผนที่', en: 'This release has no topics to place on the map' },
})
```

Replace `web/src/strings/index.ts`:

```ts
import { atlas } from './atlas'
import { common } from './common'
import { emerging } from './emerging'
import { overview } from './overview'
import { topic } from './topic'
import { ui } from './ui'

/** Every string file, so the parity test can detect a key defined twice. */
export const PARTS = { common, ui, overview, emerging, topic, atlas } as const

export const STR = { ...common, ...ui, ...overview, ...emerging, ...topic, ...atlas }

export type StrKey = keyof typeof STR
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The terrain map component** — create `web/src/features/atlas/TerrainMap.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useImperativeHandle, useRef, useState, type ReactNode, type Ref } from 'react'
import { textWidth } from '../../lib/measure'
import { glyphPath, isSignal, type StatusId } from '../../lib/status'
import { useTheme } from '../../lib/theme'
import { useTooltip } from '../../lib/tooltip'
import type { Layer } from '../../lib/urlState'
import { useElementSize } from '../../lib/useElementSize'
import { combineView, COLS, fitScreen, MAP_H, MAP_W, placeCentered, placeLabels, ROWS, zoomAt, type MapPoint, type UserView } from './mapSpace'
import type { Terrain } from './terrain'

export interface TerrainMapHandle {
  /** Pan so that topic `id` is inside the view (used when a gazetteer row is picked). */
  reveal(id: string): void
}

/** A field as drawn on the map; its array index is MapPoint.groupIndex. */
export interface MapGroup {
  name: string
  /** design-token key from taxonomy.json, e.g. "cat-03" */
  color: string
  cx: number
  cy: number
  n: number
}

export interface MapControls {
  zoomIn: string
  zoomOut: string
  reset: string
  group: string
  hint: string
}

const ROW_H = MAP_H / ROWS
const PRIORITY: Record<StatusId, number> = { EMERGING: 0, WATCH: 1, HOT: 2, DECLINING: 3, STABLE: 4, LOW_DATA: 5 }
const PAN: Record<string, [number, number]> = { ArrowLeft: [60, 0], ArrowRight: [-60, 0], ArrowUp: [0, 60], ArrowDown: [0, -60] }
const RESET: UserView = { s: 1, dx: 0, dy: 0 }

function rgb(value: string): [number, number, number] {
  const v = value.trim()
  const hex = /^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(v)
  if (hex) {
    const n = parseInt(hex[1], 16)
    return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255]
  }
  const parts = v.match(/[\d.]+/g)
  return parts && parts.length >= 3 ? [Number(parts[0]), Number(parts[1]), Number(parts[2])] : [200, 200, 200]
}

/** Hypsometric field tints as a small image (one pixel per terrain cell), dimmed outside the focused field. */
function tintCanvas(terrain: Terrain, colors: readonly [number, number, number][], focus: number | null): HTMLCanvasElement {
  const cv = document.createElement('canvas')
  cv.width = terrain.gw
  cv.height = terrain.gh
  const ctx = cv.getContext('2d')
  if (!ctx) return cv
  const img = ctx.createImageData(terrain.gw, terrain.gh)
  for (let c = 0; c < terrain.band.length; c++) {
    const b = terrain.band[c]
    const o = terrain.owner[c]
    if (!b || o < 0) continue
    const col = colors[o] ?? [200, 200, 200]
    let a = Math.min(0.92, 0.28 + b * 0.085)
    if (focus !== null && o !== focus) a *= 0.28
    img.data[c * 4] = col[0]
    img.data[c * 4 + 1] = col[1]
    img.data[c * 4 + 2] = col[2]
    img.data[c * 4 + 3] = Math.round(a * 255)
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0)
  return cv
}

/**
 * Cartographic sheet: canvas (grid, tints, contours, dots for STABLE/LOW_DATA) under an SVG overlay
 * (grid references, field names, focusable markers for signal topics and the selection, collision-free labels).
 * Keyboard: Tab to markers, Enter/Space to select; on the sheet itself arrows pan, + − zoom, 0 resets.
 */
export function TerrainMap({ ref, points, groups, terrain, hidden, layers, selected, onSelect, describe, tipFor, names, label, describedBy, controls }: {
  ref?: Ref<TerrainMapHandle>
  points: readonly MapPoint[]
  groups: readonly MapGroup[]
  terrain: Terrain
  hidden: ReadonlySet<StatusId>
  layers: ReadonlySet<Layer>
  selected: string | null
  onSelect: (id: string) => void
  describe: (p: MapPoint) => string
  tipFor: (id: string) => ReactNode
  names: ReadonlyMap<string, string>
  label: string
  describedBy: string
  controls: MapControls
}) {
  const [wrapRef, size, wrapEl] = useElementSize<HTMLDivElement>()
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null)
  const tints = useRef(new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>())
  const drag = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; dx: number; dy: number } | null>(null)
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserView>(RESET)
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<{ id: string | null; group: number | null }>({ id: null, group: null })
  const theme = useTheme()
  const tip = useTooltip()
  const W = size.width
  const H = size.height
  const view = W > 0 && H > 0 ? combineView(fitScreen(W, H), user, W, H) : null

  // Redraw the canvas after every render (pan, zoom, hover, layers, theme all change the picture).
  useEffect(() => {
    const cv = canvasRef.current
    if (!cv || !view) return
    const dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1)
    const pw = Math.round(W * dpr)
    const ph = Math.round(H * dpr)
    if (cv.width !== pw || cv.height !== ph) {
      cv.width = pw
      cv.height = ph
    }
    const ctx = cv.getContext('2d')
    if (!ctx) return
    const css = getComputedStyle(cv)
    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, pw, ph)
    ctx.setTransform(dpr * view.k, 0, 0, dpr * view.k, dpr * view.tx, dpr * view.ty)
    ctx.strokeStyle = css.getPropertyValue('--map-grid').trim()
    ctx.lineWidth = 1 / view.k
    ctx.beginPath()
    for (let gx = 0; gx <= MAP_W; gx += 100) {
      ctx.moveTo(gx, 0)
      ctx.lineTo(gx, MAP_H)
    }
    for (let r = 0; r <= ROWS; r++) {
      ctx.moveTo(0, r * ROW_H)
      ctx.lineTo(MAP_W, r * ROW_H)
    }
    ctx.stroke()
    if (layers.has('tints')) {
      const key = `${theme}|${hover.group ?? ''}`
      let tc = tints.current.get(key)
      if (!tc) {
        tc = tintCanvas(terrain, groups.map((g) => rgb(css.getPropertyValue(`--${g.color}`))), hover.group)
        tints.current.set(key, tc)
      }
      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true
      ctx.drawImage(tc, 0, 0, MAP_W, MAP_H)
    }
    if (layers.has('contours')) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = css.getPropertyValue('--map-contour').trim()
      terrain.segs.forEach((s, li) => {
        const heavy = li % 3 === 2
        ctx.globalAlpha = heavy ? 0.85 : 0.5
        ctx.lineWidth = (heavy ? 1.5 : 0.85) / view.k
        ctx.beginPath()
        for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i += 4) {
          ctx.moveTo(s[i], s[i + 1])
          ctx.lineTo(s[i + 2], s[i + 3])
        }
        ctx.stroke()
      })
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = css.getPropertyValue('--map-dot').trim()
    for (const p of points) {
      if (isSignal(p.status) || hidden.has(p.status) || p.id === selected) continue
      if (hover.group !== null && p.groupIndex !== hover.group) continue
      ctx.beginPath()
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(1.4 / view.k, Math.min(p.r * 0.4, 3.6 / view.k)), 0, Math.PI * 2)
      ctx.fill()
    }
  })

  // Ctrl/⌘ + wheel zooms about the pointer; a plain wheel keeps scrolling the page.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!wrapEl) return
    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      if (!(e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) return
      e.preventDefault()
      const r = wrapEl.getBoundingClientRect()
      setUser((u) => zoomAt(u, e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.15 : 1 / 1.15, e.clientX - r.left, e.clientY - r.top, r.width, r.height))
    }
    wrapEl.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false })
    return () => wrapEl.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel)
  }, [wrapEl])

  useImperativeHandle(
    ref,
    () => ({
      reveal(id: string) {
        const p = points.find((q) => q.id === id)
        if (!p || !view) return
        const sx = p.x * view.k + view.tx
        const sy = p.y * view.k + view.ty
        if (sx < 40 || sx > W - 40 || sy < 40 || sy > H - 40) setUser((u) => ({ ...u, dx: u.dx + W / 2 - sx, dy: u.dy + H / 2 - sy }))
      },
    }),
    [points, view, W, H],
  )

  const markers = view
    ? points
        .filter((p) => !hidden.has(p.status) && (isSignal(p.status) || p.id === selected))
        .map((p) => ({ p, sx: p.x * view.k + view.tx, sy: p.y * view.k + view.ty, r: Math.max(5.5, Math.min(18, p.r * view.k * 0.9)) }))
    : []
  const small = W < 600
  const fieldSize = small ? 13 : 16
  const fieldLabels = view
    ? placeCentered(
        groups
          .map((g, gi) => ({ g, gi }))
          .sort((a, b) => b.g.n - a.g.n)
          .map(({ g, gi }) => ({ id: String(gi), text: g.name, width: textWidth(g.name, fieldSize, 700), height: fieldSize, x: g.cx * view.k + view.tx, y: g.cy * view.k + view.ty })),
        { x: 20, y: 18, w: W - 26, h: H - 22 },
      )
    : []
  const force = new Set([selected, hover.id].filter((v): v is string => v !== null))
  const ranked = [...markers].sort((a, b) => PRIORITY[a.p.status] - PRIORITY[b.p.status] || b.p.share - a.p.share)
  const named = [...ranked.filter((m) => force.has(m.p.id)), ...ranked.filter((m, i) => !force.has(m.p.id) && layers.has('names') && i < (small ? 4 : 25))]
  const nameLabels = view
    ? placeLabels(
        named.map((m) => {
          const text = names.get(m.p.id) ?? m.p.id
          return { id: m.p.id, text, width: textWidth(text, 13, 600), height: 13, x: m.sx, y: m.sy, radius: m.r }
        }),
        [...markers.map((m) => ({ id: m.p.id, x: m.sx - m.r, y: m.sy - m.r, w: 2 * m.r, h: 2 * m.r })), ...fieldLabels.map((f) => f.box)],
        { x: 18, y: 18, w: W - 22, h: H - 22 },
        force,
      )
    : []
  const zoom = (factor: number) => setUser((u) => zoomAt(u, factor, W / 2, H / 2, W, H))
  const enter = (p: MapPoint) => setHover({ id: p.id, group: p.groupIndex >= 0 ? p.groupIndex : null })
  const leave = () => setHover({ id: null, group: null })

  return (
    <div className="sheet">
      <div
        ref={wrapRef}
        className="sheet-in"
        tabIndex={0}
        role="group"
        aria-label={label}
        aria-describedby={describedBy}
        onPointerDown={(e) => {
          if ((e.target as Element).closest('.mnode')) return
          drag.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, dx: user.dx, dy: user.dy }
          e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)
        }}
        onPointerMove={(e) => {
          const d = drag.current
          if (!d) return
          const mx = e.clientX - d.x
          const my = e.clientY - d.y
          if (Math.abs(mx) + Math.abs(my) < 3) return
          tip.hide()
          setUser((u) => ({ ...u, dx: d.dx + mx, dy: d.dy + my }))
        }}
        onPointerUp={() => {
          drag.current = null
        }}
        onPointerCancel={() => {
          drag.current = null
        }}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) return
          const move = PAN[e.key]
          if (move) {
            e.preventDefault()
            setUser((u) => ({ ...u, dx: u.dx + move[0], dy: u.dy + move[1] }))
          } else if (e.key === '+' || e.key === '=') {
            e.preventDefault()
            zoom(1.4)
          } else if (e.key === '-') {
            e.preventDefault()
            zoom(1 / 1.4)
          } else if (e.key === '0') {
            e.preventDefault()
            setUser(RESET)
          }
        }}
      >
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="terrain" aria-hidden="true" />
        {view ? (
          <svg className="ov" width={W} height={H} viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`}>
            <g aria-hidden="true">
              {COLS.split('').map((c, i) => {
                const sx = (i * 100 + 50) * view.k + view.tx
                return sx < 12 || sx > W - 12 ? null : (
                  <g key={c}>
                    <rect x={sx - 9} y={0} width={18} height={16} className="gridref-bg" />
                    <text x={sx} y={12} textAnchor="middle" className="gridref">{c}</text>
                  </g>
                )
              })}
              {Array.from({ length: ROWS }, (_, r) => {
                const sy = (r * ROW_H + ROW_H / 2) * view.k + view.ty
                return sy < 12 || sy > H - 12 ? null : (
                  <g key={r}>
                    <rect x={0} y={sy - 9} width={16} height={18} className="gridref-bg" />
                    <text x={8} y={sy + 4} textAnchor="middle" className="gridref">{r + 1}</text>
                  </g>
                )
              })}
              {fieldLabels.map((f) => (
                <text key={f.id} x={f.x} y={f.y} textAnchor="middle" className={`flabel${hover.group === null ? '' : String(hover.group) === f.id ? ' on' : ' dim'}`} style={{ fontSize: fieldSize }}>
                  {f.text}
                </text>
              ))}
            </g>
            <g>
              {markers.map(({ p, sx, sy, r }) => (
                <g
                  key={p.id}
                  data-id={p.id}
                  className="mnode"
                  transform={`translate(${sx.toFixed(1)},${sy.toFixed(1)})`}
                  role="button"
                  tabIndex={0}
                  aria-label={describe(p)}
                  aria-pressed={p.id === selected}
                  onClick={() => onSelect(p.id)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                      e.preventDefault()
                      onSelect(p.id)
                    }
                  }}
                  onPointerEnter={(e) => {
                    enter(p)
                    tip.show(tipFor(p.id), e.clientX, e.clientY)
                  }}
                  onPointerLeave={() => {
                    leave()
                    tip.hide()
                  }}
                  onFocus={(e) => {
                    enter(p)
                    tip.showAt(tipFor(p.id), e.currentTarget)
                  }}
                  onBlur={() => {
                    leave()
                    tip.hide()
                  }}
                >
                  {p.id === selected ? (
                    <>
                      <circle r={r + 12} className="sel-ring2" />
                      <circle r={r + 7} className="sel-ring" />
                    </>
                  ) : null}
                  <circle r={Math.max(r + 5, 14)} className="hit" />
                  <path className={`mk mk-${p.status}`} d={glyphPath(p.status, r)} />
                </g>
              ))}
            </g>
            <g aria-hidden="true">
              {nameLabels.map((l) => (
                <text key={l.id} x={l.x} y={l.y} textAnchor={l.anchor} className={l.id === hover.id ? 'mlabel hover' : 'mlabel'}>
                  {l.text}
                </text>
              ))}
            </g>
          </svg>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <div className="zoom" role="group" aria-label={controls.group}>
        <button type="button" aria-label={controls.zoomIn} onClick={() => zoom(1.4)}>
          <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M8 2v12M2 8h12" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" /></svg>
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-label={controls.zoomOut} onClick={() => zoom(1 / 1.4)}>
          <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M2 8h12" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" /></svg>
        </button>
        <button type="button" aria-label={controls.reset} onClick={() => setUser(RESET)}>
          <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M2.5 6V2.5H6M13.5 10v3.5H10M2.5 2.5l4 4M13.5 13.5l-4-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.8" /></svg>
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="map-hint">{controls.hint}</p>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The gazetteer** — create `web/src/features/atlas/Gazetteer.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { CiWhisker } from '../../components/CiWhisker'
import { StatusGlyph } from '../../components/StatusGlyph'
import { groupName, type AtlasRelease } from '../../data/release'
import type { Topic } from '../../data/types'
import { DEFINITION } from '../../lib/definition'
import { ciPct, fmtInt, fmtSignedPct } from '../../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../../lib/i18n'
import { buildSearchIndex, searchTopics } from '../../lib/search'
import { sortBy } from '../../lib/sort'
import { SIGNAL_STATUSES } from '../../lib/status'
import { statusName } from '../../lib/topicText'
import { FOREST_DOMAIN } from '../../pages/topicColumns'
import { gridRef, type MapPoint } from './mapSpace'

/** The map in text: every signal topic by status with its grid reference and CI, plus a search over all topics. */
export function Gazetteer({ release, points, query, onQuery, onPick, selected }: {
  release: AtlasRelease
  points: readonly MapPoint[]
  query: string
  onQuery: (q: string) => void
  onPick: (id: string) => void
  selected: string | null
}) {
  const { t, name, lang } = useI18n()
  const level = ciPct(release.ciLevel)
  const refOf = useMemo(() => new Map(points.map((p) => [p.id, gridRef(p.x, p.y)])), [points])
  const index = useMemo(() => buildSearchIndex(release.topics.map((tp) => ({ id: tp.id, th: tp.th, en: tp.en, keywords: tp.keywords ?? [], size: tp.size }))), [release])
  const q = query.trim()
  const hits = q
    ? searchTopics(index, q, 20)
        .map((h) => release.topicById.get(h.id))
        .filter((tp): tp is Topic => tp !== undefined)
    : []
  return (
    <>
      <div className="gaz-search">
        <label htmlFor="gaz-q" className="search-label">{t('map.gaz.search')}</label>
        <input id="gaz-q" type="search" className="gaz-input" value={query} autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} onChange={(e) => onQuery(e.target.value)} />
        <div className="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite">
          {q ? (hits.length ? t('map.gaz.results', { n: hits.length }) : t('map.gaz.none', { q })) : ''}
        </div>
        {q && hits.length ? (
          <ul className="gaz-hits">
            {hits.map((tp) => (
              <li key={tp.id}>
                <button type="button" className="grow" aria-pressed={tp.id === selected} onClick={() => onPick(tp.id)}>
                  <span className="g-name">
                    <StatusGlyph status={tp.status} size={12} />
                    {name(tp)}
                  </span>
                  <span className="g-ref">{refOf.get(tp.id) ?? ''}</span>
                  <span className="g-sub">{statusName(t, tp.status)} · {groupName(release, tp.group, lang)}</span>
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : null}
        {q && !hits.length ? <p className="search-none">{t('map.gaz.none', { q })}</p> : null}
      </div>
      <div className="gaz">
        {SIGNAL_STATUSES.map((st) => {
          const list = sortBy(release.topics.filter((tp) => tp.status === st), (tp) => tp.metrics.share_growth, st === 'DECLINING' ? 'asc' : 'desc')
          return (
            <div className="gcol" key={st}>
              <h3 className="gcol-h">
                <StatusGlyph status={st} size={16} />
                <span>{statusName(t, st)}</span>
                <small>{fmtInt(list.length)}</small>
              </h3>
              <ul className="glist">
                {list.map((tp) => (
                  <li key={tp.id}>
                    <button type="button" className="grow" aria-pressed={tp.id === selected} onClick={() => onPick(tp.id)}>
                      <span className="g-name">{name(tp)}</span>
                      <span className="g-ref">{refOf.get(tp.id) ?? ''}</span>
                      <span className="g-meta">
                        <span className="g-wh">
                          <CiWhisker status={tp.status} value={tp.metrics.share_growth} lo={tp.metrics.share_growth_lo} hi={tp.metrics.share_growth_hi} domain={FOREST_DOMAIN} refValue={DEFINITION.emergingMinGrowthLo} width={140} />
                        </span>
                        <span className="g-val">{fmtSignedPct(tp.metrics.share_growth)}</span>
                      </span>
                      <span className="g-sub">
                        {groupName(release, tp.group, lang)}
                        {tp.metrics.journal_driven ? ` · ${t('journalDriven')}` : ''}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
      <p className="note">{t('map.gaz.foot', { level, bar: fmtSignedPct(DEFINITION.emergingMinGrowthLo, 0) })}</p>
    </>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: The page** — replace `web/src/pages/AtlasPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { EmptyState } from '../components/EmptyState'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { StatusChip, StatusGlyph } from '../components/StatusGlyph'
import { TopicTip } from '../components/TopicTip'
import { groupName, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import { Gazetteer } from '../features/atlas/Gazetteer'
import { groupCentroids, gridRef, MAP_H, MAP_W, toMapPoints } from '../features/atlas/mapSpace'
import { TerrainMap, type MapGroup, type TerrainMapHandle } from '../features/atlas/TerrainMap'
import { buildTerrain } from '../features/atlas/terrain'
import { ciPct, fmtInt, fmtPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { prefersReducedMotion } from '../lib/scroll'
import { STATUSES, type StatusId } from '../lib/status'
import { ciText, growthText, statusName } from '../lib/topicText'
import { atlasCodec, LAYERS } from '../lib/urlState'
import { useUrlState } from '../lib/useUrlState'

export default function AtlasPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return <RequireRelease title={t('map.title')}>{(release) => <Atlas release={release} />}</RequireRelease>
}

function Atlas({ release }: { release: AtlasRelease }) {
  const { t, name, other, otherLang, lang } = useI18n()
  const [state, update] = useUrlState(atlasCodec)
  const mapRef = useRef<TerrainMapHandle>(null)
  const points = useMemo(() => toMapPoints(release.topics, release.taxonomy.groups), [release])
  const terrain = useMemo(
    () => buildTerrain(points.map((p) => ({ x: p.x, y: p.y, r: p.r, w: Math.max(0, p.share) ** 0.75, g: p.groupIndex })), release.taxonomy.groups.length),
    [points, release],
  )
  const centroids = useMemo(() => groupCentroids(points), [points])
  const names = useMemo(() => new Map(release.topics.map((tp) => [tp.id, lang === 'en' ? tp.en || tp.th : tp.th || tp.en])), [release, lang])
  if (points.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="container">
        <PageHeader title={t('map.title')} />
        <EmptyState title={t('map.empty')} />
      </div>
    )
  }
  const groups: MapGroup[] = release.taxonomy.groups.map((g, i) => ({
    name: lang === 'en' ? g.en || g.th : g.th || g.en,
    color: g.color,
    cx: centroids.get(i)?.x ?? MAP_W / 2,
    cy: centroids.get(i)?.y ?? MAP_H / 2,
    n: centroids.get(i)?.n ?? 0,
  }))
  const hidden = new Set<StatusId>(state.hide)
  const layers = new Set(state.layers)
  const counts = Object.fromEntries(STATUSES.map((s) => [s, release.topics.filter((tp) => tp.status === s).length])) as Record<StatusId, number>
  const sel = state.sel ? (release.topicById.get(state.sel) ?? null) : null
  const selPoint = sel ? points.find((p) => p.id === sel.id) : undefined
  const selCi = sel ? ciText(t, sel.metrics, release.ciLevel) : null
  const pick = (id: string) => {
    update({ sel: id })
    mapRef.current?.reveal(id)
    document.getElementById('atlas-map')?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: prefersReducedMotion() ? 'auto' : 'smooth' })
  }
  return (
    <div className="container wide">
      <PageHeader title={t('map.title')} lede={t('map.lede', { n: fmtInt(release.topics.length) })} />
      <div className="atlas-grid">
        <figure className="atlas-fig" id="atlas-map" aria-labelledby="atlas-fig-title">
          <p className="figtitle" id="atlas-fig-title">{t('figure.n', { n: 1, title: t('map.fig', { release: release.overview.release }) })}</p>
          <p className="sr-only" id="atlas-desc">{t('map.desc')}</p>
          <TerrainMap
            ref={mapRef}
            points={points}
            groups={groups}
            terrain={terrain}
            hidden={hidden}
            layers={layers}
            selected={state.sel}
            onSelect={(id) => update({ sel: id })}
            describe={(p) => `${names.get(p.id) ?? p.id} · ${statusName(t, p.status)} · ${t('map.grid')} ${gridRef(p.x, p.y)}`}
            tipFor={(id) => {
              const tp = release.topicById.get(id)
              return tp ? <TopicTip topic={tp} ciLevel={release.ciLevel} /> : null
            }}
            names={names}
            label={t('map.aria')}
            describedBy="atlas-desc"
            controls={{ zoomIn: t('map.zoomIn'), zoomOut: t('map.zoomOut'), reset: t('map.reset'), group: t('map.zoomGroup'), hint: t('map.hint') }}
          />
          <p className="source">
            {t('source.line', { release: release.overview.release, v: release.definitionVersion })} · {t('map.scale')}
          </p>
        </figure>
        <aside className="atlas-side" aria-label={t('map.key.h')}>
          <section className="cart" id="atlas-selection" aria-labelledby="sel-h" aria-live="polite">
            <h2 id="sel-h" className="cart-h">{t('map.sel.h')}</h2>
            {sel ? (
              <>
                <p className="sel-name">
                  <b>{name(sel)}</b>
                  {selPoint ? <span className="g-ref">{gridRef(selPoint.x, selPoint.y)}</span> : null}
                </p>
                <p className="tp-alt" lang={otherLang}>{other(sel)}</p>
                <p className="mt-2"><StatusChip status={sel.status} label={statusName(t, sel.status)} small /></p>
                <dl className="summary compact">
                  <div><dt>{t('col.field')}</dt><dd>{groupName(release, sel.group, lang)}</dd></div>
                  <div><dt>{t('tp.share')}</dt><dd>{fmtPct(sel.metrics.share_recent, 2)}</dd></div>
                  <div><dt>{t('tp.growth')}</dt><dd>{growthText(t, sel.metrics)}{selCi ? <small>{selCi}</small> : null}</dd></div>
                </dl>
                <p className="mt-4"><Link className="btn" to={`/topics/${sel.id}`}>{t('map.sel.open')}</Link></p>
                <p className="mt-2"><button type="button" className="linkbtn" onClick={() => update({ sel: null })}>{t('map.sel.clear')}</button></p>
              </>
            ) : (
              <p className="hint">{t('map.sel.none')}</p>
            )}
          </section>
          <section className="cart" aria-labelledby="key-h">
            <h2 id="key-h" className="cart-h">{t('map.key.h')}</h2>
            <fieldset className="keyset">
              <legend>{t('map.key.statuses')}</legend>
              <div className="keylist">
                {STATUSES.map((st) => (
                  <label key={st}>
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={!hidden.has(st)}
                      onChange={(e) => update({ hide: e.target.checked ? state.hide.filter((s) => s !== st) : [...state.hide, st] })}
                    />
                    <StatusGlyph status={st} size={14} />
                    <span>{statusName(t, st)} {fmtInt(counts[st])}</span>
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
            <fieldset className="keyset">
              <legend>{t('map.key.layers')}</legend>
              <div className="layers">
                {LAYERS.map((ly) => (
                  <label key={ly}>
                    <input type="checkbox" checked={layers.has(ly)} onChange={(e) => update({ layers: e.target.checked ? [...state.layers, ly] : state.layers.filter((x) => x !== ly) })} />
                    <span>{t(`layer.${ly}` as const)}</span>
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
            <div className="keyrow">
              <svg width="46" height="22" viewBox="0 0 46 22" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                {[3, 6, 9].map((r, i) => <circle key={r} cx={[6, 18, 35][i]} cy={11} r={r} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2" />)}
              </svg>
              <span>{t('map.key.size')}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="keyrow">
              <svg width="46" height="22" viewBox="0 0 46 22" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                <path d="M2 16c8-10 18-10 26-4s12 4 16-2" fill="none" stroke="var(--map-contour)" strokeWidth="1.5" />
                <path d="M8 19c8-7 14-6 22-2s8 2 12-1" fill="none" stroke="var(--map-contour)" strokeWidth="0.9" opacity="0.6" />
              </svg>
              <span>{t('map.key.contour')}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="keyrow">
              <svg width="46" height="22" viewBox="0 0 46 22" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                {[[8, 9], [16, 14], [24, 8], [31, 13], [39, 10]].map(([x, y]) => <circle key={`${x}-${y}`} cx={x} cy={y} r={2} fill="var(--map-dot)" />)}
              </svg>
              <span>{t('map.key.dot')}</span>
            </div>
          </section>
        </aside>
      </div>
      <Section id="gazetteer" title={t('map.gaz.h')} intro={t('map.gaz.intro', { level: ciPct(release.ciLevel) })}>
        <Gazetteer release={release} points={points} query={state.q} onQuery={(q) => update({ q })} onPick={pick} selected={state.sel} />
      </Section>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Map styles** — create `web/src/styles/map.css` and add `@import "./map.css";` after `@import "./topic.css";` in `index.css`:

```css
/* /atlas — the Cartographic signature on civic chrome: survey sheet, overlay, cartouche panels, gazetteer. */
@layer components {
  .container.wide { max-width: 1480px; }
  .atlas-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 320px; gap: 20px; margin-top: 18px; align-items: start; }
  @media (max-width: 1099px) { .atlas-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }
  .atlas-fig { margin: 0; min-width: 0; }

  .sheet { position: relative; margin-top: 10px; border: 2px solid var(--ink); background: var(--map-sheet); padding: 5px; }
  .sheet-in { position: relative; height: clamp(420px, 68vh, 760px); border: 1px solid var(--ink); overflow: hidden; touch-action: none; cursor: grab; background: var(--map-sheet); }
  .sheet-in:active { cursor: grabbing; }
  .sheet-in:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid var(--focus); outline-offset: 2px; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px var(--focus-ink); }
  @media (max-width: 520px) { .sheet-in { height: 380px; } }
  canvas.terrain { position: absolute; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; }
  .ov { position: absolute; inset: 0; overflow: visible; }
  .ov text { font-family: var(--font); }
  .gridref { font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; fill: var(--ink-2); }
  .gridref-bg { fill: var(--map-sheet); }
  .flabel { font-weight: 700; fill: var(--ink); paint-order: stroke; stroke: var(--map-sheet); stroke-width: 4px; stroke-linejoin: round; opacity: 0.86; pointer-events: none; }
  .flabel.dim { opacity: 0.35; }
  .flabel.on { opacity: 1; }
  .mnode { cursor: pointer; outline: none; }
  .mnode .hit { fill: transparent; }
  .mnode .mk { stroke: var(--map-sheet); stroke-width: 1.6; }
  .mnode:hover .mk { stroke: var(--ink); stroke-width: 2.4; }
  .mnode:focus-visible .hit { fill: var(--focus); }
  .mnode:focus-visible .mk { stroke: var(--focus-ink); stroke-width: 2.6; }
  .sel-ring { fill: none; stroke: var(--ink); stroke-width: 1.5; }
  .sel-ring2 { fill: none; stroke: var(--ink); stroke-width: 1; stroke-dasharray: 2 3; }
  .mlabel { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; fill: var(--ink); paint-order: stroke; stroke: var(--map-sheet); stroke-width: 3.5px; stroke-linejoin: round; pointer-events: none; }
  .mlabel.hover { font-size: 14px; }
  .zoom { position: absolute; right: 16px; bottom: 44px; z-index: 3; display: flex; flex-direction: column; border: 1px solid var(--ink); background: var(--bg); box-shadow: var(--shadow); }
  .zoom button { width: 44px; height: 44px; border: 0; background: transparent; display: grid; place-items: center; color: var(--ink); }
  .zoom button + button { border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  .zoom button:hover { background: var(--surface); }
  .zoom svg { width: 16px; height: 16px; display: block; }
  .map-hint { margin: 6px 0 0; font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--ink-2); }

  .atlas-side { display: grid; gap: 16px; min-width: 0; }
  .cart { position: relative; background: var(--bg); border: 1px solid var(--ink); padding: 14px 16px; }
  .cart::before { content: ""; position: absolute; inset: 3px; border: 1px solid var(--border-2); pointer-events: none; }
  .cart > * { position: relative; }
  .cart-h { font-size: 1.0625rem; margin-bottom: 8px; }
  .keyset { border: 0; margin: 0 0 10px; padding: 0; min-width: 0; }
  .keyset legend { font-weight: 700; font-size: 0.9375rem; margin-bottom: 4px; }
  .keylist { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 2px 10px; font-size: 0.9375rem; }
  .keylist label, .layers label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; cursor: pointer; min-height: 32px; }
  .keylist input, .layers input { width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: var(--ink); margin: 0; flex: none; }
  .layers { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 2px 14px; font-size: 0.9375rem; }
  .keyrow { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; margin-top: 8px; font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--ink-2); }
  .keyrow svg { flex: none; display: block; }
  .sel-name { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: baseline; gap: 4px 10px; }
  .summary.compact > div { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 7.5rem) minmax(0, 1fr); padding: 6px 0; }
  .g-ref { display: inline-block; font-weight: 700; font-size: 0.8125rem; border: 1px solid var(--ink); border-radius: 2px; padding: 0 4px; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; align-self: start; }

  .gaz-search { max-width: 560px; margin-top: 16px; }
  .gaz-input { width: 100%; min-height: 44px; padding: 6px 10px; border: 2px solid var(--field-border); border-radius: 0; background: var(--bg); color: var(--ink); font: inherit; font-size: 1rem; }
  .gaz-hits { list-style: none; margin: 8px 0 0; padding: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  .gaz { margin-top: 22px; display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)); border: 1px solid var(--ink); }
  .gcol { min-width: 0; padding: 14px 16px 18px; }
  .gcol + .gcol { border-left: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  .gcol-h { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 1.0625rem; }
  .gcol-h small { margin-left: auto; font-weight: 600; font-size: 0.8125rem; color: var(--ink-2); }
  .glist { list-style: none; margin: 10px 0 0; padding: 0; }
  .grow { width: 100%; display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto; gap: 2px 10px; padding: 9px 4px; border: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); background: transparent; text-align: left; color: var(--ink); }
  .grow:hover { background: var(--surface); }
  .grow[aria-pressed="true"] { background: var(--surface); box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--ink); }
  .g-name { font-size: 0.9375rem; font-weight: 600; line-height: 1.5; min-width: 0; display: inline-flex; gap: 6px; align-items: baseline; }
  .g-meta { grid-column: 1 / -1; display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto; gap: 8px; align-items: center; }
  .g-wh { min-width: 0; height: 18px; overflow: hidden; }
  .g-val { font-weight: 600; font-size: 0.8125rem; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .g-sub { grid-column: 1 / -1; font-size: 0.8125rem; color: var(--ink-2); line-height: 1.45; }
  @media (max-width: 1099px) {
    .gaz { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; }
    .gcol:nth-child(3) { border-left: 0; }
    .gcol:nth-child(n+3) { border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  }
  @media (max-width: 620px) {
    .gaz { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .gcol + .gcol { border-left: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--border-2); }
  }
}
```

### Task 10: `/compare` — 2–4 topics overlaid (share or count), table, key figures, CSV

R10 for researchers and editors: pick up to four topics (search combobox, or start from suggested EMERGING/HOT topics), overlay their yearly series as a share of all articles (default — not inflated by the growing corpus) or as counts. Lines are told apart by colour **and** dash pattern **and** a direct end label; the table view and a key-figures table carry the same numbers; the selection lives in the URL (`#/compare?ids=T000201,T000701&metric=count`).

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/charts/series.ts`, `web/src/charts/MultiLineChart.tsx`, `web/src/strings/compare.ts`, `web/src/styles/pages.css`
- Replace: `web/src/pages/ComparePage.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/strings/index.ts`, `web/src/styles/index.css`, `web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TopicSearch` (`exclude`), `Figure`, `DataTable`, `topicColumns` factories, `compareCodec`, `seriesOf`, `linear`/`niceTicks`, `useTooltip`, `useElementSize`, `.tr-*` axis classes from `topic.css`.
- Produces: `charts/series.ts`: `SERIES_DASHES` (slot 1–4); `charts/MultiLineChart.tsx`: `interface LineSeries {id, label, values}`, `<MultiLineChart years series format axisFormat label />`; strings `cmp.*`; `styles/pages.css` (compare, data, about classes — Task 11 appends).

```ts
import { defineStrings } from './define'

export const compare = defineStrings({
  'cmp.title': { th: 'เปรียบเทียบหัวข้อวิจัย', en: 'Compare research topics' },
  'cmp.lede': { th: 'เลือก 2–4 หัวข้อเพื่อดูแนวโน้มรายปีซ้อนกัน วัดเป็นสัดส่วนของบทความทั้งหมด (ไม่หลอกตาเพราะจำนวนบทความรวมเพิ่มขึ้นทุกปี) หรือเป็นจำนวนบทความ', en: 'Choose 2–4 topics to overlay their yearly trends, as a share of all articles (not inflated by the growing total) or as article counts.' },

  'cmp.max': { th: 'เลือกได้สูงสุด 4 หัวข้อ เอาหัวข้อออกก่อนเพื่อเพิ่มหัวข้อใหม่', en: 'Up to 4 topics. Remove one to add another.' },

  'cmp.try': { th: 'ลองเริ่มจากหัวข้อเหล่านี้', en: 'Try starting with these' },

})
```

Replace `web/src/strings/index.ts`:

```ts
import { atlas } from './atlas'
import { common } from './common'
import { compare } from './compare'
import { emerging } from './emerging'
import { overview } from './overview'
import { topic } from './topic'
import { ui } from './ui'

/** Every string file, so the parity test can detect a key defined twice. */
export const PARTS = { common, ui, overview, emerging, topic, atlas, compare } as const

export const STR = { ...common, ...ui, ...overview, ...emerging, ...topic, ...atlas, ...compare }

export type StrKey = keyof typeof STR
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The chart** — create `web/src/charts/series.ts`:

```ts
/** Dash pattern per compare-series slot: the secondary encoding next to colour (--series-1…4) and the end label. */
export const SERIES_DASHES = ['', '8 4', '3 3', '10 3 2 3'] as const
```

Create `web/src/charts/MultiLineChart.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTooltip } from '../lib/tooltip'
import { useElementSize } from '../lib/useElementSize'
import { linear, niceTicks } from './scale'
import { SERIES_DASHES } from './series'

export interface LineSeries {
  id: string
  label: string
  values: readonly (number | null)[]
}

/** 2–4 series overlaid on one axis (R10); end labels on wide screens, the legend always; crosshair + tooltip. */
export function MultiLineChart({ years, series, format, axisFormat, label }: {
  years: readonly number[]
  series: readonly LineSeries[]
  format: (v: number) => string
  axisFormat: (v: number) => string
  label: string
}) {
  const [ref, { width: W }] = useElementSize<HTMLDivElement>()
  const tip = useTooltip()
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<number | null>(null)
  if (W === 0 || years.length === 0 || series.length === 0) return <div ref={ref} className="ml-host" />
  const narrow = W < 520
  const H = Math.round(Math.max(260, Math.min(380, W * 0.55)))
  const M = { t: 18, r: narrow ? 12 : 170, b: 32, l: 56 }
  const values = series.flatMap((s) => s.values.filter((v): v is number => v !== null))
  const maxV = Math.max(0, ...values)
  const ticks = niceTicks(0, maxV * 1.05 || 1, 4)
  const yMax = Math.max(ticks[ticks.length - 1] ?? 1, maxV * 1.08) || 1
  const first = years[0]
  const last = years[years.length - 1]
  const x = linear(first, last === first ? first + 1 : last, M.l, W - M.r)
  const y = linear(0, yMax, H - M.b, M.t)
  const path = (vals: readonly (number | null)[]) => {
    let d = ''
    let pen = false
    vals.forEach((v, i) => {
      if (v === null) {
        pen = false
        return
      }
      d += `${pen ? 'L' : 'M'}${x(years[i]).toFixed(1)},${y(v).toFixed(1)}`
      pen = true
    })
    return d
  }
  const ends = series
    .map((s, i) => {
      let j = s.values.length - 1
      while (j >= 0 && s.values[j] === null) j--
      return j < 0 ? null : { i, x: x(years[j]), y: y(s.values[j] as number), text: s.label }
    })
    .filter((e): e is { i: number; x: number; y: number; text: string } => e !== null)
    .sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y)
  const labels = ends.reduce<{ i: number; x: number; y: number; ly: number; text: string }[]>((acc, e) => {
    const prev = acc[acc.length - 1]
    return [...acc, { ...e, ly: prev ? Math.max(e.y, prev.ly + 16) : e.y }]
  }, [])
  const step = narrow ? 3 : 2
  const yearTicks = years.filter((yr, i) => i === years.length - 1 || (yr - first) % step === 0)
  const content = (yr: number) => {
    const i = years.indexOf(yr)
    return (
      <>
        <div className="tip-title">{yr}</div>
        {series.map((s, k) => (
          <div className="tip-row" key={s.id}>
            <svg width="22" height="8" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
              <line x1="0" x2="22" y1="4" y2="4" style={{ stroke: `var(--series-${k + 1})` }} strokeWidth="3" strokeDasharray={SERIES_DASHES[k]} />
            </svg>{' '}
            <strong>{s.values[i] === null || s.values[i] === undefined ? '–' : format(s.values[i] as number)}</strong> {s.label}
          </div>
        ))}
      </>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="ml-host">
      <svg className="ml" width={W} height={H} viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`} role="img" aria-label={label}>
        {ticks.map((v) => (
          <g key={v}>
            <line x1={M.l} x2={W - M.r} y1={y(v)} y2={y(v)} className={v === 0 ? 'tr-base' : 'tr-gl'} />
            <text x={M.l - 8} y={y(v) + 4} textAnchor="end" className="tr-tick">{axisFormat(v)}</text>
          </g>
        ))}
        {yearTicks.map((yr) => (
          <text key={yr} x={x(yr)} y={H - M.b + 18} textAnchor="middle" className="tr-tick">{yr}</text>
        ))}
        {series.map((s, k) => (
          <path key={s.id} d={path(s.values)} className="ml-line" style={{ stroke: `var(--series-${k + 1})` }} strokeDasharray={SERIES_DASHES[k]} />
        ))}
        {ends.map((e) => (
          <circle key={e.i} cx={e.x} cy={e.y} r={3.5} className="ml-end" style={{ fill: `var(--series-${e.i + 1})` }} />
        ))}
        {narrow
          ? null
          : labels.map((l) => (
              <text key={l.i} x={l.x + 8} y={l.ly + 4} className="ml-label">{l.text.length > 24 ? `${l.text.slice(0, 23)}…` : l.text}</text>
            ))}
        {hover !== null ? <line x1={x(hover)} x2={x(hover)} y1={M.t} y2={H - M.b} className="tr-cross" /> : null}
        <rect
          x={M.l}
          y={M.t}
          width={Math.max(0, W - M.l - M.r)}
          height={H - M.t - M.b}
          className="tr-hit"
          onPointerMove={(e) => {
            const r = e.currentTarget.ownerSVGElement?.getBoundingClientRect()
            if (!r) return
            const yr = Math.max(first, Math.min(last, Math.round(x.invert(((e.clientX - r.left) * W) / r.width))))
            setHover(yr)
            tip.show(content(yr), e.clientX, e.clientY)
          }}
          onPointerLeave={() => {
            setHover(null)
            tip.hide()
          }}
        />
      </svg>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The page** — replace `web/src/pages/ComparePage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { DataTable, type Column } from '../components/DataTable'
import { EmptyState } from '../components/EmptyState'
import { Figure } from '../components/Figure'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { StatusGlyph } from '../components/StatusGlyph'
import { TopicSearch } from '../components/TopicSearch'
import { MultiLineChart, type LineSeries } from '../charts/MultiLineChart'
import { SERIES_DASHES } from '../charts/series'
import { groupName, seriesOf, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { Topic } from '../data/types'
import { ciPct, fmtCompact, fmtInt, fmtPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { sortBy } from '../lib/sort'
import { compareCodec } from '../lib/urlState'
import { useUrlState } from '../lib/useUrlState'
import { ciColumn, growthColumn, idColumn, journalsColumn, orgsColumn, shareColumn, statusColumn, topicColumn, type ColumnContext } from './topicColumns'

export default function ComparePage() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return <RequireRelease title={t('cmp.title')}>{(release) => <Compare release={release} />}</RequireRelease>
}

interface YearRow {
  year: number
  values: (number | null)[]
}

function Swatch({ slot }: { slot: number }) {
  return (
    <svg className="cmp-swatch" width="28" height="10" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
      <line x1="0" x2="28" y1="5" y2="5" style={{ stroke: `var(--series-${slot + 1})` }} strokeWidth="3" strokeDasharray={SERIES_DASHES[slot]} />
    </svg>
  )
}

function Compare({ release }: { release: AtlasRelease }) {
  const { t, name, lang } = useI18n()
  const [state, update] = useUrlState(compareCodec)
  const chosen = state.ids.map((id) => release.topicById.get(id)).filter((tp): tp is Topic => tp !== undefined)
  const years = release.series.years
  const totals = release.series.corpus_total
  const share = state.metric === 'share'
  const series: LineSeries[] = chosen.map((tp) => {
    const counts = seriesOf(release, tp.id) ?? []
    return {
      id: tp.id,
      label: name(tp),
      values: years.map((_, i) => (share ? (totals[i] ? (counts[i] ?? 0) / totals[i] : null) : (counts[i] ?? null))),
    }
  })
  const format = (v: number) => (share ? fmtPct(v, 2) : fmtInt(v))
  const axisFormat = (v: number) => (share ? fmtPct(v, v > 0 && v < 0.01 ? 1 : 0) : fmtCompact(v))
  const add = (id: string) => update({ ids: [...state.ids.filter((x) => x !== id), id].slice(0, 4) })
  const remove = (id: string) => update({ ids: state.ids.filter((x) => x !== id) })
  const suggestions = sortBy(release.topics.filter((tp) => (tp.status === 'EMERGING' || tp.status === 'HOT') && !state.ids.includes(tp.id)), (tp) => tp.score, 'desc').slice(0, 3)
  const ctx: ColumnContext = { t, name, groupName: (g) => groupName(release, g, lang) }
  const rows: YearRow[] = years.map((year, i) => ({ year, values: series.map((s) => s.values[i] ?? null) }))
  const yearColumns: Column<YearRow>[] = [
    { key: 'year', header: t('col.year'), rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => String(r.year), csv: (r) => r.year },
    ...series.map((s, k) => ({ key: s.id, header: s.label, numeric: true, cell: (r: YearRow) => (r.values[k] === null ? '–' : format(r.values[k] as number)), csv: (r: YearRow) => r.values[k] })),
  ]
  const figTitle = t(share ? 'cmp.fig.share' : 'cmp.fig.count', { a: years[0] ?? '', b: years[years.length - 1] ?? '' })
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('cmp.title')} lede={t('cmp.lede')} />
      <div className="cmp-picker">
        {chosen.length < 4 ? <TopicSearch id="cmp-search" label={t('cmp.add')} onPick={add} exclude={state.ids} /> : <p className="hint">{t('cmp.max')}</p>}
        {chosen.length ? (
          <ul className="cmp-chips" aria-label={t('cmp.chosen')}>
            {chosen.map((tp, i) => (
              <li key={tp.id} className="cmp-chip">
                <Swatch slot={i} />
                <StatusGlyph status={tp.status} size={12} />
                <Link to={`/topics/${tp.id}`}>{name(tp)}</Link>
                <button type="button" className="cmp-remove" aria-label={t('cmp.remove', { n: name(tp) })} onClick={() => remove(tp.id)}>
                  <svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="14" height="14" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path d="M3 3l10 10M13 3L3 13" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" /></svg>
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : null}
        <fieldset className="cmp-metric">
          <legend>{t('cmp.metric')}</legend>
          <label>
            <input type="radio" name="cmp-metric" checked={share} onChange={() => update({ metric: 'share' })} />
            <span>{t('cmp.metric.share')}</span>
          </label>
          <label>
            <input type="radio" name="cmp-metric" checked={!share} onChange={() => update({ metric: 'count' })} />
            <span>{t('cmp.metric.count')}</span>
          </label>
        </fieldset>
      </div>
      {chosen.length < 2 ? (
        <EmptyState title={t('cmp.needTwo')}>
          {suggestions.length ? (
            <>
              <p>{t('cmp.try')}</p>
              <ul className="cmp-suggest">
                {suggestions.map((tp) => (
                  <li key={tp.id}>
                    <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => add(tp.id)}>
                      <StatusGlyph status={tp.status} size={12} />
                      {name(tp)}
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </>
          ) : null}
        </EmptyState>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Section id="cmp-trend" title={t('cmp.chart.h')}>
            <Figure
              id="fig-cmp"
              number={1}
              title={figTitle}
              source={t('source.line', { release: release.overview.release, v: release.definitionVersion })}
              view={state.view}
              onView={(view) => update({ view })}
              legend={
                <div className="legend">
                  {series.map((s, k) => (
                    <span key={s.id}>
                      <Swatch slot={k} />
                      {s.label}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              }
              chart={<MultiLineChart years={years} series={series} format={format} axisFormat={axisFormat} label={figTitle} />}
              table={
                <DataTable
                  caption={t('table.n', { n: 1, title: t('cmp.table') })}
                  rows={rows}
                  columns={yearColumns}
                  rowKey={(r) => String(r.year)}
                  stack={false}
                  csvFilename={`atlas-${release.overview.release}-compare-${state.metric}.csv`}
                />
              }
              note={<p className="note">{t('cmp.how')}</p>}
            />
          </Section>
          <Section id="cmp-figures" title={t('cmp.metrics.h')}>
            <DataTable
              caption={t('table.n', { n: 2, title: t('cmp.metrics.table') })}
              rows={chosen}
              columns={[idColumn(), topicColumn(ctx), statusColumn(ctx), shareColumn(ctx), growthColumn(ctx), ciColumn(ctx, ciPct(release.ciLevel)), journalsColumn(ctx), orgsColumn(ctx)]}
              rowKey={(r) => r.id}
              csvFilename={`atlas-${release.overview.release}-compare-figures.csv`}
            />
          </Section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```css
/* /compare (and, from Task 11, /data and /about). */
@layer components {
  .cmp-picker { display: grid; gap: 14px; margin-top: 18px; max-width: 760px; }
  .cmp-chips { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }
  .cmp-chip { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 4px 4px 4px 10px; border: 1px solid var(--ink); max-width: 100%; }
  .cmp-chip a { font-weight: 600; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
  .cmp-swatch { flex: none; display: block; }
  .cmp-remove { display: grid; place-items: center; width: 32px; height: 32px; border: 0; background: transparent; color: var(--ink); }
  .cmp-remove:hover { background: var(--surface); }
  .cmp-metric { border: 0; padding: 0; margin: 0; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 4px 18px; }
  .cmp-metric legend { font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px; }
  .cmp-metric label { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; min-height: 36px; cursor: pointer; }
  .cmp-metric input { width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: var(--ink); margin: 0; }
  .cmp-suggest { list-style: none; margin: 10px 0 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; }
  .ml-host { width: 100%; min-height: 260px; }
  .ml { display: block; overflow: visible; }
  .ml text { font-family: var(--font); }
  .ml-line { fill: none; stroke-width: 3; stroke-linejoin: round; stroke-linecap: round; }
  .ml-end { stroke: var(--bg); stroke-width: 2; }
  .ml-label { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; fill: var(--ink); paint-order: stroke; stroke: var(--bg); stroke-width: 3px; stroke-linejoin: round; }
}
```

### Task 11: `/data` (downloads, CSV, transparency panel E12, data dictionary) and `/about` (method, definition, privacy, changelog)

Open data and trust: every published file downloadable as JSON (the real file on Pages, rebuilt from the embedded bundle in the offline edition), three CSV tables (BOM for Excel), the transparency panel E12 (completeness, trimmed years, missing years, unassigned articles, text-quality flags, ingest errors, journal-driven and unverified topics, label sources, LLM fallbacks, embedding model, taxonomy accuracy, definition version, build), the data dictionary, and the citation text. `/about` explains the method in plain language, lists the six statuses with their rules (from the same strings as `/emerging`), privacy/copyright, and the definition changelog up to the release's `definition_version`; it works even when no data loads.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/content/method.ts`, `web/src/content/changelog.ts`, `web/src/content/dictionary.ts`, `web/src/strings/data.ts`, `web/src/strings/about.ts`
- Replace: `web/src/pages/DataPage.tsx`, `web/src/pages/AboutPage.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/strings/index.ts`, `web/src/styles/pages.css` (append), `web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `dataUrl`, `toCsv`/`downloadText`, `DataTable`, `windows`/`fmtWindow`/`DEFINITION`, `useRelease`, `rule.*` strings (Task 7), `BRAND`.
- Produces: `content/method.ts`: `METHOD: {id, h: Entry, body: Entry[]}[]` (placeholders `{R} {B} {W}`); `content/changelog.ts`: `interface ChangelogEntry {version, date, th, en}`, `CHANGELOG`; `content/dictionary.ts`: `interface DictEntry {file, field, th, en}`, `DICTIONARY`; strings `data.*`, `about.*`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Content modules**

Create `web/src/content/method.ts`:

```ts
import type { Entry } from '../strings/define'

/** /about: the method in plain language. {R} {B} {W} are filled with the release's windows. */
export const METHOD: readonly { id: string; h: Entry; body: readonly Entry[] }[] = [
  {
    id: 'data',
    h: { th: 'ข้อมูลมาจากไหน', en: 'Where the data comes from' },
    body: [
      {
        th: 'ข้อมูลคือบทความในวารสารไทยที่อยู่ในฐานข้อมูล TCI และเผยแพร่ผ่าน ThaiJO ระบบอ่านชื่อเรื่อง บทคัดย่อ คำสำคัญ ปีที่พิมพ์ และสังกัดของผู้แต่ง แปลงปี พ.ศ. เป็น ค.ศ. ซ่อมตัวอักษรไทยที่เพี้ยนจากการแปลงไฟล์ PDF และตัดบทความซ้ำออก',
        en: 'The data is the articles in Thai journals indexed by TCI and published on ThaiJO. The system reads titles, abstracts, keywords, publication years and author affiliations, converts Buddhist-era years to the Gregorian calendar, repairs Thai characters damaged by PDF conversion and removes duplicates.',
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'topics',
    h: { th: 'หัวข้อวิจัยได้มาอย่างไร', en: 'How the topics are found' },
    body: [
      {
        th: 'แต่ละบทความถูกแปลงเป็นเวกเตอร์ความหมายด้วยโมเดลภาษาที่อ่านได้ทั้งไทยและอังกฤษ แล้วจัดบทความที่ความหมายใกล้กันเป็นหัวข้อ รวมกลุ่มที่ซ้ำกัน และตั้งชื่อหัวข้อจากคำสำคัญของผู้แต่งหรือด้วย AI ที่บรรณาธิการสุ่มตรวจ หัวข้อมีรหัสคงที่ข้ามรอบข้อมูลเพื่อให้ติดตามได้',
        en: 'Each article is turned into a meaning vector by a language model that reads Thai and English, articles close in meaning are grouped into topics, duplicate groups are merged, and topics are named from author keywords or by AI that editors spot-check. Topic ids stay the same across releases so topics can be followed over time.',
      },
      {
        th: 'สาขาหลักอ้างอิงสาขาของ OpenAlex เพื่อให้เทียบกับต่างประเทศได้ ตำแหน่งบนแผนที่ตรึงไว้ข้ามรอบ ระยะห่างบอกความคล้ายของเนื้อหา ไม่ใช่ภูมิศาสตร์',
        en: 'The main fields follow OpenAlex fields so the atlas can be compared internationally. Map positions are kept stable across releases; distance shows similarity of content, not geography.',
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'status',
    h: { th: 'สถานะของหัวข้อคำนวณอย่างไร', en: 'How a topic’s status is decided' },
    body: [
      {
        th: 'สถานะดูจากอัตราโตของ “สัดส่วน” บทความ ไม่ใช่จำนวนบทความ เพราะจำนวนบทความทั้งหมดเพิ่มขึ้นทุกปี อัตราโตประมาณด้วยแบบจำลอง Poisson จากทุกปีในช่วง {W} พร้อมช่วงเชื่อมั่น แล้วเทียบช่วงล่าสุด {R} กับช่วงฐาน {B} และตรวจว่าการเติบโตมาจากหลายวารสารและหลายสถาบัน',
        en: 'Status looks at growth in the share of articles, not the number of articles, because the total grows every year. Growth is estimated with a Poisson model from every year in {W}, with a confidence interval; the recent window {R} is compared with the baseline {B}, and the growth must come from several journals and institutions.',
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'forecast',
    h: { th: 'การคาดการณ์', en: 'Forecasts' },
    body: [
      {
        th: 'คาดการณ์ 2 ปีจากสัดส่วนของหัวข้อคูณจำนวนบทความรวม เลือกโมเดลด้วยการทดสอบย้อนหลังหลายจุด และแสดงตัวเลขเฉพาะหัวข้อที่โมเดลแม่นกว่าการใช้ค่าของปีล่าสุด หัวข้ออื่นแสดงว่า “แนวโน้มไม่แน่นอน” ช่วงคาดการณ์ 80% และ 95% ปรับให้ตรงกับความคลาดเคลื่อนจริงในอดีต',
        en: 'Two-year forecasts multiply the topic’s share by the total number of articles. Models are chosen by back-testing from several starting points, and numbers are shown only where the model beats repeating last year’s value; other topics say “trend uncertain”. The 80% and 95% intervals are calibrated on past errors.',
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'limits',
    h: { th: 'ข้อจำกัดที่ควรรู้', en: 'Limits to keep in mind' },
    body: [
      {
        th: 'วารสารที่เพิ่งเข้าระบบหรืออัปโหลดฉบับย้อนหลังทำให้หัวข้อดูเหมือนโตเร็ว ระบบจึงแยกสถานะ “จับตา” ออกมา ปีที่ข้อมูลยังเข้าไม่ครบจะไม่นำมาคำนวณ ข้อมูลสังกัดผู้แต่งบางส่วนไม่ครบ จึงมีหัวข้อที่ตรวจความกว้างของสถาบันไม่ได้ และตัวเลขทั้งหมดเป็นการวิเคราะห์เชิงสถิติ ไม่ใช่การประเมินคุณภาพงานวิจัย',
        en: 'A journal that was just indexed or uploaded back issues can make a topic look fast-growing, so such topics get the separate “watch” status. Years whose data is still arriving are not counted. Some author affiliations are incomplete, so some topics cannot have their institution breadth checked. All figures are statistical analysis, not an assessment of research quality.',
      },
    ],
  },
]
```

```ts
/** Changes to the status definition (docs/EMERGING_SPEC.md). /about shows entries up to the release's definition_version. */
export interface ChangelogEntry {
  version: number
  date: string
  th: string
  en: string
}

export const CHANGELOG: readonly ChangelogEntry[] = [
  {
    version: 2,
    date: '2026-10-18',
    th: 'อัตราโตของสัดส่วนประมาณด้วย Poisson GLM พร้อมช่วงเชื่อมั่น 90% เกณฑ์หัวข้อใหญ่แบบสัมพัทธ์ (25% บนสุด) เกณฑ์ความกว้าง 3 วารสารและ 3 สถาบัน และสถานะใหม่ “จับตา” กับ “ข้อมูลน้อย”',
    en: 'Share growth from a Poisson GLM with a 90% confidence interval, a relative “large” threshold (top 25%), a breadth test of 3 journals and 3 institutions, and the new “watch” and “low data” statuses.',
  },
  {
    version: 3,
    date: '2026-10-18',
    th: 'ช่วงเชื่อมั่นแบบ t ที่ทนต่อปีที่ไม่มีบทความ ธงเมื่อข้อมูลสังกัดไม่พอตรวจความกว้างของสถาบัน หัวข้อที่ประมาณอัตราโตไม่ได้ถือเป็น “ข้อมูลน้อย” และไม่นับปีที่ข้อมูลยังเข้าไม่ครบ',
    en: 'A t-based interval that copes with years without articles, a flag when affiliations are too sparse to check institution breadth, topics whose growth cannot be estimated count as “low data”, and incomplete years are not counted.',
  },
]
```

```ts
/** /data: what the published files and main fields mean (full specification: docs/DATA_SPEC.md). */
export interface DictEntry {
  file: string
  field: string
  th: string
  en: string
}

export const DICTIONARY: readonly DictEntry[] = [
  { file: 'latest.json', field: 'release, path', th: 'รอบข้อมูลล่าสุดและตำแหน่งไฟล์ manifest ของรอบนั้น', en: 'The newest release and the path of its manifest' },
  { file: 'manifest.json', field: 'files[].bytes, sha256', th: 'ขนาดและค่า sha256 ของทุกไฟล์ในรอบ ใช้ตรวจว่าไฟล์ไม่ถูกแก้ไข', en: 'Size and sha256 of every file in the release, to check the files are unaltered' },
  { file: 'manifest.json', field: 'definition_version, ci_level', th: 'ฉบับของนิยามสถานะ และระดับของช่วงเชื่อมั่นทุกช่วงในรอบนี้', en: 'Status-definition version and the level of every interval in the release' },
  { file: 'overview.json', field: 'kpis', th: 'ตัวเลขหลัก: บทความ หัวข้อ สาขา วารสาร สถาบัน และสัดส่วนที่มีบทคัดย่ออังกฤษ DOI และคำสำคัญ', en: 'Headline figures: articles, topics, fields, journals, institutions, and the shares with an English abstract, a DOI and keywords' },
  { file: 'overview.json', field: 'brief', th: 'ประโยคสรุปและประเด็นสำหรับผู้บริหาร จากแม่แบบหรือ AI ที่ตรวจตัวเลขแล้ว', en: 'Headline sentence and executive points, from templates or number-checked AI' },
  { file: 'overview.json', field: 'data_quality', th: 'ปีที่หายไป บทความที่ยังไม่จัดหมวด วิธีตรวจความครบถ้วน และธงคุณภาพข้อความ', en: 'Missing years, unassigned articles, the completeness method and text-quality flags' },
  { file: 'taxonomy.json', field: 'groups[]', th: 'สาขาหลัก (OpenAlex fields หรือกลุ่มสำรอง) สีเป็นชื่อ token เช่น cat-01', en: 'Main fields (OpenAlex fields or fallback groups); colours are token names such as cat-01' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'id', th: 'รหัสหัวข้อที่คงที่ข้ามรอบ เช่น T000123', en: 'Topic id, stable across releases, e.g. T000123' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'status', th: 'EMERGING กำลังเกิดใหม่ · WATCH จับตา · HOT กำลังมาแรง · DECLINING ลดลง · STABLE คงที่ · LOW_DATA ข้อมูลน้อย', en: 'EMERGING · WATCH · HOT · DECLINING · STABLE · LOW_DATA' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'metrics.share_recent, share_baseline', th: 'สัดส่วนบทความของหัวข้อในช่วงล่าสุดและช่วงฐาน เป็นทศนิยม 0–1', en: 'The topic’s share of articles in the recent and baseline windows, as fractions 0–1' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'metrics.share_growth, share_growth_lo, share_growth_hi', th: 'อัตราโตของสัดส่วนต่อปี (0.10 = +10%) และขอบล่าง/บนของช่วงเชื่อมั่นที่ระดับ ci_level', en: 'Yearly share growth (0.10 = +10%) and the lower/upper bounds of its interval at ci_level' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'metrics.share_ratio, no_baseline', th: 'สัดส่วนช่วงล่าสุดหารด้วยช่วงฐาน เป็น null และ no_baseline = true เมื่อช่วงฐานไม่มีบทความ', en: 'Recent share divided by baseline share; null with no_baseline = true when the baseline had no articles' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'metrics.n_journals_R, n_orgs_R', th: 'จำนวนวารสารและสถาบันในช่วงล่าสุด (เกณฑ์ความกว้าง)', en: 'Journals and institutions in the recent window (the breadth test)' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'metrics.journal_driven, orgs_unverified', th: 'โตจากวารสารเดียว / ตรวจความกว้างของสถาบันไม่ได้', en: 'Growth from a single journal / institution breadth not verified' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'metrics.low_data_reason', th: 'few_articles = บทความน้อย, not_estimable = ประมาณอัตราโตไม่ได้', en: 'few_articles = too few articles, not_estimable = growth could not be estimated' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'forecast', th: 'คาดการณ์ 2 ปี (mean, lo80/hi80, lo95/hi95) แสดงเมื่อ show = true คือโมเดลแม่นกว่าการใช้ค่าปีล่าสุด', en: 'Two-year forecast (mean, lo80/hi80, lo95/hi95), shown when show = true, i.e. the model beats repeating last year' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'nowcast', th: 'ประมาณการปีปัจจุบันจากข้อมูลถึงเดือนล่าสุด', en: 'Current-year estimate from data up to the latest month' },
  { file: 'topics.json', field: 'x, y, r', th: 'ตำแหน่งบนแผนที่ (คงที่ข้ามรอบ) และขนาด', en: 'Map position (kept stable across releases) and size' },
  { file: 'series.json', field: 'years, corpus_total, topics', th: 'จำนวนบทความรายปีของทั้งคลังและของแต่ละหัวข้อ (ค่าดิบ)', en: 'Articles a year for the whole corpus and for each topic (raw counts)' },
  { file: 'edges.json', field: 's, t, w', th: 'คู่หัวข้อที่ใกล้กันและน้ำหนักความใกล้', en: 'Pairs of related topics and their similarity weight' },
  { file: 'keywords.json', field: 'term, emerging, journal_driven', th: 'คำสำคัญเกิดใหม่ วัดจากคำสำคัญของผู้แต่ง', en: 'Emerging keywords, measured on author keywords' },
  { file: 'topics/<id>.json', field: 'articles[]', th: 'บทความตัวแทน: ชื่อเรื่อง วารสาร ปี ลิงก์ ThaiJO และ DOI (ไม่มีบทคัดย่อ)', en: 'Representative articles: title, journal, year, ThaiJO link and DOI (no abstracts)' },
  { file: 'topics/<id>.json', field: 'journals[], institutions[]', th: 'วารสารและสถาบันที่มีบทความในหัวข้อนี้มากที่สุด (ระดับองค์กร ไม่ใช่บุคคล)', en: 'Journals and institutions with the most articles on the topic (organisations, never people)' },
]
```

```ts
  'data.cite.h': { th: 'การอ้างอิงและสัญญาอนุญาต', en: 'Citation and licence' },
  'data.cite.text': { th: 'TCI Research Atlas (รอบข้อมูล {release}, {date}). แผนที่ภูมิทัศน์งานวิจัยไทย. ศูนย์ดัชนีการอ้างอิงวารสารไทย (TCI).', en: 'TCI Research Atlas (release {release}, {date}). A map of the Thai research landscape. Thai-Journal Citation Index Centre (TCI).' },
  'data.cite.licence': { th: 'เผยแพร่ภายใต้ CC BY 4.0 ไม่มีบทคัดย่อเต็มและไม่มีข้อมูลระดับบุคคล ชื่อเรื่องบทความลิงก์กลับไปยัง ThaiJO', en: 'Published under CC BY 4.0. No full abstracts and no person-level data; article titles link back to ThaiJO.' },
})
```

```ts
import { defineStrings } from './define'

export const about = defineStrings({
  'about.title': { th: 'วิธีการและนิยาม', en: 'Method and definitions' },
  'about.lede': { th: 'Atlas นี้สร้างอย่างไร สถานะแต่ละแบบหมายถึงอะไร และนิยามเปลี่ยนไปอย่างไรในแต่ละรอบ อธิบายด้วยภาษาง่าย', en: 'How this atlas is made, what each status means and how the definition has changed between releases, in plain language.' },
  'about.generic.R': { th: '3 ปีล่าสุดที่ข้อมูลครบ', en: 'the last 3 complete years' },
  'about.generic.B': { th: '5 ปีก่อนหน้านั้น', en: 'the 5 years before' },
  'about.generic.W': { th: '5 ปีล่าสุด', en: 'the last 5 years' },
  'about.def.h': { th: 'นิยามสถานะ', en: 'Status definitions' },
  'about.def.intro': { th: 'นิยามฉบับที่ {v} ช่วงเชื่อมั่นทุกช่วงใช้ระดับ {level}% ระบบตรวจสถานะตามลำดับด้านล่าง และหัวข้อได้สถานะแรกที่เข้าเกณฑ์', en: 'Definition version {v}; every interval is at the {level}% level. Statuses are checked in the order below, and a topic takes the first one it meets.' },
  'about.def.order': { th: 'เกณฑ์ตัวเลขทั้งหมดเป็นค่าตั้งของระบบและบันทึกไว้ในทุกรอบข้อมูล เพื่อให้ตรวจย้อนหลังได้', en: 'Every threshold is a setting of the system and is recorded with each release, so results can be checked later.' },
  'about.privacy.h': { th: 'ความเป็นส่วนตัวและลิขสิทธิ์', en: 'Privacy and copyright' },
  'about.privacy.body': { th: 'Atlas แสดงผลระดับหัวข้อ วารสาร และสถาบันเท่านั้น ไม่จัดอันดับหรือแสดงข้อมูลบุคคล (PDPA) ไม่เผยแพร่บทคัดย่อเพราะเป็นลิขสิทธิ์ของวารสาร ชื่อเรื่องบทความลิงก์กลับไปยัง ThaiJO และเว็บไซต์ไม่เรียกใช้บริการภายนอกหรือ AI ใด ๆ ขณะเปิดดู', en: 'The atlas shows topics, journals and institutions only; it never ranks or shows individuals (PDPA). Abstracts are not republished because they belong to the journals; article titles link back to ThaiJO, and the site calls no outside service or AI while you browse.' },
  'about.log.h': { th: 'ประวัติการเปลี่ยนนิยาม', en: 'Definition changelog' },
  'about.log.intro': { th: 'ทุกครั้งที่นิยามเปลี่ยน เลขฉบับจะเพิ่มขึ้น ตัวเลขต่างฉบับจึงไม่ควรนำมาเทียบกันโดยตรง', en: 'Each change to the definition raises its version number, so figures from different versions should not be compared directly.' },
  'about.log.version': { th: 'ฉบับที่ {v} · {date}', en: 'Version {v} · {date}' },
})
```

```ts
import { about } from './about'
import { atlas } from './atlas'
import { common } from './common'
import { compare } from './compare'
import { data } from './data'
import { emerging } from './emerging'
import { overview } from './overview'
import { topic } from './topic'
import { ui } from './ui'

/** Every string file, so the parity test can detect a key defined twice. */
export const PARTS = { common, ui, overview, emerging, topic, atlas, compare, data, about } as const

export const STR = { ...common, ...ui, ...overview, ...emerging, ...topic, ...atlas, ...compare, ...data, ...about }

export type StrKey = keyof typeof STR
```
