- [ ] **Step 5: The pages** — replace `web/src/pages/DataPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { DataTable, type Column } from '../components/DataTable'
import { OnThisPage } from '../components/OnThisPage'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { DICTIONARY, type DictEntry } from '../content/dictionary'
import { groupName, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import { dataUrl } from '../data/source'
import type { EmergingKeyword, Topic } from '../data/types'
import { toCsv, downloadText } from '../lib/csv'
import { fmtWindow, windows } from '../lib/definition'
import { ciPct, DASH, fmtDate, fmtInt, fmtNum, fmtPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import type { StrKey } from '../strings'

export default function DataPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return <RequireRelease title={t('data.title')}>{(release) => <OpenData release={release} />}</RequireRelease>
}

/** The release files as loaded (used to download from the offline file, where there is no data folder). */
function releaseFiles(r: AtlasRelease): Record<string, unknown> {
  const v = r.manifest.schema_version
  return {
    'manifest.json': r.manifest,
    'overview.json': r.overview,
    'taxonomy.json': r.taxonomy,
    'topics.json': { schema_version: v, topics: r.topics },
    'series.json': r.series,
    'edges.json': { schema_version: v, edges: r.edges },
    ...(r.keywords ? { 'keywords.json': { schema_version: v, keywords: r.keywords } } : {}),
  }
}

const FILE_INFO: Record<string, StrKey> = {
  'manifest.json': 'data.file.manifest',
  'overview.json': 'data.file.overview',
  'taxonomy.json': 'data.file.taxonomy',
  'topics.json': 'data.file.topics',
  'series.json': 'data.file.series',
  'edges.json': 'data.file.edges',
  'keywords.json': 'data.file.keywords',
}

const COMPLETENESS: Record<string, StrKey> = {
  median: 'data.q.method.median',
  configured: 'data.q.method.configured',
}

const QUALITY_FLAGS: Record<string, StrKey> = {
  thai_pua_repaired: 'data.flag.thai_pua_repaired',
  tone_marks_missing: 'data.flag.tone_marks_missing',
  stat_symbols_missing: 'data.flag.stat_symbols_missing',
  decode_replaced: 'data.flag.decode_replaced',
}

function OpenData({ release }: { release: AtlasRelease }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  const rel = release.overview.release
  const files = releaseFiles(release)
  const sizes = new Map((release.manifest.files ?? []).map((f) => [f.path, f.bytes]))
  const ov = release.overview
  const dq = ov.data_quality
  const w = windows(ov.last_complete_year)
  const llmFallbacks = (release.manifest.llm ?? []).reduce((n, s) => n + s.fallback, 0)
  const labels = release.manifest.labels
  const journalDriven = release.topics.filter((tp) => tp.metrics.journal_driven).length

  const topicCsv = () =>
    toCsv<Topic>(release.topics, [
      { header: 'id', value: (r) => r.id },
      { header: 'name_th', value: (r) => r.th },
      { header: 'name_en', value: (r) => r.en },
      { header: 'group', value: (r) => r.group },
      { header: 'group_name', value: (r) => groupName(release, r.group, lang) },
      { header: 'subfield', value: (r) => r.subfield ?? '' },
      { header: 'status', value: (r) => r.status },
      { header: 'score', value: (r) => r.score },
      { header: 'count_recent', value: (r) => r.metrics.count_recent },
      { header: 'share_recent', value: (r) => r.metrics.share_recent },
      { header: 'share_baseline', value: (r) => r.metrics.share_baseline },
      { header: 'share_ratio', value: (r) => r.metrics.share_ratio },
      { header: 'no_baseline', value: (r) => r.metrics.no_baseline ?? false },
      { header: 'share_growth', value: (r) => r.metrics.share_growth },
      { header: 'share_growth_lo', value: (r) => r.metrics.share_growth_lo },
      { header: 'share_growth_hi', value: (r) => r.metrics.share_growth_hi },
      { header: 'n_journals_R', value: (r) => r.metrics.n_journals_R },
      { header: 'n_orgs_R', value: (r) => r.metrics.n_orgs_R },
      { header: 'journal_driven', value: (r) => r.metrics.journal_driven },
      { header: 'orgs_unverified', value: (r) => r.metrics.orgs_unverified ?? false },
      { header: 'low_data_reason', value: (r) => r.metrics.low_data_reason ?? '' },
      { header: 'forecast_shown', value: (r) => r.forecast?.show ?? false },
      { header: 'cross_field', value: (r) => r.cross_field ?? false },
    ])
  const seriesRows = release.topics.flatMap((tp) => (release.series.topics[tp.id] ?? []).map((count, i) => ({ id: tp.id, year: release.series.years[i], count })))
  const seriesCsv = () =>
    toCsv(seriesRows, [
      { header: 'topic_id', value: (r) => r.id },
      { header: 'year', value: (r) => r.year },
      { header: 'articles', value: (r) => r.count },
    ])
  const keywordCsv = (rows: readonly EmergingKeyword[]) =>
    toCsv(rows, [
      { header: 'term', value: (r) => r.term },
      { header: 'count_recent', value: (r) => r.count_recent },
      { header: 'share_ratio', value: (r) => r.share_ratio },
      { header: 'no_baseline', value: (r) => r.no_baseline ?? false },
      { header: 'growth', value: (r) => r.growth },
      { header: 'growth_lo', value: (r) => r.growth_lo },
      { header: 'n_active_years', value: (r) => r.n_active_years },
      { header: 'n_orgs_R', value: (r) => r.n_orgs_R },
      { header: 'n_journals_R', value: (r) => r.n_journals_R },
      { header: 'journal_driven', value: (r) => r.journal_driven },
      { header: 'emerging', value: (r) => r.emerging },
      { header: 'topic', value: (r) => r.topic ?? '' },
    ])

  const quality: { label: string; value: string; note?: string }[] = [
    { label: t('data.q.through'), value: String(ov.last_complete_year), note: (release.manifest.trimmed_years ?? []).length ? t('data.q.trimmed', { years: (release.manifest.trimmed_years ?? []).join(', ') }) : undefined },
    { label: t('data.q.completeness'), value: dq.completeness_method, note: COMPLETENESS[dq.completeness_method] ? t(COMPLETENESS[dq.completeness_method]) : undefined },
    { label: t('data.q.windows'), value: t('data.q.windowsV', { r: fmtWindow(w.R), b: fmtWindow(w.B), w: fmtWindow(w.W) }) },
    { label: t('data.q.yearMissing'), value: fmtInt(dq.year_missing) },
    { label: t('data.q.unassigned'), value: fmtInt(dq.unassigned_articles), note: ov.kpis.articles ? t('data.q.ofArticles', { p: fmtPct(dq.unassigned_articles / ov.kpis.articles, 1) }) : undefined },
    ...Object.entries(dq.text_quality ?? {}).map(([flag, n]) => ({ label: QUALITY_FLAGS[flag] ? t(QUALITY_FLAGS[flag]) : flag, value: fmtInt(n) })),
    { label: t('data.q.ingestErrors'), value: fmtInt(release.manifest.ingest_errors ?? 0) },
    { label: t('data.q.journalDriven'), value: fmtInt(journalDriven) },
    { label: t('data.q.orgsUnverified'), value: fmtInt(release.manifest.n_orgs_unverified ?? 0) },
    ...(labels ? [{ label: t('data.q.labels'), value: t('data.q.labelsV', { llm: fmtInt(labels.llm), kw: fmtInt(labels.keywords), ed: fmtInt(labels.overrides) }) }] : []),
    { label: t('data.q.llmFallback'), value: fmtInt(llmFallbacks) },
    { label: t('data.q.embedding'), value: `${release.manifest.embedding_provider || DASH} · ${release.manifest.embedding_model}` },
    {
      label: t('data.q.taxonomy'),
      value: `${release.taxonomy.source}${release.taxonomy.version ? ` ${release.taxonomy.version}` : ''}`,
      note: release.taxonomy.holdout_accuracy_field != null ? t('data.q.taxonomyAcc', { f: fmtPct(release.taxonomy.holdout_accuracy_field, 0), s: fmtPct(release.taxonomy.holdout_accuracy_subfield ?? null, 0) }) : undefined,
    },
    { label: t('data.q.definition'), value: t('data.q.definitionV', { v: release.definitionVersion, level: ciPct(release.ciLevel) }) },
    { label: t('data.q.build'), value: `${release.manifest.pipeline_version} · ${release.manifest.config_hash}`, note: fmtDate(release.manifest.generated_at, lang) },
  ]

  const corpusRows = [
    ...ov.corpus_by_year.map((r) => ({ year: r.year, articles: r.articles, kind: 'actual' as const })),
    ...(ov.nowcast ? [{ year: ov.nowcast.year, articles: ov.nowcast.mean, kind: 'nowcast' as const }] : []),
    ...(ov.corpus_forecast ?? []).filter((r) => r.year !== ov.nowcast?.year).map((r) => ({ year: r.year, articles: r.articles, kind: 'forecast' as const })),
  ]
  const corpusColumns: Column<(typeof corpusRows)[number]>[] = [
    { key: 'year', header: t('col.year'), rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => (r.kind === 'actual' ? String(r.year) : t(r.kind === 'nowcast' ? 'data.corpus.nowcast' : 'data.corpus.forecast', { year: r.year })), csv: (r) => r.year },
    { key: 'kind', header: 'kind', cell: () => null, csv: (r) => r.kind, csvOnly: true },
    { key: 'articles', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtInt(r.articles), csv: (r) => r.articles },
  ]
  const dictColumns: Column<DictEntry>[] = [
    { key: 'file', header: t('data.dict.file'), rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => <code>{r.file}</code>, sortValue: (r) => r.file },
    { key: 'field', header: t('data.dict.field'), cell: (r) => <code>{r.field}</code>, csv: (r) => r.field },
    { key: 'meaning', header: t('data.dict.meaning'), cell: (r) => (lang === 'en' ? r.en : r.th), csv: (r) => (lang === 'en' ? r.en : r.th) },
  ]
  const toc = [
    { id: 'downloads', label: t('data.dl.h') },
    { id: 'csv', label: t('data.csv.h') },
    { id: 'quality', label: t('data.q.h') },
    { id: 'dictionary', label: t('data.dict.h') },
    { id: 'cite', label: t('data.cite.h') },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('data.title')} lede={t('data.lede', { release: rel })} />
      <div className="layout">
        <div className="main-col">
          <Section id="downloads" title={t('data.dl.h')} intro={t(release.source === 'http' ? 'data.dl.introHttp' : 'data.dl.introOffline')}>
            <ul className="dl-list">
              {Object.entries(files).map(([path, value]) => (
                <li key={path}>
                  <div>
                    <code>{path}</code>
                    <span className="dl-note">{FILE_INFO[path] ? t(FILE_INFO[path]) : ''}{sizes.has(path) ? ` · ${fmtNum((sizes.get(path) ?? 0) / 1024, 0)} KB` : ''}</span>
                  </div>
                  {release.source === 'http' ? (
                    <a className="btn-secondary" href={dataUrl(import.meta.env.BASE_URL, `${rel}/${path}`)} download={`atlas-${rel}-${path}`}>
                      {t('data.dl.json')}
                    </a>
                  ) : (
                    <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => downloadText(`atlas-${rel}-${path}`, JSON.stringify(value), 'application/json')}>
                      {t('data.dl.json')}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </Section>
          <Section id="csv" title={t('data.csv.h')} intro={t('data.csv.intro')}>
            <ul className="dl-list">
              <li>
                <div><code>topics.csv</code><span className="dl-note">{t('data.csv.topics', { n: fmtInt(release.topics.length) })}</span></div>
                <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" disabled={!release.topics.length} onClick={() => downloadText(`atlas-${rel}-topics.csv`, topicCsv())}>{t('csv.download')}</button>
              </li>
              <li>
                <div><code>series.csv</code><span className="dl-note">{t('data.csv.series')}</span></div>
                <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" disabled={!seriesRows.length} onClick={() => downloadText(`atlas-${rel}-series.csv`, seriesCsv())}>{t('csv.download')}</button>
              </li>
              {release.keywords?.length ? (
                <li>
                  <div><code>keywords.csv</code><span className="dl-note">{t('data.csv.keywords')}</span></div>
                  <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => downloadText(`atlas-${rel}-keywords.csv`, keywordCsv(release.keywords ?? []))}>{t('csv.download')}</button>
                </li>
              ) : null}
            </ul>
          </Section>
          <Section id="quality" title={t('data.q.h')} intro={t('data.q.intro')}>
            <dl className="summary">
              {quality.map((q) => (
                <div key={q.label}>
                  <dt>{q.label}</dt>
                  <dd>{q.value}{q.note ? <small>{q.note}</small> : null}</dd>
                </div>
              ))}
            </dl>
            {corpusRows.length ? (
              <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 1, title: t('data.corpus.table') })} rows={corpusRows} columns={corpusColumns} rowKey={(r) => `${r.kind}-${r.year}`} stack={false} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-corpus-by-year.csv`} />
            ) : null}
          </Section>
          <Section id="dictionary" title={t('data.dict.h')} intro={t('data.dict.intro')}>
            <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 2, title: t('data.dict.table') })} rows={DICTIONARY} columns={dictColumns} rowKey={(r) => `${r.file}:${r.field}`} />
          </Section>
          <Section id="cite" title={t('data.cite.h')}>
            <p className="cite">{t('data.cite.text', { release: rel, date: fmtDate(release.manifest.generated_at, lang) })}</p>
            <p className="note">{t('data.cite.licence')}</p>
          </Section>
        </div>
        <aside className="rail" aria-label={t('onThisPage')}>
          <OnThisPage items={toc} />
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Replace `web/src/pages/AboutPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { BRAND } from '../brand/brand'
import { OnThisPage } from '../components/OnThisPage'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { StatusGlyph } from '../components/StatusGlyph'
import { CHANGELOG } from '../content/changelog'
import { METHOD } from '../content/method'
import { useRelease } from '../data/atlasContext'
import { DEFINITION, fmtWindow, windows } from '../lib/definition'
import { ciPct, fmtNum, fmtSignedPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { STATUSES } from '../lib/status'
import { statusName } from '../lib/topicText'

/** Plain-language method, the status definition, the forecast rule, privacy and the definition changelog. Works without data. */
export default function AboutPage() {
  const { t, lang, pick } = useI18n()
  const release = useRelease()
  const version = release?.definitionVersion ?? Math.max(...CHANGELOG.map((c) => c.version))
  const level = release ? ciPct(release.ciLevel) : 90
  const w = release ? windows(release.overview.last_complete_year) : null
  const vars: Record<string, string> = w
    ? { R: fmtWindow(w.R), B: fmtWindow(w.B), W: fmtWindow(w.W) }
    : { R: t('about.generic.R'), B: t('about.generic.B'), W: t('about.generic.W') }
  const fill = (s: string) => s.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (m, k: string) => vars[k] ?? m)
  const ruleVars = {
    level,
    bar: fmtSignedPct(DEFINITION.emergingMinGrowthLo, 0),
    ratio: fmtNum(DEFINITION.emergingMinShareRatio, 1),
    dratio: fmtNum(DEFINITION.decliningMaxShareRatio, 1),
    j: DEFINITION.minJournals,
    o: DEFINITION.minOrgs,
    n: DEFINITION.minArticlesRecent,
    y: DEFINITION.recentYears,
  }
  const changes = CHANGELOG.filter((c) => c.version <= version).sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version)
  const toc = [
    ...METHOD.map((m) => ({ id: `m-${m.id}`, label: pick(m.h.th, m.h.en) })),
    { id: 'definition', label: t('about.def.h') },
    { id: 'privacy', label: t('about.privacy.h') },
    { id: 'changelog', label: t('about.log.h') },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('about.title')} lede={t('about.lede')} />
      <div className="layout">
        <div className="main-col prose">
          {METHOD.map((m) => (
            <Section key={m.id} id={`m-${m.id}`} title={pick(m.h.th, m.h.en)}>
              {m.body.map((p, i) => (
                <p key={i}>{fill(lang === 'en' ? p.en : p.th)}</p>
              ))}
            </Section>
          ))}
          <Section id="definition" title={t('about.def.h')} intro={t('about.def.intro', { v: version, level })}>
            <dl className="defs">
              {STATUSES.map((st) => (
                <div key={st}>
                  <dt>
                    <StatusGlyph status={st} size={16} />
                    <span>{statusName(t, st)}</span>
                    <code>{st}</code>
                  </dt>
                  <dd>{t(`rule.${st}` as const, ruleVars)}</dd>
                </div>
              ))}
            </dl>
            <p className="note">{t('about.def.order')}</p>
          </Section>
          <Section id="privacy" title={t('about.privacy.h')}>
            <p>{t('about.privacy.body')}</p>
            {BRAND.temporary ? <p className="note">{t('wordmarkNote')}</p> : null}
          </Section>
          <Section id="changelog" title={t('about.log.h')} intro={t('about.log.intro')}>
            <ol className="changelog">
              {changes.map((c) => (
                <li key={c.version}>
                  <p className="log-h">{t('about.log.version', { v: c.version, date: c.date })}</p>
                  <p>{lang === 'en' ? c.en : c.th}</p>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          </Section>
        </div>
        <aside className="rail" aria-label={t('onThisPage')}>
          <OnThisPage items={toc} />

    </div>

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Offline build** — replace `web/vite.config.ts`:

```ts
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { createReadStream, existsSync, statSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve, sep } from 'node:path'
import { defineConfig, type Plugin } from 'vite'
import { viteSingleFile } from 'vite-plugin-singlefile'

/** `npm run dev` serves /data/* from fixtures (or ATLAS_DATA_DIR) — the built site never contains data. */
function atlasDevData(dir: string): Plugin {
  const root = resolve(dir)
  return {
    name: 'atlas-dev-data',
    apply: 'serve',
    configureServer(server) {
      server.middlewares.use('/data', (req, res) => {
        const rel = decodeURIComponent((req.url ?? '/').split('?')[0])
        const file = resolve(root, `.${rel}`)
        if (!file.startsWith(root + sep) || !existsSync(file) || !statSync(file).isFile()) {
          res.statusCode = 404
          res.end('not found')
          return
        }
        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8')
        createReadStream(file).pipe(res)
      })
    },
  }
}

// base './' — the site works from any sub-path (GitHub Pages) and data is fetched relative to it.
// `vite build --mode offline` makes the single-file edition (dist-offline/index.html → dist/offline-template.html).
export default defineConfig(({ mode }) => {
  const offline = mode === 'offline'
  return {
    base: './',
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss(), atlasDevData(process.env.ATLAS_DATA_DIR ?? 'fixtures/example/data'), ...(offline ? [viteSingleFile({ removeViteModuleLoader: true })] : [])],
    build: { outDir: offline ? 'dist-offline' : 'dist', emptyOutDir: true },
    server: { port: 3000 },
  }
})
```

Create `web/scripts/finish-offline.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// After `vite build --mode offline`: check the single-file build against the `atlas publish` contract
// (exactly one empty atlas-data slot, everything inlined, nothing fetched from the network),
// then move it to dist/offline-template.html.
import { readFileSync, rmSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

const web = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..')
const src = join(web, 'dist-offline', 'index.html')
const out = join(web, 'dist', 'offline-template.html')
const html = readFileSync(src, 'utf8')
const OPEN = '<script type="application/json" id="atlas-data">'
const fail = (msg) => {
  console.error(`[offline] ${msg}`)
  process.exit(1)
}
if (html.split(OPEN).length !== 2) fail(`expected exactly one ${OPEN}`)
if (!html.includes(`${OPEN}null</script>`)) fail('the atlas-data slot must contain null')
const remote = html.match(/\b(?:src|href)\s*=\s*["'](?:https?:)?\/\/[^"']+/gi)
if (remote) fail(`external references: ${remote.join(', ')}`)
if (/<script[^>]+\bsrc\s*=/i.test(html) || /<link[^>]+rel=["']?stylesheet/i.test(html)) fail('scripts and styles must be inlined')
writeFileSync(out, html)
rmSync(join(web, 'dist-offline'), { recursive: true, force: true })
console.log(`[offline] ${out} (${Math.round(statSync(out).size / 1024)} KB)`)
```

```ts
import { about } from './about'
import { atlas } from './atlas'
import { brief } from './brief'
import { common } from './common'
import { compare } from './compare'
import { data } from './data'
import { emerging } from './emerging'
import { overview } from './overview'
import { topic } from './topic'
import { ui } from './ui'

/** Every string file, so the parity test can detect a key defined twice. */
export const PARTS = { common, ui, overview, emerging, topic, atlas, compare, data, about, brief } as const

export const STR = { ...common, ...ui, ...overview, ...emerging, ...topic, ...atlas, ...compare, ...data, ...about, ...brief }

export type StrKey = keyof typeof STR
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The brief page** — replace `web/src/pages/BriefPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Wordmark } from '../brand/Wordmark'
import { DataBanner } from '../components/DataBanner'
import { SegmentText } from '../components/SegmentText'
import { StatusGlyph } from '../components/StatusGlyph'
import { useAtlas } from '../data/atlasContext'
import type { AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { Kpis, Topic } from '../data/types'
import { DEFINITION } from '../lib/definition'
import { ciPct, fmtDate, fmtInt, fmtNum, fmtPct, fmtSignedPct, kpiDelta } from '../lib/format'
import { segmentHeadline } from '../lib/highlight'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { sortBy } from '../lib/sort'
import { STATUSES, type SignalStatus } from '../lib/status'
import { statusName } from '../lib/topicText'
import { useDocumentTitle } from '../lib/useDocumentTitle'
import type { StrKey } from '../strings'

const KPIS: readonly { key: keyof Kpis; label: StrKey; unit: 'count' | 'pct' }[] = [
  { key: 'articles', label: 'kpi.articles', unit: 'count' },
  { key: 'topics', label: 'kpi.topics', unit: 'count' },
  { key: 'journals', label: 'kpi.journals', unit: 'count' },
  { key: 'institutions', label: 'kpi.institutions', unit: 'count' },
  { key: 'pct_abstract_en', label: 'kpi.abstractEn', unit: 'pct' },
]
const TOP = 5

/** E4: one- to two-page A4 executive brief (printed to PDF by `npm run brief:pdf`); every number comes from the release. */
export default function BriefPage() {
  const state = useAtlas()
  const { t } = useI18n()
  useDocumentTitle(t('nav.brief'))
  if (state.status === 'ready') return <Brief release={state.release} />
  return (
    <main id="main" className="brief">
      <h1>{t('nav.brief')}</h1>
      {state.status === 'loading' ? <p className="loading" role="status">{t('loading')}</p> : <DataBanner />}
    </main>
  )
}

function Brief({ release }: { release: AtlasRelease }) {
  const { t, lang, name } = useI18n()
  const ov = release.overview
  const level = ciPct(release.ciLevel)
  const brief = ov.brief
  const headline = brief ? (lang === 'en' ? brief.headline_en : brief.headline_th) : ''
  const bullets = brief ? ((lang === 'en' ? brief.bullets_en : brief.bullets_th) ?? []) : []
  const prev = release.previousOverview
  const top = (st: SignalStatus): Topic[] =>
    sortBy(release.topics.filter((tp) => tp.status === st && tp.metrics.share_growth != null), (tp) => tp.metrics.share_growth, st === 'DECLINING' ? 'asc' : 'desc').slice(0, TOP)
  const tables = (['EMERGING', 'HOT', 'DECLINING'] as const).map((st) => ({ st, rows: top(st) })).filter((g) => g.rows.length > 0)
  const watch = ov.status_counts.WATCH ?? 0
  return (
    <main id="main" className="brief" data-brief-ready="true">
      <header className="brief-head">
        <Wordmark sub={t('brandSub')} lang={lang} />
        <p className="brief-meta">{t('brief.sub', { year: ov.last_complete_year, date: fmtDate(ov.generated_at, lang) })}</p>
      </header>
      <h1>{t('brief.title', { release: ov.release })}</h1>
      {release.sample ? (
        <p className="brief-sample">
          <strong>{t('sample.tag')}</strong> {t('sample.text')}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {headline ? (
        <p className="headline brief-headline">
          <SegmentText segments={segmentHeadline(headline, release.topics)} />
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <section aria-labelledby="brief-kpi">
        <h2 id="brief-kpi">{t('brief.kpi')}</h2>
        <table className="t brief-t">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th scope="col">{t('brief.kpi')}</th>
              <th scope="col" className="num">{ov.release}</th>
              {prev ? <th scope="col" className="num">{t('delta', { prev: prev.release })}</th> : null}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {KPIS.map((k) => (
              <tr key={k.key}>
                <th scope="row">{t(k.label)}</th>
                <td className="num">{k.unit === 'pct' ? `${fmtNum(ov.kpis[k.key], 1)}%` : fmtInt(ov.kpis[k.key])}</td>
                {prev ? <td className="num">{kpiDelta(ov.kpis[k.key], prev.kpis[k.key], k.unit, lang) ?? ''}</td> : null}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </section>
      {bullets.length ? (
        <section aria-labelledby="brief-points">
          <h2 id="brief-points">{t('brief.points')}</h2>
          <ul className="brief-points">
            {bullets.map((b, i) => (
              <li key={i}><SegmentText segments={segmentHeadline(b, release.topics, { figures: false })} mark={false} /></li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      ) : null}
      <section aria-labelledby="brief-status">
        <h2 id="brief-status">{t('brief.status')}</h2>
        <p className="brief-counts">
          {STATUSES.map((st) => (
            <span key={st}>
              <StatusGlyph status={st} size={12} /> {statusName(t, st)} {fmtInt(ov.status_counts[st] ?? 0)}
            </span>
          ))}
        </p>
        {watch > 0 ? <p className="note">{t('brief.watch', { n: fmtInt(watch) })}</p> : null}
        {tables.map(({ st, rows }) => (
          <table key={st} className="t brief-t">
            <caption>
              <StatusGlyph status={st} size={12} /> {t('brief.top', { status: statusName(t, st), n: rows.length })}
            </caption>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th scope="col">{t('col.topic')}</th>
                <th scope="col" className="num">{t('col.share')}</th>
                <th scope="col" className="num">{t('col.growth')}</th>
                <th scope="col" className="num">{t('ciShort', { level })}</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map((tp) => (
                <tr key={tp.id}>
                  <th scope="row">{name(tp)}</th>
                  <td className="num">{fmtPct(tp.metrics.share_recent, 2)}</td>
                  <td className="num">{fmtSignedPct(tp.metrics.share_growth)}</td>
                  <td className="num">{fmtSignedPct(tp.metrics.share_growth_lo, 0)} {t('to')} {fmtSignedPct(tp.metrics.share_growth_hi, 0)}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        ))}
      </section>
      <footer className="brief-foot">
        <p>{t('brief.def', { v: release.definitionVersion, level, bar: fmtSignedPct(DEFINITION.emergingMinGrowthLo, 0) })}</p>
        <p>{t('source.line', { release: ov.release, v: release.definitionVersion })}</p>
        <p>{t('brief.more')} · {t('footer.licence')}</p>
      </footer>
      <p className="no-print mt-6">
        <button type="button" className="btn" onClick={() => window.print()}>{t('brief.print')}</button>
      </p>
    </main>
  )
}
```

```css
/* /brief on screen, and print rules for the whole site (the PDF brief, printed pages). */
@layer components {
  .brief { max-width: 900px; margin-inline: auto; padding: 28px 24px 40px; }
  .brief h1 { font-size: 1.75rem; line-height: 1.35; margin-top: 18px; }
  .brief h2 { font-size: 1.1875rem; margin-top: 22px; }
  .brief-head { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 12px 24px; padding: 12px 16px; background: var(--brand); color: var(--brand-ink); border-bottom: 6px solid var(--link); }
  .brief-meta { font-size: 0.875rem; color: var(--brand-ink-2); }
  .brief-headline { margin-top: 14px; font-size: 1.5rem; max-width: none; }
  .brief-sample { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 12px; border: 2px solid var(--st-WATCH); background: var(--tag-WATCH-bg); color: var(--tag-WATCH-ink); }
  .brief-t { margin-top: 10px; font-size: 0.9375rem; }
  .brief-t caption { text-align: left; }
  .brief-points { margin: 8px 0 0; padding-left: 1.2em; display: grid; gap: 6px; }
  .brief-counts { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 4px 16px; margin-top: 6px; font-size: 0.9375rem; }
  .brief-counts span { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; }
  .brief-foot { margin-top: 24px; padding-top: 10px; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); font-size: 0.8125rem; color: var(--ink-2); display: grid; gap: 4px; }
}

@media print {
  @page { size: A4; margin: 14mm 14mm 16mm; }
  /* Always print with the light tokens, whatever theme is on screen. */
  html:root:root {
    color-scheme: light;
    --bg: #ffffff; --surface: #f3f4f5; --ink: #0b0c0e; --ink-2: #474e55; --border: #b1b4b6; --border-2: #dcdee0;
    --brand: #0f3557; --brand-ink: #ffffff; --brand-ink-2: #c9d7e4; --link: #1a5bb0; --hl: #ffe07a; --up: #00703c;
    --st-EMERGING: #13a08a; --st-WATCH: #c47a0c; --st-HOT: #a3124f; --st-DECLINING: #5b74cf; --st-STABLE: #6f777b; --st-LOW_DATA: #6f777b;
  }
  body { font-size: 10pt; background: #ffffff; }
  .no-print, .skip, .nav, .phase, .ftr, .tbl-tools, .zoom, .tablist, .rail { display: none !important; }
  mark { animation: none !important; background-size: 100% 100% !important; }
  .brief { max-width: none; padding: 0; }
  .brief h1 { font-size: 17pt; margin-top: 10px; }
  .brief h2 { font-size: 12pt; margin-top: 12px; }
  .brief-headline { font-size: 15pt; margin-top: 8px; }
  .brief-t { margin-top: 6px; font-size: 9.5pt; }
  .brief-t th, .brief-t td { padding: 3px 10px 3px 0; }
  .brief-t caption { padding-bottom: 2px; font-size: 10pt; }
  .brief-foot { margin-top: 12px; }
  .brief-head, mark, .chip { -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact; }
  table, figure, .fr-group, .point { break-inside: avoid; }
  h1, h2, h3 { break-after: avoid; }
  a { color: inherit; text-decoration: none; }
  .tabpanel { border: 0; padding: 0; }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: PDF script** — create `web/scripts/brief-pdf.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Print the executive brief (#/brief) to A4 PDFs in Thai and English with Playwright's Chromium.
//   npm run build && npm run brief:pdf -- [--site dist] [--data fixtures/example/data] [--out dist]
// Without --data the site's own data/ folder is used (after `uv run atlas publish --site dist`).
// The page uses the site's self-hosted Sarabun, so the Thai fonts are embedded in the PDF.
/* global document */
import { chromium } from '@playwright/test'
import { mkdirSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { startServer } from './serve.mjs'

const args = {}
for (let i = 2; i < process.argv.length; i += 2) args[process.argv[i].replace(/^--/, '')] = process.argv[i + 1]
const site = args.site ?? 'dist'
const out = args.out ?? site
mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true })
const server = await startServer({ site, data: args.data ?? null, port: Number(args.port ?? 4180) })
const { port } = server.address()
const browser = await chromium.launch()
try {
  for (const lang of ['th', 'en']) {
    const page = await browser.newPage()
    await page.emulateMedia({ media: 'print', colorScheme: 'light', reducedMotion: 'reduce' })
    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/#/brief?lang=${lang}`)
    await page.waitForSelector('[data-brief-ready="true"]', { timeout: 20_000 })
    await page.evaluate(async () => {
      await document.fonts.ready
      return true
    })
    const file = join(out, `brief-${lang}.pdf`)
    await page.pdf({ path: file, format: 'A4', printBackground: true, preferCSSPageSize: true })
    console.log(`[brief] ${file}`)
    await page.close()
  }
} finally {
  await browser.close()
  server.close()
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Web README** — write `web/README.md`:

````markdown
# TCI Research Atlas — เว็บ v2

เว็บ static (React 19 + Vite 7 + TypeScript + Tailwind 4) ที่อ่านข้อมูลรอบล่าสุด **ตอนเปิดหน้า** — ไม่มี server ไม่มี API key และไม่เรียก AI หรือบริการภายนอกใด ๆ
ดีไซน์อยู่ใน `../DESIGN.md` (Civic modern เป็นฐาน · ประโยคหัวเรื่องแบบ Editorial + forest plot ในหน้าภาพรวม · แผนที่ภูมิประเทศแบบ Cartographic ในหน้าแผนที่) · แบรนด์อยู่ที่ `src/brand/` จุดเดียว

## คำสั่ง

| คำสั่ง | ทำอะไร |
|---|---|
| `npm ci` | ติดตั้ง (ทุกเวอร์ชันล็อกไว้ใน `package-lock.json`) |
| `npm run dev` | dev server http://localhost:3000 อ่านข้อมูลตัวอย่างจาก `fixtures/example/data` (เปลี่ยนด้วย env `ATLAS_DATA_DIR`) |
| `npm run build` | `dist/` (วางบน static host ใดก็ได้) + `dist/offline-template.html` (ต้นแบบไฟล์เดียวให้ `atlas publish`) |
| `npm test` | unit tests (vitest) |
| `npm run e2e` | Playwright smoke: ทุกหน้า × ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง/ข้อมูลว่าง · axe · จอ 360 px · ไฟล์ออฟไลน์ไม่มี network (ต้อง build ก่อน; ครั้งแรกรัน `npx playwright install chromium`) |
| `npm run lint` · `npx tsc -b` | ตรวจโค้ด |
| `npm run fixtures` | สร้างข้อมูลทดสอบ (สังเคราะห์ ไม่ใช่ข้อมูลจริง) ใหม่ — CI ตรวจว่าไม่ drift |
| `npm run gen:types` | สร้าง `src/data/contract.ts` จาก `contract.schema.json` (หลัง `uv run atlas schema` ใน `pipeline/`) |
| `npm run brief:pdf -- [--data <โฟลเดอร์ data>] [--out <โฟลเดอร์>]` | พิมพ์หน้า `#/brief` เป็น `brief-th.pdf` และ `brief-en.pdf` (ฟอนต์ไทยฝังในไฟล์) |
| `npm run vendor:fonts` | คัดลอก Sarabun (woff2, OFL) เข้า `src/assets/fonts/` |

## ข้อมูลเข้าเว็บอย่างไร

```
web: npm run build ──► dist/  (+ offline-template.html)
pipeline: uv run atlas publish -c config.yaml --site ../web/dist
          ──► dist/data/<release>/*.json · dist/data/latest.json · dist/atlas-offline.html
```

- เว็บอ่าน `./data/latest.json` → `./data/<release>/manifest.json` → ไฟล์หลัก (path สัมพัทธ์จาก `BASE_URL` จึงวางใต้ sub-path ได้ เช่น GitHub Pages)
- **อัปเดตรายไตรมาส = คัดลอกโฟลเดอร์ `data/` ใหม่ขึ้น host** — ไม่ต้อง build เว็บใหม่ ไม่ต้องมี Node บนเครื่องผู้รัน
- `atlas-offline.html` = เว็บทั้งหมดในไฟล์เดียว เปิดจาก `file://` ได้โดยไม่ต่อเน็ต (ข้อมูลฝังใน `<script type="application/json" id="atlas-data">`)
- โหลดข้อมูลไม่ได้ / schema ไม่ใช่ 2.x / ไฟล์เสีย → แถบแจ้งเหตุผล (ไม่มีหน้าขาว) · รอบที่ `embedding_provider` เป็น `fixture` หรือ `lite` → แถบ "ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง"

## หน้า

`#/overview` ภาพรวม (KPI + Δ, ประโยคสรุป, ประเด็นผู้บริหาร, สถานะ + forest plot, ผัง size × growth) · `#/atlas` แผนที่ + ดัชนีหัวข้อ · `#/topics/:id` หัวข้อ · `#/emerging` ตารางสถานะ · `#/compare` เปรียบเทียบ 2–4 หัวข้อ · `#/data` ข้อมูลเปิด + ความโปร่งใส · `#/about` วิธีการ + นิยาม + changelog · `#/brief` หน้าพิมพ์ PDF
สถานะของตัวกรอง/การเลือกอยู่ใน URL เสมอ (เช่น `#/emerging?status=WATCH&sort=journals`) แชร์ลิงก์ได้

## โครงสร้างโค้ด

```
src/brand/       wordmark ชั่วคราว (สลับเป็นโลโก้ทางการที่ brand.ts จุดเดียว)
src/styles/      tokens.css (สีทั้งหมด light/dark) · fonts.css · CSS ของแต่ละส่วน · palette.test.ts (CVD + contrast)
src/strings/     ข้อความ UI ทุกตัว ไทย + อังกฤษ (i18n.test.ts ตรวจครบคู่)
src/data/        contract.ts (generated) · types.ts · source.ts (Http | Embedded) · release.ts (loader) · atlasContext.ts
src/lib/         format · highlight · urlState · csv · search (ตัดวรรณยุกต์ + Intl.Segmenter) · definition · theme · i18n
src/components/  ชุด UI (tabs, figure, table, whisker, search, tooltip, …)
src/charts/      forest plot · portfolio · trend · multi-line
src/features/atlas/  แผนที่ (mapSpace, terrain, TerrainMap, Gazetteer)
src/pages/       หน้าละไฟล์
fixtures/        รอบข้อมูลสังเคราะห์สำหรับ dev/test (example + empty) — ไม่ใช่ข้อมูลจริง
e2e/smoke.spec.ts  Playwright smoke spec เดียว
```

## กฎสำคัญ (ฉบับเต็มใน `../CLAUDE.md` §6 และ `../DESIGN.md`)

- สีมาจาก token ใน `src/styles/tokens.css` เท่านั้น · สถานะแสดงด้วยรูปทรง + สี + ชื่อเสมอ (▲ ◆ ● ▼ ■ วงประ)
- ห้ามตัวเอียงในภาษาไทย · ทุกข้อความต้องมีทั้ง `th` และ `en`
- ไฟล์ `.tsx` export ได้เฉพาะ component — hook/context/helper อยู่ในไฟล์ `.ts`
- ห้ามใช้ `href="#id"` ในหน้า (HashRouter) — ใช้ `scrollToId()`
- ห้าม commit ข้อมูลจริง — repo มีแต่ `fixtures/` ที่สังเคราะห์ขึ้น
````

```markdown
- **DON'T** ใช้ Tailwind class แบบ dynamic (`bg-[${color}]`) — ใช้ inline style `var(--…)` หรือ class ที่เขียนไว้ใน CSS ครบทุกค่า (`chip-${status}`)
- **DON'T** ใช้ `href="#id"` ในหน้า (HashRouter จะเปลี่ยน route) — ใช้ `scrollToId()`
- **DON'T** โหลดอะไรจากเซิร์ฟเวอร์ภายนอกตอน runtime (ฟอนต์, CDN, analytics) และไม่เรียก API/LLM จากเว็บ
- **DON'T** commit ข้อมูลจริง — repo มีแต่ `web/fixtures/` (สังเคราะห์); ข้อมูลจริงวางด้วย `atlas publish --site` ตอน deploy
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `scripts/import_atlas.sh`** — replace the whole file:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# import_atlas.sh — build web v2 and put the latest validated release on it (data/ + latest.json + offline HTML)
#
# การใช้ (จาก root ของ repo):
#   bash scripts/import_atlas.sh [pipeline/config.yaml]
#
# ทำอะไรบ้าง:
#   1. npm ci (ถ้ายังไม่มี node_modules) + npm run build  → web/dist/ และ web/dist/offline-template.html
#   2. uv run atlas publish --site web/dist                → web/dist/data/<release>/, data/latest.json, atlas-offline.html
#      (publish ตรวจ release ก่อน — ถ้ามี error จะไม่วางอะไรเลย)
#   3. รายงานผล — จากนั้นอัปโหลด web/dist/ ขึ้น static host (รอบถัดไปคัดลอกแค่ data/ ก็พอ)
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
CONFIG="${1:-$ROOT/pipeline/config.yaml}"

cd "$ROOT/web"
[[ -d node_modules ]] || npm ci
npm run build

cd "$ROOT/pipeline"
uv run atlas publish -c "$CONFIG" --site "$ROOT/web/dist"

echo
echo "[import] เสร็จสิ้น ✓"
echo "  - ดูผล local:  cd web && node scripts/serve.mjs --site dist   แล้วเปิด http://127.0.0.1:4173/"
echo "  - ไฟล์ออฟไลน์: web/dist/atlas-offline.html (เปิดได้โดยไม่ต่อเน็ต)"
echo "  - deploy:       อัปโหลด web/dist/ ขึ้น static host"
```

```yaml
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v7
```

### Task 14: GitHub Pages deploy workflow with a labelled synthetic demo release

The owner pre-authorised GitHub Pages and public releases. This task adds the workflow only; **the controller runs the deployment** (enable "Settings → Pages → Source: GitHub Actions" on `santipongth/tci-research-atlas`, push, run the workflow). Until a real release exists the public site shows the synthetic example release — refused by the staging script unless its manifest marks it as sample data (`embedding_provider: fixture | lite`), so every page carries the "ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง — … ห้ามนำตัวเลขไปอ้างอิง" banner and the brief carries the same notice. The same staging also writes `atlas-offline.html` with the demo data. A real release later replaces `data/` with `uv run atlas publish --site` (or a pipeline-side deploy step); the web build does not change.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/scripts/stage-demo-data.mjs`, `.github/workflows/pages.yml`
- Modify: `web/package.json` (script `stage:demo`), `web/README.md` (one paragraph)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `npm run build` output (`dist/`, `dist/offline-template.html`), `fixtures/example/data`.
- Produces: `npm run stage:demo -- [site]` → `<site>/data/{latest.json, 2026Q3/, 2026Q4/}` + `<site>/atlas-offline.html`; workflow `Pages` (`workflow_dispatch` + pushes to `main` touching `web/**`) with jobs `build` → `deploy` (environment `github-pages`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the staging script** — create `web/scripts/stage-demo-data.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Put the SYNTHETIC example release into a built site for the public demo (GitHub Pages):
//   npm run build && npm run stage:demo -- dist
// → <site>/data/{latest.json, 2026Q3/, 2026Q4/} and <site>/atlas-offline.html (the offline edition with the same data).
// Refuses a release that is not labelled as sample data (manifest.embedding_provider fixture | lite), so the
// "ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง / Sample data" banner always shows on the demo. Real releases go through `uv run atlas publish --site`.
import { cpSync, existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { dirname, join, relative, resolve, sep } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

const web = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..')
const site = resolve(process.argv[2] ?? join(web, 'dist'))
const src = join(web, 'fixtures', 'example', 'data')
const fail = (msg) => {
  console.error(`[demo] ${msg}`)
  process.exit(1)
}
if (!existsSync(join(site, 'index.html'))) fail(`${site} is not a built site — run npm run build first`)
const latest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(src, 'latest.json'), 'utf8'))
const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(src, latest.path), 'utf8'))
if (!['fixture', 'lite'].includes(manifest.embedding_provider)) fail('the demo release must be sample data (embedding_provider fixture | lite)')

cpSync(src, join(site, 'data'), { recursive: true })

const walk = (dir) => readdirSync(dir).flatMap((n) => (statSync(join(dir, n)).isDirectory() ? walk(join(dir, n)) : [join(dir, n)]))
const root = join(src, latest.release)
const files = Object.fromEntries(walk(root).map((p) => [relative(root, p).split(sep).join('/'), JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, 'utf8'))]))
const template = join(site, 'offline-template.html')
if (existsSync(template)) {
  const slot = '<script type="application/json" id="atlas-data">null</script>'
  const html = readFileSync(template, 'utf8')
  if (html.split(slot).length !== 2) fail('offline-template.html must contain exactly one empty atlas-data slot')
  const payload = JSON.stringify({ latest, files }).replaceAll('</', '<\\/')
  writeFileSync(join(site, 'atlas-offline.html'), html.replace(slot, () => `<script type="application/json" id="atlas-data">${payload}</script>`))
}
console.log(`[demo] sample release ${latest.release} → ${join(site, 'data')}${existsSync(template) ? ' + atlas-offline.html' : ''}`)
```

Add to `web/package.json` `"scripts"`:

```json
    "stage:demo": "node scripts/stage-demo-data.mjs",
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Try it locally**

Run: `npm run build && npm run stage:demo -- dist && node scripts/serve.mjs --site dist`
Expected: `[demo] sample release 2026Q4 → …\dist\data + atlas-offline.html`; `http://127.0.0.1:4173/#/overview` shows the example release with the yellow "ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง" tag on every page, `#/brief` shows the sample notice, `dist/atlas-offline.html` opens from `file://`. Then `git status` shows no change (dist is ignored).

- [ ] **Step 3: The workflow** — create `.github/workflows/pages.yml`:

```yaml
name: Pages

# Public demo of web v2 on GitHub Pages with the SYNTHETIC example release (every page shows a "Sample data" banner).
# Deployment is run by the maintainer (owner pre-authorised Pages + public releases). A real release replaces
# web/dist/data with `uv run atlas publish --site web/dist` instead of `npm run stage:demo`.
on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - "web/**"
      - ".github/workflows/pages.yml"

permissions:
  contents: read
  pages: write
  id-token: write

concurrency:
  group: pages
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: web
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with:
          node-version: "24"
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: web/package-lock.json
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm test
      - name: Build (Pages site + offline template)
        run: npm run build
      - name: Demo data — synthetic sample release, labelled on every page
        run: npm run stage:demo -- dist
      - uses: actions/configure-pages@v6
      - uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v5
        with:
          path: web/dist

  deploy:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment.outputs.page_url }}
    steps:
      - id: deployment
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v5
```

